** touch (=do_touch=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: cmds3.c:3694
:MIN_POS: RES
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: touch <object | character>
*** Parameters
- =target= :: =only_argument=; =generic_find= over inventory, room objects, equipment and characters in the room (=FIND_OBJ_INV | FIND_OBJ_ROOM | FIND_OBJ_EQUIP | FIND_CHAR_ROOM=). Characters take precedence when both match. Empty argument prints a usage joke.
*** What it does
Social/trigger command. Touching another character emits act messages to you, the victim and the room; touching yourself is refused humorously. Touching an object is blocked while =BERZERK= is active; otherwise it plays the object's =msg_to_room= / =msg_to_char= (or default "touches the $o" texts) and, if the object has a valid =touch_to_room=, teleports the toucher there (=char_from_room=/=char_to_room=) and announces arrival with =msg_to_dest= or "arrives in a flash of light." This is the mechanism for touch-activated portals.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: object =msg_to_room=, =msg_to_char=, =msg_to_dest=, =touch_to_room=; =affected_by_spell(ch,BERZERK)=
- Writes :: =ch->in_room= and room people lists when a =touch_to_room= teleport fires
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: none. Room change is in memory; picked up by the next routine player save.
- No log lines.

** apexlist (=do_list_apex_levels=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: cmds3.c:3848
:MIN_POS: DEA
:MIN_LEVEL: I10 (2010)
:END:
*** Syntax
: apexlist
*** Parameters
- none (function takes only =ch=; shows the caller's own distribution).
*** What it does
Prints the caller's APEX point distribution: for each attribute (hitpoints, mana, power, stopping-all, resist-all, the seven stats, hitroll, damroll, life steal, melee haste, armour penetration, spell power) the points spent vs the =APEX_*_LIMIT= and the resulting bonus (points x =APEX_*_RATIO=). Note the Dexterity line multiplies =apex_points.hitpoints= instead of =apex_points.dexterity= (same copy/paste slip exists in the unused =append_apex_point_bonuses=).
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->apex_points.*=, =APEX_*_LIMIT/RATIO= constants
- Writes :: none
*** Persistence
- Read-only; no state change.
- No log lines.

** Helpers in cmds3.c
- =compare_name= / =compare_host= / =compare_level= / =compare_fd= (167-197) :: =qsort= comparators over =connection_data**= used by =do_users= sort options.
- =describe_conn= (205) :: name for a connection: character name when playing, else account name, else "Unknown".
- =reverse_string= / =format_number= / =get_exp_formated= (393-436) :: build a comma-separated decimal string (static buffer) for experience values in =do_levels=.
- =format_commas_number= (452) :: thread-safe comma formatting of a =long long= into a caller buffer; used by score/gold/who.
- =num_punct= (469) :: older comma formatter into a static buffer; used by =wizard.c= and =spec_rooms.c=, not by the commands above.
- =compare_strings= (729) :: =str_cmp= comparator for sorting command-name arrays.
- =select_flags= (927) :: builds the " (away,invis N,noshout,notell,silenced,writing)" suffix for who lines; immortal-only flags shown only to immortals.
- =race_header_types[]= / =guild_header_types[]= / =home_header_types[]= (963-1029) :: two-letter race tags and unused tag tables for who output.
- =pretty_time= (1031) :: =cftime= wrapper formatting a =time_t= for =do_info=.
- =is_in_class_set= (1117) :: true if the character's class abbreviation appears in the given string and the character is not anonymous (or anonymity is overridden).
- =imort_names[]= / =imm_rank= (1128-1142) :: immortal rank titles indexed by (level-I1)*3+sex.
- =format_clan_string= (1152) :: who line in clan format (clan name or "-> Anonymous <-" for stealth clans/anon players, rank, name, title, flags).
- =is_new_player= (1171) :: viewer is a "new player" (level <= 20, no remorts, non-PK clan) and the target's invis level <= 2000; lets newbies see mortal-invisible players in who.
- =format_who_string= (1189) :: standard who line: "[ Anonymous ]", "[ Ra-Lvl Cl ]", "[ AVATAR ]", "[P*O*L*I*C*E]" or immortal rank, then name, title and =select_flags=.
- =arena_who_string= (1217) :: compact contestant line for =who arena=.
- =describe_obj_location= (1560) :: locates an object: room vnum, carrier/equipper (including containers), warehouse or account warehouse.
- =describe_object= (1597) :: appends an =owhere= line (count>0) or an rstat-style line with phaseout timer (count==0) to a string block.
- =describe_socketed_object= (1608) :: appends a line for a gem found in an object's socket, with the host object's location.
- =describe_char= (1617) :: appends a where-style line (mob: name, vnum, room, zone; player: name, room, zone, host or <link-dead>) or an rstat-style line.
- =set_mock= (1842) :: fills a =mock_char_data= (thac0, body AC, hp, average damage) for =do_consider=.
- =value_stat= (2046) :: words for a 3..18 stat value ("very low" .. "extremely high"); no callers anywhere in the tree (dead code).
- =get_skill_affects= (2077) :: prints skill-derived =affected_by= bits (sneak, hide, druid forms) for self or, for immortals, a named target.
- =has_affecting_spells= (2170) :: true if any =affected_by= bit or any =affected= entry exists.
- =get_remort_affects= (2185) :: prints remort bonus table (name, rank/max, modifier, bonus) for self or an immortal-named target.
- =gain_apex_points= (2239) :: APEX points awarded for killing a mob, bucketed by =get_mob_rank= (0 up to 50), multiplied by the Blessing of Knowledge pulse affect. Called from =fight.c=/=offensive.c=, not from a command here.
- =get_ability_cooldowns= (2300) :: prints remaining time on =affected_pulse_cooldown= entries, item-set ability cooldowns and equipment use cooldowns.
- =get_spell_or_skill_name= (2449) :: maps a spell or skill number to its display name (strips a leading '*').
- =get_highest_setbonus_for_set= / =get_highest_active_setbonus= (2466/2518) :: highest defined bonus tier of an item set, and highest tier the character currently has active.
- =get_active_setbonuses= (2572) :: lists item sets worn with the active tier (green when maxed).
- =get_spell_affects= (2630) :: the big affect printer: =affected_by= bits, =affected= spell list, =affected_pulse= abilities (blessings, soul link, murderous/perilous stacks, item buffs, item-set abilities), berserk, silenced/confused markers.
- =update_kills_table= (3753) :: inserts/updates "<name> (<clan>)" with a rating in the in-memory top-10 =kill_table= and bubble-sorts it descending; clan shown as "(None)" for clan index < 4 or private/stealth clans. Never written to disk.
- =list_kills_table= (3824) :: prints the top-10 =kill_table= leaderboard.
- =add_apex_point= (3876) :: empty stub; does nothing.
- =append_apex_point_bonuses= (3882) :: applies APEX point bonuses (hp, mana, power, stopping, resistances, stats, hit/dam, life steal, haste, penetration, spell power) to a character's live totals. No callers anywhere in the tree (dead code); APEX bonuses are evidently applied elsewhere.

* wizard.c — immortal administration (71 commands)

** norent (=do_norent=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:53
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I9 (2009)
:END:
*** Syntax
: norent
: norent <obj vnum>
*** Parameters
- =vnum= :: =one_argument=, =atoi=. Omitted → list mode. Present → toggle that vnum in the no-rent list.
*** What it does
With no argument prints the current list of object vnums explicitly marked no-rent (ten per line) and the count. With a vnum, if it is already in the list (=is_no_rent=) it is removed, otherwise appended; the whole list is then rewritten to disk. The list is loaded at boot by =boot_no_rent= and consulted by =is_no_rent= (rent/save code) to refuse renting those objects.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: globals =no_rent[]=, =num_no_rent=
- Writes :: =no_rent[]= (REALLOC on add, shift-down on remove), =num_no_rent=
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: =fopen("norent","w+")= → lib file =norent= (count line, then one vnum per line). Report "Couldn't write to norent." on failure.
- Note: the removal loop runs =for (i=j; i<num_no_rent-1; i++)= after =num_no_rent= was already decremented, so the last element is never shifted down: removing a non-final entry drops the *last* vnum and duplicates the second-to-last instead (off-by-one bug).

Current/max pairs are shown for hp, mana, moves and power; =max_hp= uses =hit_limit=; =speed= uses =calc_speed(v,0)=; =obj_count= uses =obj_owned_count=; =race= prints =race_list[GET_RACE(v)]=.

** fine (=do_fine=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:202
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I8 (2008)
:END:
*** Syntax
: fine <name> <amount>
*** Parameters
- =name=   :: first =one_argument=; resolved with =get_char_vis= (room, then world).
- =amount= :: second =one_argument=, =atoi=; must be > 0 or nothing happens (silently).
*** What it does
Tells the victim "You have been fined N coins." then calls =fine(tch,amount,ch,1)= (reception.c:210): gold is taken from the victim's pocket, then bank, then (because =take_equip=1) inventory and worn equipment are stripped item by item until the amount is covered; the gold taken is credited to the immortal =ch=. The =else= branch for a non-immortal caller (=fine(tch,amount,0,0)= plus a LOG_POLICE line) is unreachable in practice: the command needs I8, and =fine()= returns 0 immediately when =to_whom= is NULL anyway.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =GET_GOLD(tch)=, =GET_BANK(tch)=, =tch->carrying=, =tch->equipment[]=
- Writes :: =tch->gold=, =tch->bankgold=, =ch->gold= (+= taken), victim inventory/equipment (=unequip_char=, =take_obj_list=)
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_POLICE,"FINE: [...]")= only on the unreachable mortal branch.

** chaos (=do_chaos_open=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:350
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I8 (2008)
:END:
*** Syntax
: chaos <lo> <hi> <start_delay> <cost/level> <length> [pot]
*** Parameters
- =lo=, =hi=          :: level range allowed to join; stored in globals =chaos_lo=, =chaos_hi=.
- =start_delay=       :: game hours (ticks) of announcements before the arena closes to entry; =arena_ticks_till_start = value+1=.
- =cost/level=        :: entry cost per player level (=arena_cost_per_level=).
- =length=            :: game hours the fight runs after closing (=arena_ticks_till_close=).
- =pot=               :: optional starting prize pot; if ≤0 defaults to =chaos_hi*arena_cost_per_level=.
- All parsed with successive =one_argument= + =atoi=; the first five are mandatory (usage printed if any missing).
*** What it does
Opens a "killing fields" arena event. Purges rooms 900..972 (=ARENA_START= .. =ARENA_START+ARENA_TOT-1=; occupants dumped into room 0/the Void, objects too) via =hash_iterate_range(...purge_one_room)=, then recreates them with =do_form(ch," 900 972",-1)= (names "The killing fields."). Every arena room gets =ARENA|PEACEFUL|RELOC_DISTORT= flags, =tele_time=0=, =tele_look=1=, and a random =tele_targ= inside the 8x8 main grid (rooms 900..963) or inside the 3x3 endgame grid (964..972). Finally =arena_monitor()= is called, which reschedules itself each tick: announces the event and cost while =arena_ticks_till_start>1=; at 1 builds mazes with =create_maze=, clears PEACEFUL and closes entry; thereafter counts non-immortal players in the arena, moves survivors to the endgame grid with 5 hours left, declares a lone survivor winner (full =do_restore=, affects stripped, sent home, pot awarded) or splits half the pot among survivors when time expires (mobs left inside are extracted).
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =room_db= range 900..972, =chaos_lo/hi=
- Writes :: globals =chaos_lo=, =chaos_hi=, =arena_ticks_till_start=, =arena_cost_per_level=, =arena_ticks_till_close=, =arena_pot=; arena =room_data= (=room_flags=, =tele_*=); via =arena_monitor=: players' =gold=, room, affects, events.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; arena rooms exist only in memory (not written by this command). Player gold changes persist on the next routine save.
- Log lines: none directly (=send_to_all= / =sports_cast= broadcasts only).

** resetcds (=do_resetcds=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:643

:END:
*** Syntax
: resetcds <name>
*** Parameters

Clears every cooldown on the target: removes all =affected_pulse_cooldown= entries (=pulse_affect_cooldown_remove=), removes all active =item_set_ability= entries (=item_set_ability_remove=), and zeroes =last_use_time= and =last_ws_time= on every worn item. Works on players and mobs. Reports to both parties.

- Writes :: those lists (emptied), =equipment[i]->last_use_time=, =->last_ws_time=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

*** Syntax
: set

: set <name> skill <skill#> <learned>
: set <mob> hate|fear <minutes> <person>

*** Parameters
- =name=  :: =one_argument=, =get_char_vis= (room then world). No name → list of fields the caller's level may set.
- =field= :: =one_argument=, prefix match (=is_abbrev=) against =set_field[]=, restricted to entries whose =min_level= ≤ caller level.
- =value= :: =one_argument= + =atol= for numeric/flag fields; rest of line for string fields. NPC callers are ignored.
- Field table (type: 0=integer in [min,max]; 1=flag toggle - value 0/1 or omitted to toggle; 2=class name; 3=string; 4=banish; 5=disabled; 6=race):
  | field        | type | range / flag        | min lvl | effect                                                                  |
  |--------------+------+---------------------+---------+-------------------------------------------------------------------------|
  | strength     | 0    | 0..99               | I8      | =ADD_STR= by diff, =base_stats.strength= += diff                        |
  | intelligence | 0    | 0..90               | I8      | as above for INT                                                        |
  | wisdom       | 0    | 0..90               | I8      | as above for WIS                                                        |
  | dexterity    | 0    | 0..90               | I8      | as above for DEX                                                        |
  | constitution | 0    | 0..90               | I8      | as above for CON                                                        |
  | addstrength  | 0    | 0..100              | I9      | NO-OP (case 5 is empty) - prints "Ok."                                  |
  | gold         | 0    | 0..MAX_SLONG        | I9      | =v->gold=                                                               |
  | experience   | 0    | 0..MAX_SLONG        | I9      | =v->exp=                                                                |
  | mana         | 0    | 0..32000            | I9      | =max_mana= += diff; =mana= = max                                        |
  | hp           | 0    | 0..10000000         | I9      | =max_hit= += diff; =hit= = max                                          |
  | move         | 0    | -32000..32000       | I9      | =max_move= += diff; =move= = max                                        |
  | align        | 0    | -1000..1000         | I7      | =v->alignment=                                                          |
  | sex          | 0    | -3..3               | I9      | =v->sex=                                                                |
  | race         | 6    | 0..255              | I9      | digit → =v->race=val=; else =which_race= name lookup                    |
  | hit_bonus    | 0    | -100..100           | I9      | =v->hit_bonus=                                                          |
  | bank         | 0    | 0..MAX_SLONG        | I9      | =v->bankgold=                                                           |

  | melee_haste  | 0    | 0..1000             | I9      | =v->melee_haste=                                                        |
  | life_steal   | 0    | 0..1000             | I9      | =v->life_steal=                                                         |
  | height       | 0    | 1..350              | I9      | =v->height=                                                             |
  | spell_haste  | 0    | 0..1000             | I9      | =v->spell_casting_haste=                                                |
  | spell_vamp   | 0    | 0..1000             | I9      | =v->spell_casting_vamp=                                                 |
  | base_alignment | 0  | 0..2                | I9      | 0→=base_alignment=0=, 1→1000, 2→-1000                                   |

  | shield       | 0    | 0..10000000         | I9      | =v->hit_shield = MAX(1,val)=, =update_pos=, victim told of barrier      |
  | assassinate  | 0    | 0..1000             | I11     | =v->assassinate=                                                        |
  | no_wear      | 1    | ACT_NO_WEAR         | I9      | mob-only flag                                                           |
*** What it does
General-purpose immortal field editor. Numeric fields are range-checked against the table; flag fields toggle when no value is given, or take 0/1. Flag fields whose table =min= is 1 are mob-only (=ACT_*= in =v->act=), =min= 0 are player-only (=PLR_*=), enforced with "Can only set that flag for mobiles/players". String fields with a value set it immediately (mob strings are detached from the prototype first so the shared string is not freed); with no value the caller is dropped into the line editor (=ch->desc->str=, =max_str= from the table, terminate with =@=). =levels=: setting I10/I11 is allowed only for callers named Countess, Alcanzar or Nitemare - anyone else is logged at LOG_URGENT, their host is wizlocked (=do_wizlock(ch,ch->desc->host,-1)=) and their socket closed; otherwise the new level must be ≤ the caller's level and the victim must currently be below the caller (or caller ≥ I10, or caller is Nitemare); on success =v->level= is set and =reset_affects(v)= rebuilds affects. =banished=: a mob is simply moved to room 6999 (=LOAD_ROOM_BANISHED=); a player has =PLR_BANISHED= toggled - set → moved to 6999 with the hell messages, cleared → pardoned and moved to =L_ROOM_MORT= (bindpoint) or =LOAD_ROOM_CRIMINAL= (64490) if =rent_disable= is still active. Account-banished players (=A_BANISHED=) cannot be banished here.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =set_field[]=, =GET_LEVEL(ch)=, victim fields listed above, =ch->desc->host=, class/skill tables (=get_class=, =cl->skills/spells=), =get_clan_with_index=
- Writes :: the victim field named in the table; =v->act= bits; =v->skills[]/v->spells[]= (=find_or_add_skill_data=, =find_or_add_spell_data=, =set_skill=); mob hate/fear lists; =ch->desc->str/max_str= (editor); room membership for banish; =wizlocked= list on the level-abuse path
*** Persistence
- No direct save for almost everything; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save. Mob changes apply to the instance only (not the prototype/zone file).
- Exceptions: =rbonus add= etc. call =do_save(victim,"",-1)= → =save_char= → =stash/<c>/<name>=; the =levels= abuse path calls =do_wizlock= → lib file =wizlocked=.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_URGENT,"%s tried to make %s implementor.")= on the levels abuse path.

** runninglog (=do_running_log=)

*** Syntax

*** What it does
Toggles =PLR_NOVIEWLOG= on the caller via =do_toggle=: setting it prints "You will no longer see the running log", clearing it prints "You will now see the running log". The flag suppresses the live log feed that immortals otherwise receive. NPCs ignored.

*** Syntax
: spy <player>
*** Parameters

Toggles per-character command logging (=PLR_LOG=) on a player. Refused for NPCs, for targets of level ≥ the caller ("$E might object"), and for characters whose *account* is already logged (=A_LOG=). When set, every command the player types is written by =log_action= (nlog.c:215) as LOG_MORTSPY (account, room, name, command).

- Writes :: =vict->act= bit =PLR_LOG=
*** Persistence

Flips the global =log_all= flag. While on, =command_interpreter= sends every mortal's command line to =log_action=, which writes "[account]{room}name:command" via =slog= for players that are not otherwise logged (immortals are always logged as LOG_IMMSPY regardless; level ≥ I10 never logged).

** wizlock (=do_wizlock=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:1474
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I7 (2007)
:END:
*** Syntax
: wizlock
: wizlock <host or address pattern>
*** Parameters
- =host= :: rest of line (=only_argument=). Empty → list. Note the empty test is on the raw =argument=, so an all-blank argument adds an empty string.
*** What it does
Maintains the site lock list =wizlocked=: with no argument lists the entries; otherwise removes the entry if present (=remove_string=) or adds it (=add_string=), then rewrites the file. Connections from a matching host are refused in =comm.c:592= (=address_in_list=). Also invoked internally with =cmd=-1= by =do_set levels= to lock out an abuser's host; =nanny.c= adds hosts to the same list on repeated bad logins.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: global =string_list wizlocked=
- Writes :: =wizlocked= list
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: =write_lock_list("wizlocked",&wizlocked,ch)= → lib file =wizlocked= (one host per line); re-read at boot by =boot_lock_list=.

** echo (=do_echo=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:1514
:MIN_POS: SLE (sleeping)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: echo
: echo <text>                 (immortals)
: echo -d <descriptor#> <text> (I9+)
*** Parameters
- no argument :: toggles the caller's =PLR_ECHO= flag (command echo).
- =text=      :: rest of line; only acted on if =IS_IMMORTAL(ch)=; mortals giving text get no response at all.
- =-d <fd>=   :: I9+; =fd= matched against =connection_data.identifier=; text is =dsendf='d to that connection only.
*** What it does
Mortal use: toggle echo on/off. Immortal use: sends the text verbatim to everyone else in the room (=send_to_room_except=) and replies "Ok."; with =-d= sends privately to one descriptor (any connection state). NPCs ignored. Bug: the "Descriptor %d not found" message passes =buf= (a string) for =%d=.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->act=, =connection_list=
- Writes :: =ch->act= bit =PLR_ECHO= (toggle path only)
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** reboot (=do_reboot=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:1558
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I9 (2009)
:END:
*** Syntax
: reboot
: reboot <minutes>          (float allowed; 0 cancels)
: reboot clean
*** Parameters
- none        :: report time remaining on the scheduled reboot, or "No reboot currently scheduled."
- =minutes=   :: =atof=; must start with a digit or '.'; negative rejected; 0 clears =reboot_time=.
- =clean=     :: creates the file-flag =BOOT_CLEAN= (does not schedule anything).
*** What it does
Schedules an automatic reboot: sets the global =reboot_time= (=now + delay=) and broadcasts "AUTO REBOOT SET FOR N MINUTE(S) [N SECOND(S)]" with a bell to every connection. =handle_reboot= (wizard.c:1629, called from the main loop) then sends countdown warnings at intervals and, when the time arrives, broadcasts "AUTO REBOOT! Autosaved.", logs it, and sets =shutdwn=1; rboot=1= so the game loop exits with status 1 for the wrapper script to restart. =clean= only writes the =BOOT_CLEAN= marker consumed by the external restart script for a clean boot. Bug: the status line passes a =long= to =%.0f=.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =reboot_time=
- Writes :: global =reboot_time=; (=handle_reboot=: =shutdwn=, =rboot=)
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: =reboot clean= → =fopen("BOOT_CLEAN","w+")= in the lib dir (contents "."). Otherwise no save.
- Log lines: =handle_reboot= → =vlog(LOG_DEBUG,"AUTO REBOOT! Autosaved.")= when the reboot fires.

** transfer (=do_trans=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:1769
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I4 (2004)
:END:
*** Syntax
: transfer <name>
: transfer all          (I9+)
*** Parameters
- =name= :: rest of line (=only_argument=); resolved with =get_char_vis_world=.
- =all=  :: every connected, playing character (=IS_CONN=); requires level ≥ I9.
*** What it does
Pulls the victim into the immortal's room: "disappears in a mushroom cloud" / "arrives from a puff of smoke", stops their fight, =char_from_room/char_to_room=, sets =victim->was_in_room= to the new room (so a link-dead character is not bounced back by =command_interpreter=), and forces a =do_look=. Refused if the victim outranks the caller; if the victim is in a different worldzone and caller < I9 (=in_wrong_world=); and for callers ≤ I4 unless the victim has =consent <caller>= set (=HAS_CONSENT=), in which case the victim is told about the attempt.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =connection_list=, =GET_LEVEL=, =victim->consent=, =GET_WORLDZONE=
- Writes :: =victim->in_room= (room lists), =victim->was_in_room=, =victim->fighting=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_IMMSPY, ...)= on the worldzone refusal and the consent refusal.

** shutdown (=do_shutdown=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:1882
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I9 (2009)
:END:
*** Syntax
: shutdown
: shutdown kill
*** Parameters
- none   :: reboot: broadcasts "Reboot by <name>. Autosaved.", sets =shutdwn=1=. The game loop exits with status 0 and the =PR_SERVER_SCRIPT= wrapper restarts it.
- =kill= :: broadcasts "Shutdown by <name>.", sends SIGKILL to the parent process (=kill(getppid(),9)=, i.e. the wrapper loop) so no restart happens, then sets =shutdwn=1=.
- anything else :: "Go shut down someone your own size."
*** What it does
Stops the game loop (=while (!shutdwn)= in comm.c:130); on exit =main.c= frees world data and exits. Player saving is done by the normal shutdown path, not here. NPCs ignored.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: none
- Writes :: global =shutdwn=; parent process killed on =kill=
*** Persistence
- No direct save here (the shutdown sequence saves players elsewhere).
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_DEBUG,"Reboot by X.")= / ="Shutdown by X."=.

** snoop (=do_snoop=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:1907
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I6 (2006)
:END:
*** Syntax
: snoop <name>
: snoop <descriptor#>    (I10+)
: snoop                  (stop snooping)
*** Parameters
- =name=  :: rest of line, =get_char_vis=; empty while snooping → treated as own name (stops).
- =descriptor#= :: positive number, matched to =connection_data.identifier= (I10+ only; also lets you snoop connections without a character).
- =cmd=   :: =-1= internal call: returns immediately unless currently snooping.
*** What it does
Attaches the caller's descriptor to the target descriptor's =snooped_by[]= slot (up to =MAX_SNOOP= = 5 snoopers per target; "Busy already." when full), replacing any previous snoop target, so the target's I/O is mirrored to the caller. Snooping yourself (or a target that resolves to your own descriptor) detaches you. Refused, with the target warned in bold red, if the target character's level ≥ the caller's - unless the caller is named "Bunta" (hard-coded bypass). Targets with no link cannot be snooped.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =connection_list=, =victim->desc=, =d->snooped_by[]=, =ch->desc->snooping=
- Writes :: =ch->desc->snooping=, =d->snooped_by[i]=, previous target's =snooped_by[]=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only (descriptor state).

:MIN_LEVEL: I6 (2006)
:END:
*** Syntax
: mutateinfo
*** Parameters
- none (declared as =(ch, arg)=)
*** What it does
Pages a table of every =apply_fields[]= entry that has mutation naming data: apply type name, offset, field name, and the positive/negative adjective and "of ..." suffix used when items are mutated.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =apply_fields[]=, =apply_types[]=
- Writes :: none
*** Persistence
- Read-only; no state change.

** switch (=do_switch=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:2003
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I5 (2005)
:END:
*** Syntax
: switch <mob>
*** Parameters
- =mob= :: rest of line, =get_char= (anywhere in the world; visibility not checked).
*** What it does
Moves the caller's connection into a mob's body: =ch->desc->character = victim=, =desc->original = ch=, =victim->desc = ch->desc=, =ch->desc = 0=. The immortal's own body is left descriptor-less until =return=. Refused: switching into yourself; while snooping; into a body that is a player or already has a descriptor ("already in use"); into a mob standing in a =NO_GOTO= or =LORD_ONLY= room unless the caller's level ≥ 2009.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =victim->desc=, =ROOM_FLAG(victim->in_room,...)=, =ch->desc->snooping=
- Writes :: =ch->desc->character=, =ch->desc->original=, =victim->desc=, =ch->desc=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only.

** return (=do_return=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:2052
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: return
*** Parameters
- none
*** What it does
Undoes =switch= (or any other =desc->original= possession): the descriptor's character is restored to the original body, the original body's =desc= re-linked, and the possessed body's =desc= cleared. "Huh? Talk sense..." if not currently switched. Mortal-accessible because it acts on whoever is possessing.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->desc->original=
- Writes :: =ch->desc->character=, =ch->desc->original=, original body's =desc=, =ch->desc=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only.

** force (=do_force=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:2072
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I7 (2007)
:END:
*** Syntax
: force <name> <command...>
: force all <command...>
*** Parameters
- =name=, =command= :: =half_chop=: first word is the target, remainder is the command line. Target via =get_char_vis=.
- =all= :: every connection that is playing (=!i->connected=), is not the caller, and whose character has no =funct= (special proc) pending.
- =cmd= :: NPC callers are only allowed when =cmd == 0=.
*** What it does
Makes the victim execute the command line as if typed: "$n has forced you to 'cmd'." then =command_interpreter= + =command_process= on the victim. Players of level ≥ the caller cannot be forced ("Oh no you don't!!"); NPCs of any level can.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =connection_list=, =GET_LEVEL=
- Writes :: whatever the forced command writes
*** Persistence
- No direct save by itself (the forced command may save).

** as (=do_as=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:2123
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I9 (2009)
:END:
*** Syntax
: as <name> <command...>
*** Parameters
- =name=, =command= :: =half_chop=; target via =get_char_vis=. NPC callers only when =cmd == 0=.
*** What it does
Runs one command *as* the victim but with the output routed to the caller: the caller's descriptor is temporarily attached to the victim (=my_desc->character = vict; vict->desc = my_desc=), the command is interpreted and processed, then the descriptors are restored. Unlike =force=, the victim is not told. Refused when the victim is a player of level ≥ the caller. Requires the caller to have a descriptor (otherwise logged as LOG_URGENT).
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =GET_LEVEL=
- Writes :: =ch->desc->character= and =vict->desc= (temporarily); whatever the command writes
*** Persistence
- No direct save by itself.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_MISC,"%s did '%s' as %s")= unless caller ≥ I11; =vlog(LOG_URGENT,...)= if caller has no connection.

** asimm (=do_asimm=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:2170
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I10 (2010)
:END:
*** Syntax
: asimm <name> <command...>
*** Parameters
- as =as=.
*** What it does
Identical to =as= except that for the duration of the command the victim's =level= is set to the caller's level (restored afterwards), so a mortal or mob can be made to run immortal-only commands.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =GET_LEVEL=
- Writes :: =vict->level= (temporarily), =ch->desc->character=, =vict->desc= (temporarily); whatever the command writes
*** Persistence
- No direct save by itself.
- Log lines: same as =as=.

** load (=do_load=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:2279
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I6 (2006)
:END:
*** Syntax
: load mobile <vnum | name>
: load object <vnum | name>
*** Parameters
- =mobile|object= :: =one_argument=, =is_abbrev= ("m", "o" suffice).
- =vnum|name=     :: rest of line; digits → vnum; otherwise the first prototype whose namelist matches is found by =hash_iterate(&mob_db|&obj_db, find_number, name)=.
*** What it does
Mobile: =get_mob(vnum,0)= creates an instance and places it in the caller's room with "summoned $N from the ether" messages. Object: =do_load_object= (wizard.c:2232) creates the object with =get_obj=, sets up its events, stamps =obj->loaded_by_imm= with the caller's name, and puts it in the caller's inventory ("$n has created $p!"). Callers below I9 may not load vnum 3097 (the imp board; attempt logged LOG_URGENT) and may not exceed a limited item's =max_exist= ("Unable to load. There are N in the world..."); their loads are logged. Anyone loading a limited item (=max_exist<32000=) sees the new world total.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =mob_db=, =obj_db=, =index_mem.world_count/max_exist=
- Writes :: new =char_data= in room / new =obj_data= in inventory; =obj->loaded_by_imm=; prototype =world_count= (via =get_obj=)
*** Persistence
- No direct save; the object persists only when the immortal's player file is next saved (the mob is transient).
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_LOAD_OBJ,"<imm> loaded [limited] item '<name>' [total x of y].")= for callers < I9; =vlog(LOG_URGENT,"%s tried to load the imp board.")=.

** advance (=do_advance=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:2422
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I10 (2010)
:END:
*** Syntax
: advance <player> <level>
*** Parameters
- =player= :: =one_argument=, =get_char_room_vis= (must be in the same room); NPCs refused.
- =level=  :: =one_argument=, must begin with a digit; must be > the victim's current level, ≤ the caller's level and < I10.
*** What it does
Raises a player to the given level by granting experience: =gain_exp_regardless(victim, EXP_NEEDED(newlevel, class) - GET_EXP(victim))=, which loops =advance_level= once per level gained (hp/practice gains, "You raise a level"). Flavour text is sent to the victim. Lowering a level is refused ("Can't dimish a players status (yet)"). The final =else= ("IMPOSSIBLE! IDIOTIC!") is unreachable because a victim ≥ I10 already fails the =newlevel>=I10= check.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =GET_LEVEL(victim)=, =GET_EXP=, =GET_CLASS=, =classes[].multi=
- Writes :: =victim->exp=, and via =advance_level=: =level=, =max_hit=, =spells_to_learn=, etc.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

*** What it does
Load-testing aid: =n= times, loads mobs 1054, 1055 and 1056 into the caller's room and starts two fights (=hit(mob,mob2)=, =hit(mob3,mob)=), i.e. 3n mobs brawling. A large block of further mob pairs (1057..1096) is commented out.

- Writes :: new mob instances in =ch->in_room=; their =fighting= state

** iremort (=do_remort=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:2632
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I8 (2008)
:END:
*** Syntax
: iremort <player> [level]
*** Parameters
- =player= :: =one_argument=, =get_char_room_vis=; must be a PC, not immortal, exactly level 500; with ≥30 remorts refused unless caller ≥ 2010.
- =level=  :: optional; default 1. BUG: the check is inverted (=if (isdigit(*level))= → "second argument must be a positive integer"), so any numeric value is rejected and a non-numeric value yields =atoi=0=. The value is in any case never used after validation - the victim always restarts at level 0 then =advance_level=.
*** What it does
Immortal-driven remort of a level-500 player. Sends the "soul ripped from your body" text, strips all affects, calls =raw_kill_no_move(victim)= (death cry, dispel, cooldowns cleared, *a corpse is made from the victim's inventory/equipment*, kills/hates/fears deleted), then zeroes =max_level=, =level=, sets =exp=1=, =max_hit=10=, refills hp/power/move (+ a CON-based move bump), resets hunger/thirst/fullness to 24, =spells_to_learn=0=, sets every existing skill and spell's =learned= to 0, increments =remort_count=, runs =advance_level= (to level 1) and saves. Two apparent bugs: =DeleteHatreds(ch); DeleteFears(ch);= act on the *immortal*, and =ch->mana = GET_MAX_MANA(victim)= sets the immortal's mana instead of the victim's.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =GET_LEVEL(victim)=, =victim->remort_count=
- Writes :: =victim->affected=, =max_level=, =level=, =exp=, =max_hit=, =hit=, =power=, =max_move=, =move=, =conditions[]=, =fullness=, =spells_to_learn=, =skills[].learned=, =spells[].learned=, =remort_count=; corpse object in room; =ch->mana=; =ch= hate/fear lists
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: =do_save(victim,"",0)= → =save_char(victim, LOAD_ROOM_MORTAL)= → =stash/<c>/<name>= (victim sees "Saving <name>.").

** restore (=do_restore=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:2736
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I7 (2007)
:END:
*** Syntax
: restore <name>
: restore all
*** Parameters
- =name= :: rest of line (=only_argument=), =get_char= (anywhere, no visibility check; mobs allowed).
- =all=  :: every playing connection other than the caller whose character is not immortal and has no =funct=.
- =cmd=  :: =-1= internal call (arena winner/survivors): restores =ch= itself silently, no "healed by" message.
*** What it does
Single target: sets mana, power, hit and move to their maxima. For mobs that is all. For players: if the victim is mortal and the *caller's* =CONFIG_HUNGER= config bit is set, hunger/thirst/fullness go to 24, otherwise they are set to -1 (disabled) - this tests =ch->config_bits=, not the victim's, which looks unintended. Victims ≥ I5 get =job = CLASS_IMMORTAL=. Victims above =MAX_MORT= (2000) or of class BUILDER/CLASS_IMMORTAL have their skill and spell lists freed and rebuilt with every class spell/skill at 100 (=set_spell=/=set_skill=). Victims ≥ I7 get fixed base stats (INT/WIS/DEX/CON 25, STR 30) copied into =stats=. =update_pos= is applied and the victim is told who healed them. =all=: mortals only get hp/mana/power/move maxed and hunger/thirst/fullness 24.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =connection_list=, =ch->config_bits=, =GET_LEVEL(victim)=, =CLASS(victim).spells/skills=
- Writes :: =victim->mana/power/hit/move=, =conditions[HUNGER|THIRST]=, =fullness=, =job=, =skills[]/spells[]= (freed and rebuilt), =base_stats=, =stats=, position
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** Helpers in wizard.c (part A)
- =boot_no_rent= (30) :: loads lib file =norent= into =no_rent[]= at boot.
- =is_no_rent= (45) :: returns 1-based index if an object vnum is in the no-rent list, else 0.
- =prefix_search= (106) :: abbreviation lookup in a "\n"-terminated string array (used by =data=).
- =arena_monitor= (236) :: self-rescheduling tick handler for the chaos arena: announcements, closing, endgame relocation, winner/draw payout.
- =get_setinfo_bonuses= (405) :: prints one item-set bonus tier (applies, pulse abilities, cooldown timers) to =ch= or as speech.
- =print_set_items_in_set= (611) / =print_set_items_for_set= (632) :: list all object prototypes that belong to an item set, marking equipped ones.
- =do_polymorph= (798) :: empty stub, not registered.
- =do_toggle= (803) :: generic flip of a =PLR_*= bit in =ch->act= with on/off messages.
- =set_field[]= (829) :: the =set= command's field table (name, type, min, max/flag, min level).
- =write_lock_list_no_ch= (1444) / =write_lock_list= (1459) :: dump a =string_list= to a lib file (=wizlocked=, =no-new=).
- =handle_reboot= (1629) :: main-loop hook that issues countdown broadcasts and fires the scheduled reboot (=shutdwn=rboot=1=).
- =find_number= (2222) :: =hash_iterate= callback that finds the first mob/obj prototype matching a name (for =load=).
- =do_load_object= (2232) :: creates an object for an immortal with limit/imp-board checks and LOG_LOAD_OBJ logging.
- =do_start= (2333) :: new-character setup (starting gear, prompt, stats, =advance_level=, =do_save=); called from nanny, not a command.
- =fine= (reception.c:210) :: takes gold from pocket, bank, then inventory/equipment; credits =to_whom=.
- =do_form= (builder.c:1004) :: allocates empty rooms in a vnum range; =cmd=-1= names them "The killing fields.".
- =purge_one_room= (builder.c:230) :: dumps occupants and contents into room 0 and removes the room from =room_db=.

** repair (=do_imm_repair=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:2816
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I9 (2009)
:END:
*** Syntax
: repair <player|mob>
: repair all
*** Parameters
- =target=  :: rest of line (=only_argument=); resolved world-wide with =get_char=; literal =all= repairs every connected non-immortal player except the invoker.
*** What it does
Immortal repair of every armor/weapon piece a target owns. Calls =RepairAllArmor(ch, victim, 3)= (choice 3 = worn equipment plus inventory, recursing one level into containers); because the repairer is a PC, =RepairOneArmor= charges no gold and simply sets =value[VA_EFF_AC]= back to =value[VA_MAX_AC]= and clears =ITEM_DAMAGED=, then =reset_affects()= the victim. For a PC victim the position is recomputed with =update_pos= and the victim is told who repaired them; NPC victims are repaired silently. The =cmd == -1= branch (repair self, no message) has no caller anywhere in the tree. With =all=, immortals are skipped.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =connection_list=, =i->connected=, =i->character->funct=, =IS_IMMORTAL(victim)=, target's =equipment[]= and =carrying=
- Writes :: each armor's =obj->value[VA_EFF_AC]=, =CLEAR_BIT(obj->wear_flags, ITEM_DAMAGED)=; weapons via =RepairOneWeapon=; =reset_affects(victim)=; =victim->position= (via =update_pos=)
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

With no argument toggles =PLR_NOSHOUT= on the invoker (stop hearing shouts). With a target: NPCs are refused; a target of equal or higher level is refused ("might object"); otherwise, if the invoker is I5+, the target's =PLR_NOSHOUT= is toggled (set = "gods take away your ability to shout", cleared = "you can shout again"). Since MIN_LEVEL is already I5 the inner level test is always true for registered use. Returns silently if the invoker is an NPC.

** debug (=do_debug=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:2963

:MIN_LEVEL: I9 (2009)
:END:
*** Syntax
: debug <level>
: debug malloc
: debug <player>
*** Parameters
- =level=   :: one argument; a number sets the global =DEBUG= (0 turns debugging off).
- =malloc=  :: literal; calls =MallocDebug(2)= to toggle malloc tracing.
- =player=  :: any non-numeric word; =get_char_vis=; must be a PC.
*** What it does
Three modes. Numeric: sets the global integer =DEBUG= used as a verbosity gate in db.c, mobact.c, account.c, limits.c and player.save.c. =malloc=: toggles the malloc debugging state. A name: toggles the =PLR_DEBUG= bit on a visible player (refused on mobs). If compiled with =MALLOC_DEBUG=, every invocation also dumps the malloc table first.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =arg=, =vict->act=
- Writes :: global =DEBUG=; =SET_BIT/CLEAR_BIT(vict->act, PLR_DEBUG)=; malloc debug state
*** Persistence
- No direct save; =DEBUG= is process-lifetime only; =PLR_DEBUG= persists with the victim's next player save.

:PROPERTIES:

:MIN_LEVEL: I1 (2001)
:END:
*** Syntax
: invisible

*** Parameters
- =level=  :: optional number; clamped to [0, own level]; immortals below I6 are further capped at 999 (so they can never be wizinvis to other immortals).
*** What it does
Wizard invisibility. Without a number it toggles: if =invis_level > 0= it becomes 0 ("totally visible"), otherwise it becomes the invoker's own level ("invisible to all lower levels"). With a number it sets =ch->invis_level= directly, with the clamps above. Quirk: the clamp to own level is skipped when =GET_HIT(ch) == 13= (a hidden back door letting a character with exactly 13 hit points set any invis level, subject to the 999 cap below I6).

- Writes :: =ch->invis_level=
*** Persistence

: noaffect <player>

- =player=  :: required; first word checked for presence, then the whole argument is resolved with =generic_find(FIND_CHAR_WORLD)=; must be a PC of level <= invoker.

Strips every entry in =vict->affected= via =affect_remove= (spells, remort bonuses, item-set affects, everything in that list), tells the victim "You are normal again." Refuses NPCs and higher-level targets. Always ends with "Ok." even after a refusal message. Silently ignored for NPC invokers.

** file (=do_file=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:3065
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I9 (2009)
:END:
*** Syntax
: file <shops|news|credits|donations|services|motd|help|info|welcome|mxp|races|classes|info_classes|termcap|reject_msg|broke|debtor|socials|clan|item_set|sectors|table>
*** Parameters
- =which=  :: one argument, exact match (except =sectors=, which accepts an abbreviation). Anything else prints the usage list.
*** What it does
Hot-reloads one data file from the lib directory into its in-memory global. Text files: =news=, =credits=, =donations=, =services=, =motd= (also refreshes =motd_time= from the file mtime), =help= (help page; prints a reminder to also run =file table=), =info=, =welcome=, =class_help_info= (=info_classes=), =reject_msg=, =pc_is_broke= (=broke=), =pc_is_debtor= (=debtor=), =mxp_elements= (=mxp=, then re-pushes the element list to every MXP-enabled connection). Structured data: =shops= frees the whole =shop_index= list and re-runs =boot_shp= + =assign_the_shopkeepers=; =races= re-reads =race.out=; =classes= re-runs =boot_classes=; =termcap= re-reads terminal definitions; =socials= re-reads socials; =clan= re-reads =clans.out=; =item_set= re-reads =item_sets.out=; =sectors= shells out to =./rebuild-world= then re-reads =sector.out=; =table= closes and rebuilds the =help_table= keyword index. Ends with "Ok." on success.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: files named above under the lib dir; =connection_list= (mxp)
- Writes :: globals =news=, =credits=, =donations=, =services=, =motd=, =motd_time=, =help=, =info=, =welcome=, =mxp_elements=, =classhelp=, =toomany=, =broke=, =debtor=, =shop_index=, race/class/clan/item-set/sector tables, =help_fl=, =help_index=, =top_of_helpt=
*** Persistence
- Read-only with respect to game state (loads files into memory); =sectors= runs the external =./rebuild-world= script, which may rewrite world files.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_DEBUG, "Could not open help file.")= if =help_table= is missing.

** police (=do_police=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:3170
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I8 (2008)
:END:
*** Syntax
: police <fd>
: police <name>
: police -<host prefix>
: police -new
*** Parameters
- =fd=      :: numeric descriptor id (=d->identifier=) as shown by =users=.
- =name=    :: exact player name (case-insensitive) of any connection with a character attached.
- =-host=   :: dash plus a prefix of the connection's host string (=is_abbrev=).
- =-new=    :: literal; targets every connection that is not yet playing (login/menu/creation).
*** What it does
Forcibly disconnects sockets. Each match goes through =police(d, level)=: a playing connection whose character is below =level= is unsnooped, run through =exit_game= (which saves the character) and closed; a non-playing connection is simply closed; a playing character at or above =level= is left alone. Level is the invoker's, except =-new= which uses 2010 (I10). The =-host= form reports "Booting <fd>" for each hit. Quirk: after a numeric fd is not found the code falls through and also tries the name / host branches with the same string.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =connection_list=, =d->identifier=, =d->host=, =d->character->name=, =GET_LEVEL=
- Writes :: closes descriptors (=close_socket=), extracts characters (=exit_game=)
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: via =exit_game= → =save_char(ch, NOWHERE)= → =stash/<c>/<name>= for each booted playing character.

** passwd (=do_passwd=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:3223
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I10 (2010)
:END:
*** Syntax
: passwd <player> <newpassword>
*** Parameters
- =player=       :: first word; must NOT be in the game (=get_char= must fail).
- =newpassword=  :: second word; max 10 characters.
*** What it does
Sets the password stored in an offline player's file. Loads the player into a temporary =char_data= with =load_char=, encrypts the new password with =crypt(password, name)= truncated to 10 chars, stores it in =tmp->pw=, saves and frees the temp character. If the player file names an account, the command warns that the character belonged to that account and clears =tmp->account_name= before saving, i.e. it detaches the character from its account (use =rchar= to clean the account side). Silently ignored for NPC invokers.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: player file =stash/<c>/<name>=
- Writes :: =tmp->pw=, =tmp->account_name= (freed/NULL)
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: =save_char(tmp, NOWHERE)= → =stash/<c>/<name>=.

** clean (=do_clean=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:3267
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I10 (2010)
:END:
*** Syntax
: clean <player|mob>
*** Parameters
- =target=  :: rest of line, =get_char_vis=. (=cmd == -1= would mean "self", but nothing calls it that way.)
*** What it does
Hard reset of a character's derived stats: removes all affects, frees the kill list (=v->kills=), sets all six =armor[]= slots to 100 and =stopping[]= to 0, copies the "real" attributes back over the current ones (STR/INT/WIS/DEX/CON/CHA/LCK), zeroes =hit_bonus=, =dam_bonus= and all five =apply_saving_throw[]=, forces =POSITION_STANDING= and clears every =affected_by= bit. Note it does not re-apply equipment affects afterwards, so worn-gear bonuses are lost until the character re-equips or is otherwise reset.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =GET_R*= real attributes
- Writes :: =v->affected= (emptied), =v->kills= (freed, pointer left dangling), =v->armor[0..5]=, =v->stopping[0..5]=, current attributes, =v->hit_bonus=, =v->dam_bonus=, =v->position=, =v->apply_saving_throw[0..4]=, =v->affected_by=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** resetallplayers (=do_reset_all_players=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:3300
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I11 (2011)
:END:
*** Syntax
: resetallplayers <eq|inv|bank|abank|all>
*** Parameters
- =scope=  :: one argument, exact: which item pool to re-baseline (worn, inventory, bank warehouse, account warehouse, or all). Anything else prints a warning and usage.
*** What it does
Batch "soft" item reset across the entire player base. Reads every name from =players.new= (the player index), skips a hard-coded exclusion list (bunta, countess, syn, korgoth, tasha, aurellia, kali, desmo, khensu), and for each remaining name link-loads the character into the invoker's room, clears its cooldowns (=do_resetcds=), pulse affects, cooldown affects and item-set abilities, runs =do_resetplayer(ch, "<name> <scope> soft")= (re-reads each object from its prototype, keeping mutations, deleting items that no longer exist), then =do_purge(ch, name, 1)= which for a PC goes through =exit_game= and thereby saves and frees the character. Prints "Reset player: X" per player. Warns that it causes very long lag. Bug: the loop calls =affect_remove= on =ch= (the invoker), not on =temp_player=, so the invoker loses their own affects while the loaded players keep theirs. Loading players who are already online would create a second copy; nothing guards against that.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =players.new=, every player file =stash/<c>/<name>=, =character_list=
- Writes :: each loaded player's equipment/inventory/warehouse objects and affects; =ch->affected= (emptied, see bug); =character_list=, room contents (temporary)
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: for each player, =do_purge= → =exit_game= → =save_char(vict, NOWHERE)= → =stash/<c>/<name>=.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_DEBUG, "No player file found.")= when =players.new= is missing.

** linkload (=do_linkload=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:3395
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I9 (2009)
:END:
*** Syntax
: linkload <player>
: linkload <path>
*** Parameters
- =player=  :: one argument; a bare name is mapped to =stash/<c>/<name>= via =name_to_path=; an argument starting with =~=, =.= or =/= is used literally as a file path.
*** What it does
Loads an offline player's file into the world as a link-dead character standing in the invoker's room, via =link_load_char=: the new =char_data= is pushed onto =character_list=, =timer= is zeroed and =was_in_room= is set to the invoker's room so a later =trans= / =at= does not send them back. The room sees "$n has been link-loaded." The character has no descriptor; it can then be inspected, =set=, given items, and =purge=d (which saves it). Silently ignored for NPC invokers.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: player file
- Writes :: =character_list=, room people list, =tmp->timer=, =tmp->was_in_room=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; the loaded character is saved only when later purged / extracted through =exit_game=.

** crash (=do_crash=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:3416
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I10 (2010)
:END:
*** Syntax
: crash <anything>
*** Parameters
- =text=  :: rest of line, copied with =$= escapes collapsed (the copy is unused: the =popen= block that consumed it is =#if 0=).
*** What it does
Deliberate crash test, gated on the invoker's title: does nothing unless =ch->title= contains "Crash"; if it also contains "Hard", computes =ch->level / zero()= where =zero()= returns 0, raising SIGFPE and taking the server down (into the crash-save handler, or a core dump if =dumpmode= turned crash save off). Also, =arg++= before copying skips the first character of the argument unconditionally.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->title=, =ch->level=
- Writes :: none (process terminates)
*** Persistence
- No direct save; a triggered crash runs =signal_fatal= → =save_all_players= when crash save is on.

*** Syntax

*** Parameters
- =substring=  :: one argument, lower-cased; 3..10 characters; any new name containing it is rejected.
- =-exactname= :: leading dash makes it an exact-match ban instead.
*** What it does
Appends a banned-name pattern to the =trashcan= file consulted by =new_name_ok= at character creation. Refuses if the pattern is too short/long or if =new_name_ok(pattern)= already fails (which also catches mob names, fill words and existing entries). Reports whether a literal string or substring pattern was added.

- Writes :: appends one line to =trashcan=
*** Persistence

*** What it does
Without a number reports the current =max_players= cap. With a number (I9+) sets the global =max_players=, refusing values above the compile/boot-time =abs_max_con=, and writes the new value to the =maxplayers= file so it can be re-read at boot. Non-numeric arguments print a hint.

*** What it does
Iterates =character_list= and, for every non-mob character (including link-loaded ones and the invoker), runs =do_set(ch, "<name> <field> <value>", cmd)=. All of =do_set='s level and field checks apply per target. Ignored for mob invokers.

** revoke, arevoke (=do_revoke=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:3554
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I9 (2009)
:END:
*** Syntax
: revoke <player> <command>
: arevoke <account|@player> <command>
*** Parameters
- =player=   :: (=cmd == CMD_REVOKE=, 32) a visible character, =get_char_vis=.
- =account=  :: (=cmd == CMD_AREVOKE=, 34) an account name, or =@name= to resolve a player's account (=get_account_char=).
- =command=  :: a command name, resolved with =find_command=; its =cmd_info= minimum level must not exceed the invoker's.
*** What it does
Manages the per-character (=revoke=) or per-account (=arevoke=) command grant/revoke lists that the interpreter consults before running a command. If the command is currently on the target's =granted= list, that grant is removed instead ("Grant removed"/"Agrant removed") and nothing else happens. Otherwise the command is added to the =revoked= list (refusing duplicates). Refuses targets of higher level than the invoker and commands the invoker could not use themselves. Player targets are told about the change.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =cmd_info[]=, =v->granted=, =v->revoked=, =a->granted=, =a->revoked=
- Writes :: =v->revoked= / =v->granted= (=cmd_list= arrays, realloc'd) or the account's equivalents
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: account form only, =save_acct(a)= → =account/<c>/<name>=. Character form: no direct save; persisted with the next routine player save.

** grant, agrant (=do_grant=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:3618
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I9 (2009)
:END:
*** Syntax
: grant <player> <command>
: agrant <account|@player> <command>
*** Parameters
- =player=   :: (=cmd == CMD_GRANT=, 31) visible character.
- =account=  :: (=cmd == CMD_AGRANT=, 33) account name or =@player=.
- =command=  :: command name via =find_command=; minimum level must be <= invoker's level.
*** What it does
Mirror image of =revoke=. If the command is on the target's =revoked= list, the revoke is lifted ("Revoke removed"/"Arevoke removed") and the function returns. Otherwise the command is added to the =granted= list, letting the character (or every character on the account) use a command above their level. Same level/ownership refusals as =revoke=; player targets are notified.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =cmd_info[]=, grant/revoke lists
- Writes :: =v->granted= / =v->revoked= or =a->granted= / =a->revoked=
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: account form only, =save_acct(a)= → =account/<c>/<name>=. Character form: no direct save; persisted with the next routine player save.

** account (=do_account=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:3756
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: account
: account report <account|@player>

: account add <account|@player> <dollars[.cents]>
: account sub <account|@player> <dollars[.cents]>
*** Parameters
- =subcommand=  :: abbreviation of report/set/add/sub (I10+ only).
- =target=      :: account name or =@player= (=get_account_char=).
- =amount=      :: =parse_dollars_cents=: optional leading =-=, dollars, optional =.cc= (a single digit after the point is treated as tens of cents). Stored as integer cents.
*** What it does
Requires an attached descriptor with an account. Below I10 the command is read-only: shows the invoker's own real-money account balance (=acct->account=, in cents), paging the =pc_is_debtor= text if negative or =pc_is_broke= if zero. At I10+ it administers any account's balance: =report= prints it; =set=/=add=/=sub= change it, print old and new balance, log the change and save the account. Malformed input prints the four-line usage.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->desc->acct->account=, globals =debtor=, =broke=
- Writes :: =a->account= (target account's balance in cents)
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: =save_acct(a)= → =account/<c>/<name>= after set/add/sub.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_ACCOUNT, "ACNT:<imm>,<target>,<op>:<amount>,<old>,<new>")=.

** lockout (=do_lockout=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:3836
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I9 (2009)
:END:
*** Syntax
: lockout <player> <minutes> <reason>
*** Parameters
- =player=   :: =get_char_vis_world=; must be a PC of lower level than the invoker.
- =minutes=  :: integer > 0 (converted to seconds).
- =reason=   :: rest of line, stored and shown to the player.
*** What it does
Temporary ban. Sets =v->lockout_till = now + minutes*60= and =v->lockout_message=, tells the player why they are being kicked, then =exit_game(v, 0)= which saves the character and, because =lockout_till= is set, puts the descriptor into =CON_WIZLOCK= instead of the account menu. Login code refuses the character until the timestamp passes and shows the stored message. Missing reason prints usage but still proceeds (with an empty/odd message since =arg++= is applied regardless).
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: levels
- Writes :: =v->lockout_till=, =v->lockout_message=; descriptor state via =exit_game=
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: =exit_game= → =save_char(v, NOWHERE)= → =stash/<c>/<name>= (lockout fields are in the player file).

** dumpmode (=do_coredump=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:3876
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I10 (2010)
:FLAGS: CMDF_DISABLED
:END:
*** Syntax
: dumpmode
: dumpmode <n>
*** Parameters
- =n=  :: optional integer; > 0 means "give me a core dump", anything else (including 0) re-enables crash save.
*** What it does
Controls what happens on a fatal signal. With no argument reports whether =crash_save= is on. =n > 0= calls =crash_save_off=, which resets SIGABRT/SIGFPE/SIGBUS/SIGSEGV/SIGPWR/SIGSYS to =SIG_DFL= and sets =crash_save = 0=, so a crash produces a core dump instead of saving players. Otherwise =crash_save_on= reinstalls =signal_fatal= (which calls =save_all_players=) and sets =crash_save = 1=. The command is registered with =CMDF_DISABLED=, so it is off until an I9+ runs =enable dumpmode=.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: global =crash_save=
- Writes :: global =crash_save=, process signal handlers
*** Persistence
- No direct save; process-lifetime setting.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_DEBUG, "Crash save off.")= from =crash_off=.

*** Syntax
: rchar <account> <charname>

*** What it does
Removes a character name from an account's =char_list= via =remove_char=, which shifts the array down, decrements =num_chars=, logs the removal and saves the account. Intended cleanup after =passwd= detaches a character. The player file itself is untouched. "Done." is printed twice on success (once inside the branch and once unconditionally after =free_acct=).
*** Data touched

- Writes :: =a->char_list[]=, =a->num_chars=
*** Persistence

- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_DELETE, "<char> deleted from <account> account")=.

** adelete (=do_adelete=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:3997
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I10 (2010)
:END:
*** Syntax
: adelete <account>
*** Parameters
- =account=  :: one argument, account name.
*** What it does
Deletes an entire account through =delete_account=: requires the =PURGED/account= directory to exist (else logs urgent and fails); disconnects every connection logged into that account (unsnoop, =exit_game= if playing, =close_socket=); calls =delete_char= on every character in =char_list= (each logged); drops all references; moves the account file into =PURGED/account= with =/bin/mv=; decrements =num_accounts=. Silently ignored for NPC invokers.
*** Data touched

- Writes :: descriptors closed, characters extracted and deleted, =num_accounts=
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: account file =account/<c>/<name>= moved to =PURGED/account/=; player files handled by =delete_char= (moved/removed per its implementation); =exit_game= saves any online character first.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_DELETE, "<imm> deleted account <name>")=; from =delete_account=: =LOG_DELETE "<char> deleted"= per character, =LOG_DELETE "Account <name> deleted"=, =LOG_URGENT= if =PURGED/account= is missing.

** aset (=do_aset=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:4034
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I5 (2005)
:END:
*** Syntax
: aset
: aset <account|@player> <field> [value]
*** Parameters
- =target=  :: account name or =@player= (=get_account_char=).
- =field=   :: abbreviation of an entry in =aset_field[]=, filtered by the invoker's level: banished (I6), email (I9), log (I8), maiden (I9), name (I9), nonew (I7), password (I9), silence (I5), stupid (I5), nopurge (I7), noshout (I5), ver_exempt (I9), ver_mailed (I9), verified (I9).
- =value=   :: rest of line. Strings: free text (password max 10 chars). Flags: =0=, =1=, or omitted to toggle.
*** What it does
Edits account-level fields. With no argument lists the fields the invoker may set. String fields set =email_address=, =maiden_name=, =real_name=, or =pw= (crypt'ed with the account name as salt, truncated to 10). Flag fields set/clear a bit in =a->flags= (=A_BANISHED=, =A_LOG=, =A_NONEW=, =A_SILENCE=, =A_STUPID=, =A_NOPURGE=, =A_NOSHOUT=, =A_VER_EXEMPT=, =A_VER_MAILED=, =A_VERIFIED=). Setting =banished= additionally hauls every online character of the account out of combat and into room 6999 (=LOAD_ROOM_BANISHED=); clearing it moves them to =LOAD_ROOM_CRIMINAL= (64490) if their =rent_disable= is still in the future, else to their bind point / 3000. The "simple integer" case 2 exists but no field uses it. Always saves the account on success.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =aset_field[]=, =a->char_list=, =vict->rent_disable=
- Writes :: =a->email_address=, =a->maiden_name=, =a->real_name=, =a->pw=, =a->flags=; online characters' room and fighting state
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: =save_acct(a)= → =account/<c>/<name>=.

:PROPERTIES:

:END:
*** Syntax
: comment <account|@player> read

*** Parameters

- =mode=    :: abbreviation of read (I5+), write (any I1+), clear (I9+).
*** What it does
Immortal notes attached to an account. =read= pages =a->notes= through a string block. =write= puts the invoker into the line editor (=ch->desc->str= pointing at an =acct_edit= block, terminated with =@=); when the editor finishes, =save_acct_edit= reloads the account, appends the new text plus newline to the existing notes and saves. =clear= frees the notes and saves. Leak: the =clear= level refusal returns without =free_acct(a)=, leaving a reference on the account.

- Writes :: =a->notes=; =ch->user_data=, =ch->desc->str=, =ch->desc->max_str=, =ch->funct= (editor setup)
*** Persistence

- Log lines: =LOG_URGENT= from =save_acct_edit= if the account vanished during editing.

*** Syntax

*** What it does
Clears =CMDF_DISABLED= on the named =cmd_info[]= entry, making it usable again. Logs the change, and if the command is =gossip= announces the re-enable to everyone except the invoker. Then rewrites the =disable= file from scratch with the names of all still-disabled commands (this file is read by db.c at boot to re-apply disables). Ignored for NPC invokers.

:PROPERTIES:

:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)

:FLAGS: CMDF_NODISABLE
:END:
*** Syntax
: disable
: disable <command>
*** Parameters
- =command=  :: optional command name. No argument lists the currently disabled commands.
*** What it does
Sets =CMDF_DISABLED= on the named command so the interpreter refuses it for everyone. Refuses commands flagged =CMDF_NODISABLE= (=enable=/=disable= themselves) and ones already disabled. Logs the change, announces if it is =gossip=, and appends the command name to the =disable= file. Ignored for NPC invokers.

- Writes :: =cmd_info[cmd_no].flags |= CMDF_DISABLED=
*** Persistence

- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_MISC, "Command \"X\" disabled.")=; =LOG_URGENT= on file error.

** arena (=do_arena=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:4376
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: arena
*** Parameters
- none.
*** What it does
Mortal entry into an open arena match (matches are opened by the immortal =chaos=/arena-start command that sets =chaos_lo=, =chaos_hi= and =arena_ticks_till_start=). Refuses if the player is not in worldzone 0 (PR-CLASSIC), if no match is accepting entrants (=arena_ticks_till_start <= 0=), if the player's level is outside [=chaos_lo=, =chaos_hi=], if banished (=PLR_BANISHED= or account =A_BANISHED=), or if already in an =ARENA= room. Entry costs =arena_cost_per_level * level= gold, which is moved into =arena_pot=. The player is then fully restored (=do_restore(ch,"",-1)=), stripped of all affects, taken out of combat, stops following, is moved to a random room in 900..963 (=ARENA_START + rand(ARENA_ROOMS)=, 8x8), looks, and a SPORTS NEWS broadcast announces the new pot. NPCs and descriptor-less characters are ignored.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =GET_WORLDZONE(ch)=, =arena_ticks_till_start=, =chaos_lo/hi=, =ch->act=, account flags, =ROOM_FLAG(in_room, ARENA)=, =GET_GOLD(ch)=
- Writes :: =ch->gold=, global =arena_pot=, =ch->affected= (emptied), fighting/follower state, =ch->in_room=; hit/mana/move via =do_restore=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** goodies (=do_goodies=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:4443
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I9 (2009)
:END:
*** Syntax
: goodies <vnumlo> [vnumhi] [count]
*** Parameters
- =vnumlo=  :: required object vnum (0 is invalid).
- =vnumhi=  :: optional; defaults to =vnumlo= if smaller/missing.
- =count=   :: optional total number of objects to load, capped at 1024; 0/omitted = one of each vnum found.
*** What it does
Builds a fresh, prototype-less container object ("a bag of goodies", =ITEM_CONTAINER=, takeable, cost 100000) directly in =object_list=, then walks =obj_db= for every object prototype in [vnumlo, vnumhi] (up to 1024 entries), loads =count= objects cycling through the found vnums with =do_load_object= (which enforces max-exist limits only below I9) and puts them in the bag, and finally gives the bag to the invoker. Room sees "makes a strange magical gesture" / "has created a bag of goodies". If no prototypes fall in range, nothing happens and nothing is said.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =obj_db= via =hash_iterate_range=
- Writes :: =object_list= (new bag + loaded objects), =ch->carrying=, per-prototype world counts (via =do_load_object=)
*** Persistence
- No direct save; the bag has no vnum/prototype so it will not survive a player save/rent cleanly.

** newbieset (=do_newbieset=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:4503
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I9 (2009)
:END:
*** Syntax
: newbieset <player>
*** Parameters
- =player=  :: first word; must be a PC visible in the same room (=get_char_room_vis=), level 1..100.
*** What it does
Loads a fixed kit of 20 objects (vnums 102004, 21003, 3904, 2134, 15807, 5214, 3907, 3903, 1592, 6861, 5230, 6511, 93050, 2133, 5109, 1330, 1322, 3902, 6856, 9209: pollen sack, strange boots, studded leather set, fiery red cloak, spiked helm, quartz ball, bracelet, wristband, pale blue stone, dwarven ring, ring of tomorrow, vulcan mail, gauntlets, shield of pharaoh, amulet of the light, boar tusk, dwarven hammer) via =do_load_object= and drops each into the target's inventory, then tells them to =wear all=. Declared with only two parameters (=ch, argument=) although the dispatch table passes three; works on the C ABI but is a prototype mismatch. Any =do_load_object= failure returns NULL, which =obj_to_char= would dereference.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =victim->level=
- Writes :: =victim->carrying= (20 new objects), =object_list=, prototype world counts
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
