** maze (=do_maze=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:4668
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I9 (2009)
:END:
*** Syntax
: maze <startroom> <width> <height>
*** Parameters
- =startroom=  :: first vnum of a contiguous block of =width*height= rooms, all of which must not yet exist.
- =width=, =height=  :: each >= 2.
*** What it does
Generates a teleport maze in memory. Refuses if any room in the block already exists. Calls =do_form(ch, "<start> <end>", -2)= to allocate the rooms (cmd -2 names them "The Grudge Arena" / "You know you want to kill someone."), then =create_maze(start, width, height)= (maze.c) to carve exits, then flags every room =ARENA|RELOC_DISTORT= with =tele_time = 0=, =tele_look = 1= and a random =tele_targ= inside the block, so rooms randomly relocate occupants within the maze.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: room table via =get_rp=
- Writes :: new =room_data= entries (=allocate_room=, name, description, zone, exits), =rp->room_flags=, =rp->tele_time=, =rp->tele_look=, =rp->tele_targ=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; rooms exist in memory only until a builder saves the zone (=rsave= / =save_range=).

** atell (=do_atell=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: wizard.c:4720
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I9 (2009)
:END:
*** Syntax
: atell <account|@player> <message>
*** Parameters
- =target=   :: account name or =@player= (=half_chop= first word).
- =message=  :: rest of line, passed through =fix_spec= (escapes format specifiers/colour).
*** What it does
Sends a tell to whichever character is currently playing on a given account, regardless of which character that is. Looks up the account, scans =connection_list= for descriptors whose =acct= is that account and takes the last playing one's character as the recipient; drops the recipient if the sender cannot see them. Both sides get the blue "tells you" / "You tell" lines. Bug: both messages substitute =vict->name= (the recipient) where the sender's name is intended, so the recipient sees "<their own name> tells you '...'".
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =connection_list=, =d->acct=, account file (reference-counted via =get_account_char= / =free_acct=)
- Writes :: none
*** Persistence
- Read-only; no state change.

** Helpers in wizard.c (part B)
- =police= (3155) :: disconnect one descriptor if not playing or if its character is below the given level; playing characters go through =exit_game= (saved) then =close_socket=.
- =link_load_char= (3364) :: load an offline player file (name → =stash/<c>/<name>=, or literal path) into =character_list= and the caller's room, with =was_in_room= set; used by =linkload=, =resetallplayers= and =fetch_char=.
- =zero= (3411) :: returns 0; exists only so =do_crash= can divide by it.
- =in_list= / =remove_list= / =add_list= (3517/3529/3543) :: manipulate a =cmd_list= (realloc'd =s32= array of command indices) for grant/revoke.
- =calc_dollars= / =calc_cents= / =fmt_dollars_cents= / =parse_dollars_cents= (3695..3728) :: convert between integer cents and "$D.CC" strings for =account=.
- =fetch_char= (3731) :: find a PC by name in =character_list= or on any descriptor, else link-load it (sets =*loaded=); not used by the commands in this range.
- =in_bad_room= (3914) :: true unless the room is one of the I1 load rooms, in the character's build range, above =build_floor=, or room 64491; used by the =PLR_LEASH= check in interpreter.c.
- =in_wrong_world= (3931) :: world-zone containment check; every path returns 0 (the two "changed to 0 -perci" edits made it a no-op).
- =get_world_ch= (3977) :: index of the =worldzone[]= range containing the character's room, or -1.
- =aset_field[]= (4020) :: table of =aset= field names, types (0 string / 1 flag / 2 int), flag bits and minimum immortal level.
- =add_to_goodies_list= (4429) :: =hash_iterate_range= callback collecting object vnums (max 1024) for =goodies=.
- =save_all_players= (4706) :: emergency save (called from the fatal-signal handler): =remove_junk= then =save_char(ch, NOWHERE)= for every PC in =character_list=.
- =set_player_remort_bonus= (4768) :: implementation of =set <player> rbonus ...= (list/add/all/none/rcount/mod), saving the victim with =do_save= after changes; belongs to =do_set=, not to any command in this range.
- External: =RepairAllArmor= (spec_mob.c:542), =get_account_char= (stat.c:22), =get_account= / =save_acct= / =delete_account= / =remove_char= / =save_acct_edit= (account.c), =new_name_ok= (utils.c:984), =do_resetplayer= (utils.c:1537), =create_maze= (maze.c:73), =crash_save_on= / =crash_save_off= (signals.c), =exit_game= (handler.c:4594).

* offensive.c — combat (31 commands)

** hit (=do_hit=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:151
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: hit <victim> [location]
*** Parameters
- =victim=   :: first word, =argument_interpreter=; resolved with =get_char_room_vis=.
- =location= :: optional second word; only its first letter matters: f/c/t (feet, claws, tail) → 1, l (legs) → 2, a/w (arms, wings) → 3, h (head) → 5, anything else → 4; omitted → =LOCATION_BODY= (4).
- =cmd=      :: =CMD_HIT= (56); not examined (=do_kill= passes 0).
*** What it does
Starts ordinary melee. Refused in a PEACEFUL room (=check_peaceful=, immortals > I8 exempt), then =unsneak(ch)= (drops sneak, prowl, vanish, shimmer). Hitting yourself just prints a joke. Otherwise =CORRUPT(ch,victim)= must allow the fight (pet/charmie rules, 500-level spread rule =MIN_CHOSEN_LEVEL_DIFF=, WORLD_CLASSIC clan PK flags) and =find_criminal=/=make_criminal= may flag the attacker as a criminal ("hit"). A charmed attacker whose master may not attack the victim, or who targets its own master, is refused. A PC victim under =SPELL_SHIMMER= is untouchable (miss messages, no fight started). No skill check at all: if the attacker is exactly POSITION_STANDING and =victim != ch->fighting=, the aim location is stored in =ch->aimloc= (clamped by =locations[HeightClass(ch)-HeightClass(victim)+6][9]=), "Aiming for the ..." is printed, =hit(ch,victim,TYPE_UNDEFINED)= runs one attack round (=hit()= itself calls =AddHated(victim,ch,HOUR)=, =start_fighting= both ways, consumes 1 move, thac0 roll, =hit_landed=→=damage=), and the attacker gets =WAIT_STATE(ch, 2*WAIT_SEC)=. Already fighting that victim, or not standing (e.g. sitting, running) → "You do the best you can!" and nothing happens.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->fighting=, =ch->position=, =ch->master=, =ch->affected= (AFF_CHARM), =victim->affected= (SPELL_SHIMMER), room flags via =CORRUPT=, clan data.
- Writes :: =ch->aimloc=; via =unsneak= removes affects; via =stop_running= clears =ch->next_move=/=remaining_moves=; via =hit()=: =ch->fighting=, =victim->fighting=, =victim->attackers=/=ch->attackers=, =ch->move=, =victim->hates= (=AddHated=), hit points via =damage=; =ch->wait= += 2 s; =make_criminal= may set criminal state and summon police.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =make_criminal= → =vlog(LOG_CHEAT, "Char X attacks Y with 'hit'")= when a PC attacks a PC-flagged victim without consent.

** kill, slay (=do_kill=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:245
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting) for kill; DEA (dead) for slay
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal) for kill; I9 (2009) for slay
:END:
*** Syntax
: kill <victim>
: slay <victim>
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: first word (=argument_interpreter=), =get_char_room_vis=.
- =cmd=    :: =CMD_KILL= (55) → plain =do_hit=; =CMD_SLAY= (60) → immortal instant kill.
*** What it does
Always =unsneak(ch)= first. Unless the caller is a PC of level ≥ I9 *and* =cmd == CMD_SLAY=, this is exactly =do_hit(ch, argument, 0)= (so =kill= is an alias for =hit=). Slay: no target → "Kill who?"; self → refused. If the slayer is below I10 and the victim is above I9, the *slayer* dies instead (=die(ch)=) and the victim is told who tried. Otherwise gory messages and =die(victim)= (=dieWithXPPenalty(1.0)=: exp penalty, corpse, =extract_char=). No wait state, no skill roll.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =GET_LEVEL(ch)=, =GET_LEVEL(victim)=, =IS_NPC(ch)=, =cmd=.
- Writes :: everything =die()= touches on the dead character (exp, hp, position, room, corpse objects, extraction); via =do_hit= for the kill path.
*** Persistence
- No direct save here; =die()= → =dieWithXPPenalty= handles death bookkeeping (player death path saves/extracts the character; see fight.c:531).
- Log lines: =dieWithXPPenalty= → =vlog(LOG_DEATH, ...)= for player deaths.

** backstab (=do_backstab=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:283
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: backstab <victim>
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=, =get_char_room_vis= (required).
- =cmd=    :: =CMD_BACKSTAB= (61); unused.
*** What it does
Peaceful-room check. Requires a wielded weapon whose =value[3]= (damage type) is 1, 10 or 11 (pierce/stab types). Refused if =victim->attackers >= 3= (unless ch ≥ I5), if ch is already fighting (unless ≥ I5), or if the victim is fighting and ch is not ASSASSIN/NINJA/≥ I5. =CORRUPT= and =find_criminal=/=make_criminal("backstab")= run *before* the weapon-type and busy checks. Skill check: =percent = number(1,101)=; =percent += 50-DEX-LCK= if the victim already =Hates= ch and is not fighting; =percent -= 50= if the victim cannot see ch. Success when =percent < get_skill(SKILL_BACKSTAB) + remortBonus + stealth_penalty(ch)= where remortBonus = 2×REMORT_IMPROVE_BACKSTAB + REMORT_ADVANCED_SKILL_BACKSTAB (+5 if the victim is in SKILL_SHADOWFORM). Success: =AddHated(victim,ch,2*HOUR)=, temporary =ch->hit_bonus += base= (base = 4 if the victim was idle, 0 if fighting, +8 if asleep, +2 always), =apply_shadowform_bonus(ch)= (3% chance of a 3-pulse shadowform), =hit(ch,victim,SKILL_BACKSTAB)= (backstab multiplier applied inside =hit_landed=), hit_bonus restored, =apply_backstab_bonus_stack(ch)= (stacking BACKSTAB_BONUS_STACK buffs: melee haste, all-stopping, APPLY_BACKSTAB), =add_combo_point(1-2)=. Failure: =AddHated(HOUR)=, =apply_shadowform_bonus=, =damage(ch,victim,0,SKILL_BACKSTAB)= (starts the fight), =FailSkill(SKILL_BACKSTAB)= (may reset the skill to 1 if above guild max), 35% chance of the bonus stack + combo point anyway. Wait: 2 s if REMORT_ADVANCED_SKILL_BACKSTAB bonus > 10, else 3 s. PLR_DEBUG prints the roll.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->equipment[WIELD]->value[3]=, =victim->attackers=, =ch->fighting=, =victim->fighting=, class, skills, remort effects, =victim->hates=, =victim->affected_pulse=.
- Writes :: =ch->hit_bonus= (temporarily), =victim->hates= + =ACT_HATEFUL=, fighting pointers/=attackers= via =hit=/=damage=, victim hp, =ch->affected_pulse= (shadowform, BACKSTAB_BONUS_STACK), =victim->affected_pulse= (AFFECT_COMBO_POINT), skill value via =FailSkill=, =ch->wait= (2–3 s), sneak/prowl/vanish/shimmer removed by =unsneak=.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =make_criminal= → =vlog(LOG_CHEAT, ... 'backstab')= when applicable.

** order (=do_order=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:394
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 2
:FLAGS: CMDF_NOARENA
:END:
*** Syntax
: order <charmie> <command...>
: order follower|followers <command...>
*** Parameters
- =charmie= :: first word (=half_chop=); =get_char_room_vis=; literal "follower"/"followers" targets every charmed follower in the room.
- =command= :: the rest of the line, fed to =command_interpreter= as the follower.
*** What it does
=unsneak(ch)=. Refused if either part is missing, if ch has =PLR_STUPID= or account flag =A_STUPID= (tongue twisted), if ordering yourself, if the target is ignoring you, or if ch is itself charmed. Single target: refused while the target is writing (=v->desc->str=); everybody sees "$n gives $N an order"; if =v->master != ch= or v is not =AFF_CHARM= the target just looks indifferent; if =v->wait= is non-zero they're "too busy". Otherwise the message is parsed with =command_interpreter(v,&p)=; if the resolved command carries =CMDF_NOORDER= it is refused and logged as a cheat attempt; else =command_process(v, cmd_no, p)= executes it as the follower. "followers": every =AFF_CHARM= follower in the same room that has no =wait= runs the command (busy ones are reported); note the =CMDF_NOORDER= check is *not* applied on this path. "Nobody here is a loyal subject" if none qualified. No wait state is imposed on the orderer.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->act= (PLR_STUPID), account flags, =ch->followers=, =k->follower->in_room=, =v->master=, =v->wait=, =v->desc->str=, =cmd_info[].flags=.
- Writes :: nothing directly; whatever the ordered command does to the follower.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_CHEAT, "%s tried to order %s to %s")= on a CMDF_NOORDER command; =vlog(LOG_URGENT, "k->follower is NULL in do_order(followers)!!!")= on a corrupt follower list.

** flee (=do_flee=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:498
:MIN_POS: SIT (sitting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:FLAGS: CMDF_FLUSH (priority 100: flushes lower-priority queued commands)
:END:
*** Syntax
: flee
*** Parameters
- none (argument ignored).
*** What it does
Refused silently while paralysed, confused, or with =ch->wait > PULSE_VIOLENCE=; refused with a message while falling (=fallen_rooms=), held by SPELL_CLINGING_VINES, under the BERZERK affect, or at/below POSITION_STUNNED. Blind fleers have a 5% chance (roll < 5 of 101) to run into something: 0–2 s wait and nothing else. =stop_running=. If sitting or lower (and not RACE_BIRD) the character only scrambles to POSITION_STANDING with a 2 s wait — the flee itself needs a second command. With no exits: "nowhere to run". Not fighting: up to 6 random exit picks, skipping DEATH rooms, =EX_HIDDEN= exits and NO_MOB rooms for NPCs; the first passable pick is taken via =Move(ch,d,0)= (success → "You flee head over heels", failure → "too exhausted") and the function returns; no valid pick in 6 tries → "PANIC!". Fighting: if the opponent is not in the same room, =stop_fighting(ch)= and a LOG_DEBUG line. Otherwise up to 6 random picks (DEATH and NPC/NO_MOB skipped; hidden exits *not* skipped here); the first =CAN_GO= pick is attempted: if =GET_MOVE(ch) >= 0= and =Move= succeeds, an NPC fleer =AddFeared(ch,opponent,HOUR)= (except mob vnum 112100), a PC fleer rolls against the opponent's hp% and, when the opponent is a PC, calls =AddHated(ch,opponent,HOUR)= and =SetHunting(opponent,ch)= (the latter is a no-op for PCs — see notes); arena contests get a =sports_cast=; then =stop_fighting(opponent)= if it was fighting ch, and =stop_fighting(ch)=. If the move fails: exhausted message and return. The experience-loss code is commented out (=amtToLose= is always 0). No skill check.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->fallen_rooms=, =ch->wait=, affects/pulse affects, =ch->position=, =ch->race=, room exits (=rp->exits=, =exit_info=, =to_room= flags), =ch->fighting=, =GET_MOVE=, opponent hp.
- Writes :: =ch->position= (→ STANDING when scrambling up), =ch->wait= (0–2 s), room/position changes via =Move= (in_room, movement points, followers), =ch->fears= / =ch->hates= lists (=AddFeared=/=AddHated=), fighting pointers, =attackers= and positions via =stop_fighting= (also resets every character fighting the target to POSITION_STANDING).
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save (room change is picked up by =Move=).
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_DEBUG, "%s fighting %s while not in same room. Corrected by flee.")=; =sports_cast= in arena contests.

** bash (=do_bash=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:811
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: bash [victim]
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; defaults to =ch->fighting= when absent/not found. Mobs always bash their current opponent.
*** What it does
Wrapper: =unsneak=, resolve target, refuse under the BERZERK affect or targeting self, then =phys_bash(ch,victim)=. =phys_bash=: peaceful-room check; refused if =ch->attackers > 10=, =victim->attackers >= 12=, no SKILL_BASH, DRUID not in =AFF_BEARFORM=, or (after =stop_running=) not standing. =CORRUPT= + criminal check ("bash"). Roll: =percent = number(1,101) - dex_app[DEX(ch)].reaction*10 + dex_app[DEX(victim)].reaction*10=; success when =percent <= get_skill(SKILL_BASH) + remortBonus= (remortBonus = 2×REMORT_IMPROVE_BASH, capped so skill+bonus ≤ 100). Failure (victim above STUNNED): attacker falls to POSITION_SITTING, =damage(ch,victim,0,SKILL_BASH)= (starts the fight), =damage(ch,ch,10,TYPE_UNDEFINED)= (10 self-damage), =FailSkill(SKILL_BASH)=, 2 s wait. Success but victim already below POSITION_FIGHTING (sitting or worse): attacker sits, takes 10 self-damage, 2 s wait, "fall on your face". Success on a standing victim: =WAIT_STATE(victim, WAIT_SEC*(4 + number(0, 8+extra)/4))= (4–6 s, extra = REMORT_IMPROVE_BASH/5), =victim->position = POSITION_SITTING=, 1–2 combo points, =damage(ch,victim,5,SKILL_BASH)=; attacker =WAIT_STATE(ch, WAIT_SEC*(5 + number(0, 7-extra)/4))= (5–6.75 s).
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->attackers=, =victim->attackers=, skills, class, =ch->affected= (AFF_BEARFORM, BERZERK), =ch->position=, =victim->position=, =dex_app[]=, remort data.
- Writes :: =ch->position= (SITTING on failure), =victim->position= (SITTING on success), =ch->wait=, =victim->wait=, hp of both via =damage=, fighting pointers/=attackers= via =damage=→=start_fighting=, =victim->affected_pulse= (combo points), skill value via =FailSkill=, sneak affects via =unsneak=.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =make_criminal= LOG_CHEAT when applicable.

** pummel (=do_pummel=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:942
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: pummel [victim]
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; defaults to =ch->fighting=. Mobs always pummel their opponent.
*** What it does
Same wrapper as bash (unsneak, BERZERK and self refusals) then =phys_pummel=. Preconditions mirror =phys_bash= (attackers 10/12, SKILL_PUMMEL known, standing) minus the druid rule. Roll identical to bash but against =SKILL_PUMMEL + REMORT_IMPROVE_PUMMEL= (×1, capped to 100). Failure: attacker sits, =damage(victim,0,SKILL_PUMMEL)=, 10 self-damage, =FailSkill=, 2 s wait. Success on a victim below POSITION_FIGHTING: no effect, message, =WAIT_STATE(ch, WAIT_SEC*2 - remort_count/30)=. Success on a standing victim: =dam = dice(4, ((DEX + STR/2) * (remort_count + avg_weapon_dam) + level) / (30 - remort_count/10))= where avg_weapon_dam = =damnodice*(damsizedice/2+0.5) + DAMROLL + str_app[16].todam=; crit (×2) if =number(1,101) <= (DEX+LCK+INT+remort_count+dam_bonus+level/10)/10=; × remort multiplier (IMPROVE_PUMMEL, 50); halved against AFF_SANCTUARY. If the same roll > =15 - REMORT_IMPROVE_PUMMEL=: knockdown — =WAIT_STATE(victim, WAIT_SEC*(4 + number(extra, 8+extra)/4))=, =victim->position = POSITION_SITTING=, combo 1–2, =damage(dam, SKILL_PUMMEL)=. Otherwise damage is divided by =(1.3 - bonus/100)=, "fails to knock them down", victim wait =(2 + number(extra, 3+extra)/4)= s, =damage(dam, TYPE_UNDEFINED)=, combo 1–2. Attacker wait =(5 + number(0, 7-extra)/4)= s either way.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: as bash plus =ch->damnodice=, =ch->damsizedice=, =GET_DAMROLL=, =ch->dam_bonus=, =ch->remort_count=, =victim->affected= (AFF_SANCTUARY).
- Writes :: =ch->position=, =victim->position=, =ch->wait=, =victim->wait=, hp of both, fighting pointers/=attackers=, combo-point pulse affects, skill via =FailSkill=.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =make_criminal= LOG_CHEAT when applicable.

** rescue (=do_rescue=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:980
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:FLAGS: CMDF_FLUSH (priority 50)
:END:
*** Syntax
: rescue <victim>
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=, =get_char_room_vis=; required (the auto-pick of a group member is commented out).
*** What it does
Peaceful-room check. Refused for self, for your own opponent, or when =victim->attackers >= 3 + max(dex_app[DEX].reaction, 0)=. Someone in the room must have =fighting == victim=, else "nobody is fighting". Only immortals or =is_fighter= classes (CLASS flag FLAG_FIGHTER) may proceed. Skill: =number(1,101) > get_skill(SKILL_RESCUE)= → fail, =FailSkill(SKILL_RESCUE)=, no wait. Success: =CORRUPT(ch,attacker)= and criminal check ("rescue") against the attacker found; messages; then =tmp_ch = victim->fighting= (the victim's *own* target, which may differ from the attacker found, or be NULL if the victim is not fighting back); if set, =stop_fighting(victim->fighting)= (this also clears *everyone* fighting that mob) and =stop_fighting(victim)=; =stop_fighting(ch)= if fighting; =start_fighting(ch,tmp_ch)= and =start_fighting(tmp_ch,ch)=. Both rescuer and victim get a 1 s wait.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =victim->attackers=, room =people= list, =tmp_ch->fighting=, =victim->fighting=, =ch->fighting=, class flags, =SKILL_RESCUE=.
- Writes :: =victim->fighting=, =mob->fighting=, =ch->fighting=, all =attackers= counters and positions touched by =stop_fighting=/=start_fighting=, fight events, =ch->wait= and =victim->wait= (1 s), skill via =FailSkill=.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only.
- Log lines: =make_criminal= LOG_CHEAT when applicable.

** assist (=do_assist=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:1071
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: assist [group member]
*** Parameters
- =group member= :: =only_argument=; when empty, =find_group_member_fighting(ch)= picks the leader if the leader is fighting in the room, else the last follower of the leader who is fighting in the room.
*** What it does
Peaceful-room check. Refused for self, for your current opponent, when you are already fighting, when =victim->attackers >= 10=, or when the assisted character is an un-charmed mob ("not your fight"). The assisted character must be fighting someone. =CORRUPT(ch, their opponent)= and criminal check ("assist"), then =start_fighting(ch, opponent)= (one-way: the mob is not set to fight ch; =start_fighting= also refuses if the mob already has ≥ 6 attackers). No skill roll, no damage this round. =WAIT_STATE(victim, WAIT_SEC*2)= — the 2 s wait lands on the *assisted* character, not on ch (looks like a bug; comment says "same as hit").
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->master=, =leader->followers=, =follower->fighting=, =victim->fighting=, =victim->attackers=, =victim->act= (AFF_CHARM).
- Writes :: =ch->fighting=, =opponent->attackers=, =ch->position= (auto-stand in =set_fighting=), strike event scheduled, =victim->wait= (2 s).
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only.
- Log lines: =make_criminal= LOG_CHEAT when applicable.

** kick (=do_kick=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:1135
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: kick [victim]
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; defaults to =ch->fighting=; mobs always kick their opponent.
- =cmd=    :: =CMD_KICK= (62); unused.
*** What it does
Peaceful-room check, =unsneak=, BERZERK and self refusals, =ch->attackers > 10= / =victim->attackers >= 12= refusals, =CORRUPT= + criminal ("kick"). No "skill known" gate. Roll: =percent = ((10 - GET_BODY_AC(victim)/10) << 1) + number(1,101)= versus =get_skill(SKILL_KICK) + 2×REMORT_IMPROVE_KICK= (capped to 100). Failure: =damage(ch,victim,0,SKILL_KICK)= if the victim is alive (starts the fight), =FailSkill(SKILL_KICK)=. Success (victim alive): NINJA → =damage(MIN(level/2,70) * (2 + remort_count/5), SKILL_KICK)= and 1–3 combo points; then, independently, WARRIOR → =MIN(level/2,70) * (1 + remort_count/5)= and 1–2 combo points, *else* (every non-warrior, including a ninja who already kicked) → =MIN(level/2,50) * (1 + remort_count/10)= and 1 combo point. So a ninja lands two damage calls. Always =WAIT_STATE(ch, WAIT_SEC*3)=.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =GET_BODY_AC(victim)=, =ch->attackers=, =victim->attackers=, skills, class, =ch->remort_count=, =victim->position=.
- Writes :: victim hp, fighting pointers/=attackers= via =damage=, combo-point pulse affects on victim, skill via =FailSkill=, =ch->wait= (3 s), affects removed by =unsneak=.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =make_criminal= LOG_CHEAT when applicable.

** vital (=do_vital_stab=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:1219
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: vital [victim]
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; defaults to =ch->fighting=; mobs always use their opponent.
- =cmd=    :: =CMD_VITAL_STAB= (80); unused.
*** What it does
Peaceful check, =unsneak=, self refusal, requires SKILL_VITAL_STAB > 0, refused if =ch->attackers > 6= or =victim->attackers >= 6=. =CORRUPT= + criminal ("vital"). Roll: =percent = number(1,101) + (50 - DEX - LCK)= versus =skill + 2×REMORT_IMPROVE_VITAL_STAB= (capped to 100). Failure: =damage(0, SKILL_VITAL_STAB)=, =FailSkill=. Success on a live victim, by class: NINJA — "Ninja Strike" messages; if the victim is above STUNNED, not already SLEEPING and is an NPC, =victim->position = POSITION_SLEEPING= (knocked out; the wait-state line is commented out); =damage(MIN(level/2, 100+DAMROLL) * number(1,4), SKILL_VITAL_STAB)=, 1–3 combo points. ASSASSIN — poison save via =CheckResistanceDamageTypeWithMinScale(POISON_DAMAGE)=: on success =affect_join= SPELL_POISON (duration 2, APPLY_STR -2, AFF_POISON), messages, 2 combo points, =damage(MIN(level/2, 70+DAMROLL) * number(1,6))=; on resist 1–2 combo points and =MIN(level/2, 70+DAMROLL) * number(1,2)=. Everyone else — =damage(MIN(level/2, 50))=, 1–2 combo points. Always =WAIT_STATE(ch, WAIT_SEC*5)=.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->attackers=, =victim->attackers=, DEX/LCK, skills, class, =GET_DAMROLL=, =victim->position=, victim resistances.
- Writes :: =victim->position= (SLEEPING, ninja vs NPC), =victim->affected= (SPELL_POISON / AFF_POISON, STR -2), victim hp, fighting pointers/=attackers=, combo points, skill via =FailSkill=, =ch->wait= (5 s).

** execute (=do_execute=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:1322
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: execute [victim]
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; defaults to =ch->fighting=; mobs always use their opponent.
- =cmd=    :: =CMD_EXECUTE=; unused.
*** What it does
Peaceful check, =unsneak=, self refusal, must be standing. PC victims are refused unless ch ≥ I9 or ch's namelist is "huttan". If fighting, only the current opponent may be executed. Requires SKILL_EXECUTE > 0, =victim->attackers < 6=, victim not dead. No =CORRUPT=/criminal call of its own (=damage()= does both). Gate: the victim must have =hit/max_hit < 0.25 * levelRatio= (levelRatio = (500+level ch)/(500+level victim)), or max hp < 1000, or ch immortal; otherwise "laughs at your puny attempt", =damage(0, SKILL_EXECUTE)=, wait 2 + number(1,3) s. Roll: =needed = (skill/7) * max(1, remort_count) + 3×REMORT_IMPROVE_EXECUTE= (capped 100), =roll = max(5, number(1,101))=; hit when =needed * levelRatio >= roll=. Hit: purity multiplier =bonus_multiplier_for_class(FLAG_FIGHTER,"Wa",STR)= × =(1 + combo_points/5)=, =consume_combo_points=, =damage(1800 * multiplier, SKILL_EXECUTE)=; wait 3 + number(1,4) s if the victim survived, 2 s if it died. Miss: dodge messages, =damage(0, SKILL_EXECUTE)=, wait 2 + number(1,3) s. PLR_DEBUG prints roll/threshold.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->position=, =ch->fighting=, =ch->namelist=, =victim->attackers=, =GET_HIT/GET_MAX_HIT(victim)=, levels, =ch->remort_count=, skill, combo points on victim.
- Writes :: victim hp, fighting pointers/=attackers= via =damage=, removes the AFFECT_COMBO_POINT pulse affect from the victim (=consume_combo_points=), =ch->wait=.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =make_criminal= (inside =damage=) LOG_CHEAT when applicable.

** berzerk (=do_berzerk=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:1435
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: berzerk
*** Parameters
- none intended. NOTE: the function is declared =do_berzerk(char_data *ch, char_data *v)= but is dispatched through the untyped =command_pointer()= as =(ch, argument, num)=, so =v= actually receives the argument *string* pointer and is then used in =act(..., v, TO_VICT)=. Not to be confused with =berserk= (cmds1.c =do_berserk=, SKILL_BERSERK).
*** What it does
Peaceful check. Refused if already under the BERZERK affect ("You aren't Rambo!") or SKILL_BERZERK is 0. Requires =GET_HIT(ch) > GET_MAX_HIT(ch)/25=, else "You must have more HP". No skill roll. Applies three affects of type BERZERK, duration 1 tick each: =APPLY_MELEE_HASTE +15=; =APPLY_ALL_STOPPING= with modifier =(body stopping + worn stopping)/-10= (a penalty); =APPLY_RESIST_ALL= with modifier =WIS+INT=. Prints frenzy messages (the TO_VICT one goes to the bogus =v=), then =affect_join(ch,&af,TRUE,FALSE)= re-joins the resist affect onto the existing BERZERK affect. Costs =ch->hit -= max_hit/40= and =ch->move -= (max_hit/40)/3=. Wait =WAIT_SEC*3 + number(7,15)= pulses. While berzerk the character cannot flee, bash, pummel or kick.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =GET_HIT/GET_MAX_HIT=, =ch->stopping[LOCATION_BODY]=, worn stopping, WIS/INT, =ch->affected=, skill.
- Writes :: =ch->affected= (BERZERK ×3, joined), =ch->hit=, =ch->move=, =ch->wait=.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** preparation (=do_preparation=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:1624
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: preparation
*** Parameters
- none (argument ignored).
*** What it does
Self-buff/utility: refused while SKILL_PREPARATION is on cooldown (=check_spell_skill_cooldown=), if the skill is < 1, if =ch->power= is below =power_needed= (75, divided by remort multipliers for REMORT_IMPROVE_PREPARATION (40) and REMORT_ADVANCED_SKILL_PREPARATION (96)), or if not standing. Roll: =number(1,101) > skill + remortBonus + dex_app_skill[DEX].hide + stealth_penalty(ch)= → fail: cooldown =120 - remort_count= s, =power -= needed/2=, wait 2 s + number(2,3) pulses. (remortBonus starts as ADVANCED_SKILL_PREPARATION/3 but is *overwritten* with 1.5×REMORT_IMPROVE_VANISH when that remort effect is present — copy/paste bug.) Success: if fighting, =stop_fighting= on the opponent (if it was fighting ch) and on ch — combat is broken off; messages; =do_resetcds(ch, ch->name, 0)= (the wizard command: removes *every* pulse-affect cooldown and item-set ability on the character found by =get_char(ch->name)=, and re-arms equipment cooldowns); =power -= needed=; cooldown SKILL_PREPARATION for =cooldown_length= (900 s divided by the same multipliers); =apply_backstab_bonus_stack= if ADVANCED bonus > 0 or a 50% roll; wait 1–2 s.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->affected_pulse_cooldown=, skill, remort effects, =ch->power=, =ch->position=, =ch->fighting=, DEX, worn equipment (=stealth_penalty=).
- Writes :: =ch->power=, =ch->affected_pulse_cooldown= (all removed, then SKILL_PREPARATION added), =ch->item_set_ability= list cleared, equipment ability timers, =ch->fighting= / opponent's fighting + =attackers= via =stop_fighting=, =ch->affected_pulse= (BACKSTAB_BONUS_STACK), =ch->wait=.

The SKILL_VANISH pulse affect is removed by =unsneak()=, i.e. by almost any attack command, and grants +6 to the assassinate "need".

** ambush (=do_ambush=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:1960
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: ambush [victim]
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; defaults to =ch->fighting=; mobs always use their opponent.
*** What it does
Peaceful check, cooldown (SKILL_AMBUSH), skill < 1, =unsneak= (after the checks, so sneak is gone before the sneak damage bonus is tested), self refusal, only the current opponent while fighting, must be standing, =victim->attackers < 6=, =CORRUPT= + criminal ("ambush"). Skill roll: =needed = skill + REMORT_IMPROVE_AMBUSH= (cap 100), =roll = number(1,101)=; hit when =needed > roll + number(1, victim level/50)= and damage > 0. Damage: =dice(purity * ((STR+DEX+WIS)/1.9 + 2*remort + DEX + STR + base_attack_damage(WIELD, SKILL_AMBUSH)) / 1.45, 6)= with purity = =bonus_multiplier_for_class(FLAG_THIEF,"Th",DEX)=, passed through =CheckResistanceAttackType= for the wielded weapon's damage type, ×1.1 if AFF_SNEAK (already stripped), × remort multiplier (IMPROVE_SNEAK, 50), ×5 for mobs above level 250. On hit against a live victim: IMPROVE_AMBUSH damage increase; crit tiers by =roll= — < needed/12 ×5.5 "SUPERSNEAKY" (bonus 3, combo 2–4), < needed/6 ×2.5 "sneaky" (bonus 2, combo 1–3), < needed/3 ×1.5 "bushpeeking" (bonus 1, combo 1–2). Two SKILL_AMBUSH pulse buffs go on the *attacker*: one with initial =APPLY_MELEE_HASTE +25+…=, per-pulse =APPLY_MELEE_PENETRATION dam/(23-…)=, =reset_timer_on_hit=, and =cooldown = 15 - …= (this is what puts ambush on cooldown); a second with per-pulse =APPLY_MELEE_HASTE=. Room/victim messages (the attacker's own "You ambush" line is commented out), 1–2 combo points, =damage_no_resist_check(dam, attacktype)=; if the victim survives and bonus > 0, =ambush_bonus_effect= adds AMBUSH_AFTER_EFFECT (=APPLY_MELEE_HASTE -bonus*number(1,10)=, duration = bonus) to the victim. Attacker wait 3 s + number(2,5) pulses (mortals only); =WAIT_STATE(victim, WAIT_SEC*(4 + number(2, 8+extra)/3.5))=. Miss: =damage(0, TYPE_UNDEFINED)=, messages, =AddHated(victim,ch,HOUR)=, wait 3 s + number(1,2) pulses (mortals). PLR_DEBUG prints the roll.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: cooldowns, skill, =ch->equipment[WIELD]=, stats, remort, =victim->attackers=, =ch->fighting=, victim resistances/level.
- Writes :: =ch->affected_pulse= (2× SKILL_AMBUSH), =ch->affected_pulse_cooldown=, victim hp, fighting pointers/=attackers=, =victim->affected_pulse= (combo points, AMBUSH_AFTER_EFFECT), =victim->hates=, =ch->wait=, =victim->wait=, affects stripped by =unsneak=.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =make_criminal= LOG_CHEAT when applicable.

** cheapshot (=do_cheapshot=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:2132
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: cheapshot [victim]
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; defaults to =ch->fighting=; mobs always use their opponent.
*** What it does
Same gate sequence as ambush (peaceful, cooldown SKILL_CHEAPSHOT, skill < 1, =unsneak=, self, current-opponent-only, standing, =victim->attackers < 6=, =CORRUPT= + criminal "cheapshot"). =needed = skill + 2×REMORT_IMPROVE_CHEAPSHOT + ADVANCED_SKILL_CHEAPSHOT/2= (cap 100) vs =roll + number(1, victim level/50)=. Damage =dice(purity(THIEF,DEX) * ((STR+DEX+INT)/3 + 2*remort + DEX + WIS + base_attack_damage) / 2.5, 3)=, resistance-checked, ×8 for mobs > 250, × remort multipliers (IMPROVE 25, ADVANCED 80); =stun_length= = 4 × remort multipliers (IMPROVE 20, ADVANCED 48). Hit on a live victim: messages (debug shows =WeaponCheck= damage), =damage_no_resist_check(dam, attacktype)=, 3–5 combo points, attacker wait 2 s + number(1,2) pulses (mortals), =add_stun(ch, stun_length, victim)= — an AFFECT_CONFUSION pulse affect with =no_msg= (stopping penalties like daze; note =add_stun= checks confusion on *ch*, not the victim). The pulse-affect block for a cheapshot debuff is commented out. Miss: =damage(0, SKILL_CHEAPSHOT)=, messages, =AddHated(HOUR)=, wait 3 s + number(1,2) pulses. No cooldown is ever added for SKILL_CHEAPSHOT (the cooldown check is dead).
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: as ambush.
- Writes :: victim hp, fighting pointers/=attackers=, =victim->affected_pulse= (combo points, AFFECT_CONFUSION), =victim->hates=, =ch->wait=, sneak affects.

** garrote (=do_garrote=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:2264
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: garrote [victim]
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; defaults to =ch->fighting=; mobs always use their opponent.
*** What it does
Same gate sequence as ambush (cooldown SKILL_GARROTE, criminal mode "garrote"). =silence_length= = 25 for a mob victim, 10 for a PC, × remort multipliers (IMPROVE_GARROTE 25, ADVANCED 96). =needed = skill + 2×IMPROVE + ADVANCED/2= (cap 100) vs =roll + number(1, victim level/50)=. Damage =dice(purity(THIEF,DEX) * ((STR+DEX+INT)/3 + 2*remort + DEX + WIS + base_attack_damage) / 2.5, 3)=, resistance-checked, ×8 for mobs > 250, × remort multipliers (25 / 80). Hit on a live victim: a SKILL_GARROTE pulse affect on the *victim* — duration =MAX(10, 10 + remort/10 + IMPROVE)=, =cooldown = 38 - IMPROVE= (this cools down the attacker), =PULSE_TYPE_DAMAGE= of =dam/3= to =APPLY_HIT= per pulse (a bleed), initial =APPLY_ALL_STOPPING -number(5,10)=, =total_dam = WeaponCheck(...)=; messages; =damage_no_resist_check(dam, attacktype)=; attacker wait 3 s + number(1,4) pulses (mortals); =WAIT_STATE(victim, WAIT_SEC*3)=; =add_silence(victim, silence_length)= (AFFECT_SILENCE cooldown-type affect, "You have been silenced"); 2–4 combo points. Miss: =damage(0, SKILL_CRIPPLING_STRIKE)= (wrong skill constant — copy/paste), messages, =AddHated(HOUR)=, 5 s cooldown on SKILL_GARROTE, wait 3 s + number(1,2) pulses.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: as ambush plus =IS_MOB(victim)=.
- Writes :: =victim->affected_pulse= (SKILL_GARROTE DoT, combo points), =victim->affected_pulse_cooldown= (AFFECT_SILENCE), =ch->affected_pulse_cooldown= (SKILL_GARROTE), victim hp, fighting pointers/=attackers=, =victim->hates=, =ch->wait=, =victim->wait=.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =make_criminal= LOG_CHEAT when applicable.

** mute_strike (=do_mute_strike=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:2403
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: mute_strike [victim]
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; defaults to =ch->fighting=; mobs always use their opponent.
*** What it does
Peaceful, cooldown (SKILL_MUTE_STRIKE), skill < 1, =unsneak=, self, standing, =victim->attackers < 6=, =CORRUPT= + criminal ("mute strike"). Unlike the thief strikes there is *no* current-opponent restriction. =silence_length= = 17 (mob victim) / 8 (PC), × remort multipliers (IMPROVE_MUTE_STRIKE 25, ADVANCED 96). =needed = skill + 2×IMPROVE + ADVANCED/2= (cap 100) vs =roll + number(1, victim level/50)=. Damage =dice(purity(FLAG_FIGHTER,"Wa",STR) * ((STR+DEX+INT)/3 + 2*remort + DEX + WIS + base_attack_damage) / 1.5, 2)=; *not* pre-checked against resistances (the final =damage()= call does that); ×8 for mobs > 250; × remort multipliers (25 / 80). Hit on a live victim: tiers — roll < needed/12 ×2.5 "LIP CRACKING" (silence ×2.1, combo 1–3), < needed/6 ×1.9 "muffling" (×1.7, combo 1–2), < needed/3 ×1.4 "suppressing" (×1.2, combo 1). Mob attackers are damage-capped by level (75 … 2200) and then to 750 overall. Messages, +1 combo point, =damage(ch, victim, dam, attacktype)=, =add_silence(victim, silence_length)=, attacker =WAIT_STATE(ch, WAIT_SEC*(4 + number(0, 6-extra)/4))= (applies to immortals too), victim 2 s wait, cooldown =50 - IMPROVE= s. Miss: =damage(0, SKILL_MUTE_STRIKE)=, messages, =AddHated(HOUR)=, 5 s cooldown, wait 3 s + number(1,2) pulses (mortals).
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: as garrote.
- Writes :: victim hp, fighting pointers/=attackers=, =victim->affected_pulse= (combo points), =victim->affected_pulse_cooldown= (AFFECT_SILENCE), =ch->affected_pulse_cooldown= (SKILL_MUTE_STRIKE), =victim->hates=, =ch->wait=, =victim->wait=.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =make_criminal= LOG_CHEAT when applicable.

** whirlwind (=do_whirlwind=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:2556
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: whirlwind [first victim]
*** Parameters
- =first victim= :: =only_argument=, optional; overridden by =ch->fighting= whenever ch is fighting. Used only to pick who the first swing lands on.
*** What it does
Area attack. Peaceful, cooldown (SKILL_WHIRLWIND), skill < 1, =unsneak=, self refusal, must be standing. No attacker-count limit and no up-front =CORRUPT= (each target is =CORRUPT=-checked in the loop; no =find_criminal= call — =damage= handles crime). =minimum_swings = number(2,6) + IMPROVE_WHIRLWIND/5 + ADVANCED_SKILL_WHIRLWIND/12=. Base damage =dice(purity(FLAG_BASIC,"Bb",STR) * ((STR+DEX+CON)/2 + 2*remort + DEX + STR + base_attack_damage(ch, NULL, WIELD, SKILL_WHIRLWIND)) / 2.2, number(7,13))=, × remort multipliers (40 / 72). Loops =minimum_swings= times over everyone in the room, skipping self, group members (=in_group_and_room=), own charmies (=master == ch=) and anyone =CORRUPT= forbids; stops once =enemies_hit > minimum_swings*4=. Per swing: =needed = skill + IMPROVE/2= (cap 100), fresh roll; per-target damage is resistance-adjusted (computed *before* the first-swing retarget to =victim=), ×3 for mobs > 250. Hit (=needed > roll + number(1, enemy level/50)=, dam > 0, enemy alive): tiers < needed/12 ×3.5 "DEVASTATING" (combo 2–3+), < needed/6 ×2 "massive" (combo 1–2+), < needed/3 ×1.5 "staggering" (combo 1+); messages only for tiered hits; =damage_no_resist_check(dam2, SKILL_WHIRLWIND)=; base damage decays ×0.9 per hit; on a surviving enemy: 1–2+ combo points, a chance to swing at the same enemy again (=roll < needed/(number(6,12)-…)=), and with ADVANCED bonus ≥ 16 a chance (=roll > 65-…=) to knock the enemy to POSITION_SITTING and either =add_stun= it (=roll > 110-…= and not already confused, messages) or give it a =WAIT_SEC*(2 + number(0,5)/3)= wait. Miss: =damage(0, SKILL_WHIRLWIND)=, messages, =AddHated(HOUR)=, base damage ×0.95. After the loop: if anything was hit, cooldown =12 - (remort/10 + IMPROVE/3 + ADVANCED/6)= s and (mortals) wait 3 s + number(1,2) pulses; otherwise "fails to hit a single target" and no cooldown.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: room =people= list, group/master relations, stats, skill, remort, each enemy's level/resistances/position/pulse affects.
- Writes :: hp of every enemy hit, fighting pointers/=attackers= for each (via =damage=), =enemy->position= (SITTING), =enemy->wait=, =enemy->affected_pulse= (combo points, AFFECT_CONFUSION), =enemy->hates=, =ch->affected_pulse_cooldown=, =ch->wait=, sneak affects.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =make_criminal= (inside =damage=) LOG_CHEAT when applicable.

** peek (=do_peek=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:2722
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: peek <victim>
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=, =get_char_room_vis=, required.
*** What it does
Non-combat inventory snoop. Refused on cooldown (SKILL_PEEK), skill < 1, while fighting, without a target, on self, or when not standing. =unsneak=. Gate: =roll = MAX(30, victim level/10)= (capped to 99); if =roll > get_skill(SKILL_PEEK)= → fail messages (victim is told), wait 2 s + number(1,2) pulses, cooldown =10 - remort_count/6=. Success: =needed = 89 - REMORT_IMPROVE_PEEK - REMORT_ADVANCED_SKILL_PEEK - skill/10=; messages (the victim is told someone peeked); each object in =victim->carrying= is shown (=obj_color_short_description=) when =number(1,101) > needed=; empty inventory reported. Wait 3 s + number(1,2) pulses, cooldown =20 - remort_count/3=. No =CORRUPT=, no crime, no fight started.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: cooldowns, skill, =ch->fighting=, position, =victim->level=, =victim->carrying=, remort.
- Writes :: =ch->wait=, =ch->affected_pulse_cooldown= (SKILL_PEEK), sneak affects via =unsneak=.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only (wait/cooldown only).

** crippling_strike (=do_crippling_strike=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:2799
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: crippling_strike [victim]
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; defaults to =ch->fighting=; mobs always use their opponent.
*** What it does
Same gate sequence as ambush (cooldown SKILL_CRIPPLING_STRIKE, current-opponent-only, criminal mode "crippling strike"). =needed = skill + 2×REMORT_IMPROVE_CRIPPLING_STRIKE + (if REMORT_ADVANCED_SKILL_CRIP_STRIKE) IMPROVE_CRIPPLING_STRIKE/2= (cap 100) vs =roll + number(1, victim level/50)=. Damage =dice(purity(THIEF,DEX) * ((STR+DEX+INT)/2.5 + 2*remort + DEX + WIS + base_attack_damage) / 2.3, 3)=, resistance-checked, ×8 for mobs > 250, × remort multipliers (IMPROVE 25; the ADVANCED branch also multiplies by the IMPROVE bonus, 80). Hit on a live victim: IMPROVE damage increase; tiers < needed/12 ×4.5 "DEVASTATING" (bonus 3), < needed/6 ×2.8 "massive" (bonus 2), < needed/3 ×1.7 "staggering" (bonus 1). A SKILL_CRIPPLING_STRIKE DoT pulse affect goes on the victim: duration =MAX(12, 11 + remort/15 + IMPROVE/5)= × remort(IMPROVE_SPELL_DURATION,100), proc every =5 - …= pulses, =PULSE_TYPE_DAMAGE= of =dam/(23-…)= to =APPLY_HIT=, =can_stack= 2+ (paladins scale by remort/15, others by remort/364), =stack_multiplier=, =reset_timer_on_hit=, =total_dam = WeaponCheck(...)=. Messages, 1–2 combo points, =damage_no_resist_check(dam, attacktype)=; if the victim survives: =strike_bonus_effect= for bonus > 0 (STRIKE_AFTER_EFFECT: =APPLY_RESIST_FORCE -bonus*number(1,10)=, duration = bonus, clamped by =affect_join_with_min_max=), for bonus > 1 two extra combo grants (+1 and +1–2), and (mortals) wait 3 s + number(1,4) pulses — no wait at all if the blow killed the victim. Miss: =damage(0, SKILL_CRIPPLING_STRIKE)=, messages, =AddHated(HOUR)=, wait 3 s + number(1,2) pulses. No cooldown is ever added for this skill (the pulse affect has no =cooldown=).
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: as ambush plus class (PALADIN).
- Writes :: =victim->affected_pulse= (SKILL_CRIPPLING_STRIKE DoT, combo points), =victim->affected= (STRIKE_AFTER_EFFECT), victim hp, fighting pointers/=attackers=, =victim->hates=, =ch->wait=, sneak affects.

** strike (=do_strike=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:2963

:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: strike [victim]
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; defaults to =ch->fighting=; mobs always use their opponent.
- =cmd=    :: =CMD_STRIKE= (81); unused.
*** What it does
Warrior/paladin "heroic strike". Same gate sequence as ambush (cooldown SKILL_STRIKE, current-opponent-only, criminal mode "strike"). Purity = =bonus_multiplier_for_class(FLAG_FIGHTER, PALADIN ? "Pa" : "Wa", STR)=. =needed = skill + 2×REMORT_IMPROVE_STRIKE= (cap 100), minus =10 - IMPROVE/2= for paladins; vs =roll + number(1, victim level/50)=. Damage =dice(purity * ((STR+DEX+WIS)/4 + 2*remort + DEX + STR + base_attack_damage) / 2.3, 3)=, resistance-checked, ×8 for mobs > 250. Hit on a live victim: IMPROVE damage increase; tiers < needed/12 ×5.5 "DEVASTATING" (bonus 3, combo 2–3), < needed/6 ×2.5 "massive" (bonus 2, combo 1–2), < needed/3 ×1.5 "staggering" (bonus 1, combo 1). Second roll: if =8 + remort/15 + IMPROVE/5 > number(1,100)= the attacker gains a hit shield: =add_hit_shield_to_char(ch, MAX(number(15,65), number(dam, dam*(1+…))))= (non-paladins divide it by =1.5 - IMPROVE/100=; capped at =GET_MAX_SHIELD=). Non-paladin damage is divided by =1.5 - IMPROVE/100=. A SKILL_STRIKE DoT pulse affect on the victim identical in shape to crippling strike (duration MAX(12, …), proc every 5-…, =dam/(23-…)= to =APPLY_HIT=, stacking, =reset_timer_on_hit=). Messages, 1–2 combo points, =damage_no_resist_check(dam, attacktype)=; if the victim survives: =strike_bonus_effect= for bonus > 0 and (mortals) wait 3 s + number(1,4) pulses. Miss: =damage(0, SKILL_STRIKE)=, messages, =AddHated(HOUR)=, wait 3 s + number(1,2) pulses. No cooldown is ever added for SKILL_STRIKE.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: as crippling strike plus =GET_SHIELD/GET_MAX_SHIELD(ch)=.
- Writes :: =ch->hit_shield=, =victim->affected_pulse= (SKILL_STRIKE DoT, combo points), =victim->affected= (STRIKE_AFTER_EFFECT), victim hp, fighting pointers/=attackers=, =victim->hates=, =ch->wait=, sneak affects.

** holy_strike (=do_holy_strike=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:3133
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: holy_strike [victim]
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; defaults to =ch->fighting=; mobs always use their opponent.
*** What it does
Mana-costed strike. =mana_needed = 25 - (remort/3 + REMORT_IMPROVE_HOLY_STRIKE/2)=, doubled for TEMPLAR. Peaceful, cooldown (SKILL_HOLY_STRIKE), =ch->mana < mana_needed= → "Not enough mana", skill < 1, then the usual gates (=unsneak=, self, current-opponent-only, standing, =victim->attackers < 6=, =CORRUPT= + criminal "holy strike"). Purity: PALADIN =(FLAG_FIGHTER,"Pa",STR)=, TEMPLAR =(FLAG_CLERIC,"Wa",STR)=, else =(FLAG_FIGHTER,"Wa",STR)=. =needed = skill + 2×IMPROVE= (cap 100), paladins −(10 − IMPROVE/2); vs =roll + number(1, victim level/50)=. Damage =dice(purity * ((STR+DEX+WIS)/3 + 3*remort + DEX + STR + base_attack_damage) / 2, 4)=, resistance-checked, ×6 for mobs > 250. Hit on a live victim: IMPROVE damage increase; tiers < needed/12 ×7 "DEVASTATING" (combo 2–4), < needed/6 ×4 "massive" (combo 1–3), < needed/3 ×2 "staggering" (combo 1–2). Shield roll: =12 + remort/10 + IMPROVE/5 > number(1,100)= → =add_hit_shield_to_char(ch, MAX(number(25,100), number(dam, dam*(1+…))))= (templars divide by =1.4 - IMPROVE/50=). Templar damage divided by =1.4 - IMPROVE/50=. A SKILL_HOLY_STRIKE DoT pulse affect on the victim: duration =MAX(20, 19 + remort/10 + IMPROVE/5)= × duration multiplier, proc every =3 - …= pulses, =dam/(23-…)= to =APPLY_HIT=, stacking (=can_stack= 2+…, =stack_multiplier=), and a finishing spell: =SPELL_HOLY_WORD= when 5 stacks are reached (=finish_spell_lvl_limit= 250, =finish_spell_remort_limit= 5). Messages, =ch->mana -= mana_needed= (mortals), 1–2 combo points, =damage_no_resist_check(dam, attacktype)=, wait (mortals) =WAIT_SEC*(5 - (remort/30 + IMPROVE/10)) + number(3 - remort/30, 6 - …)= pulses. Miss: =ch->mana -= mana_needed/2= (mortals), =damage(0, SKILL_HOLY_STRIKE)=, messages, =AddHated(HOUR)=, wait =WAIT_SEC*(4 - …) + number(3, 6 - …)= pulses. No cooldown is ever added for SKILL_HOLY_STRIKE.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->mana=, class, as strike.
- Writes :: =ch->mana=, =ch->hit_shield=, =victim->affected_pulse= (SKILL_HOLY_STRIKE DoT with finishing spell, combo points), victim hp, fighting pointers/=attackers=, =victim->hates=, =ch->wait=, sneak affects.

** wimp (=do_wimp=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:3314
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: wimp
: wimp <hp>
*** Parameters
- =hp= :: leading whitespace skipped; if the first character is a digit, =atoi= of the rest; must be ≤ =GET_MAX_HIT(ch)/3=. Any other non-empty argument prints usage.
*** What it does
Sets the auto-flee threshold. With a number: refuses values above one third of max hp, else =ch->whimpy_level = n= and confirms. With no argument it toggles: if =whimpy_level != WIMP_OFF= (-10) it is set to =WIMP_OFF= ("no longer a wimp"); otherwise it is set to =GET_MAX_HIT(ch)/5=. No wait, no skill.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->whimpy_level=, =GET_MAX_HIT(ch)=.
- Writes :: =ch->whimpy_level=.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save (=whimpy_level= is field 58 in player.save.c → =stash/<c>/<name>=).

** breath (=do_breath=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:3355
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: breath [victim]
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; defaults to =ch->fighting=.
*** What it does
Breath weapon for dragons and for anyone carrying a temporary breath-weapon spell affect. Peaceful check. First looks for an affect of type 177..181 (=SPELL_FIRE_BREATH= … =SPELL_LIGHTNING_BREATH=) on ch: if found, =manacost = 0=, the weapon is =bweapons[count - FIRST_BREATH_WEAPON]= and the affect is consumed (=affect_from_char=). NOTE =bweapons[]= starts with =cast_geyser=, so index 0 (fire-breath affect) casts geyser, gas casts fire, …, lightning casts acid — off by one. Without an affect, ch's =vnum= is looked up in =breath_monsters[]= (spec_mob.c); not listed → "You don't have a breath weapon, potatohead."; listed with no breaths → LOG_URGENT + message; else a random one of its breaths is picked, and =manacost = scan->cost= (the =GET_MANA(ch) <= -3*manacost= check that zeroes the weapon runs *before* manacost is assigned, so it just tests mana ≤ 0). Target resolution, =CORRUPT=, criminal ("breath"), then =breath_weapon(ch, victim, manacost, weapon)= (spec_mob.c): "rears back and inhales", =choose_victims= picks area targets, each non-immortal target in the room dodges on =number(1,6) > 4= or is hit by =weapon(level, ch, "", SPELL_TYPE_SPELL, target, 0, level)=; =ch->mana -= manacost=; a NULL weapon just "coughs and sputters". Always =WAIT_STATE(ch, WAIT_SEC*2)=.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->affected= (breath spells), =ch->vnum=, =breath_monsters[]=, =GET_MANA(ch)=, =ch->fighting=, room people.
- Writes :: =ch->affected= (breath affect removed), =ch->mana=, hp/affects/fighting of every target hit (via the cast_* breath functions → =damage=), =ch->wait= (2 s).
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_URGENT, "monster %s has no breath weapons")=; =make_criminal= LOG_CHEAT when applicable.

** shadowform (=do_shadowform=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:3422
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: shadowform
*** Parameters
- none (argument ignored).
*** What it does
Self-buff. Refused on cooldown (SKILL_SHADOWFORM), skill < 1, =ch->power < 30= (divided by remort multipliers — the ADVANCED branch reuses the IMPROVE_SHADOWFORM bonus, 96), not standing, while fighting, or when already in shadowform. =length = MAX(4, level/83)= plus remort additions; cooldown 180 s reduced by remort. Roll =number(1,101) > skill + remortBonus + dex_app_skill[DEX].hide + stealth_penalty= → fail: cooldown =45 - remort_count/2=, half power, wait 2 s + number(2,3) pulses. Success: mobs get at least 10 pulses; messages; pulse buff SKILL_SHADOWFORM on ch with initial =APPLY_RESIST_ALL +number(30,40) + remort_count/2=, duration =length=, cooldown =cooldown_length - (remort/3 + DEX/6)=, finish message "Your body fades back into reality."; =power -= needed=; =apply_backstab_bonus_stack= if the ADVANCED bonus is non-zero; wait 1–3 s. Side effects elsewhere: a shadowformed *victim* gives attackers +5 to backstab and +4 to assassinate "need"; =apply_shadowform_bonus= (called from backstab/assassinate) grants a 3-pulse version on a 3% roll (improved by remort).
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: cooldowns, skill, =ch->power=, position, =ch->fighting=, =ch->affected_pulse=, DEX, equipment, remort.
- Writes :: =ch->affected_pulse= (SKILL_SHADOWFORM, optional BACKSTAB_BONUS_STACK), =ch->affected_pulse_cooldown=, =ch->power=, =ch->wait=.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** assassinate (=do_assassinate=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: offensive.c:3591
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: assassinate <victim>
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=, =get_char_room_vis=, required.
*** What it does
Two-stage instant-kill attempt. Peaceful check, cooldown (SKILL_ASSASSINATE), target required, not self, must wield a weapon. =CORRUPT= + criminal (mode string "backstab"). Weapon =value[3]= must be 1, 10 or 11. Refused (below I5) if the victim is fighting or if ch is fighting; a victim with =IsImmune(IMM_BACKSTAB)= brushes it aside (=AddHated(HOUR)=) unless ch ≥ I11. Base: ASSASSIN or ≥ I5 → 0, NINJA → 1, others → 4. Stage 1 (backstab-style roll): =percent = number(1,101)=; sleeping victim → base += 14, percent −= 20; victim =Hates= ch and is idle → percent += 50−DEX−LCK; victim cannot see ch → percent −= 40. Mobs without SKILL_BACKSTAB get it set to 90. Pass when =percent < get_skill(SKILL_BACKSTAB) + stealth_penalty(ch)=. Stage 2 ("bingo"): =need= = +4 sneak, +4 hide, +6 SKILL_VANISH, +4 each for victim blind / confused / paralysed / shadowformed, +10 if the room is dark and only ch sees in the dark; +1 if SKILL_ASSASSINATE beats a d101 and, for SKILL_ASSASSINATE2..15, +((i−ASSASSINATE2)/2+1) each that beats a d101; level-difference bonus by class (ASSASSIN/imm MIN(75, Δ/5), NINJA MIN(65, Δ/6), REAVER MIN(50, Δ/8), else MIN(20, Δ/50)); +(HITROLL+DEX)/10; +force resistance/20; +REMORT_IMPROVE_ASSASSINATE; +ADVANCED/2; minus an "apex nerf" from =gain_apex_points(ch,victim)= (mob rank: ×1 below 10, ×2 below 30, else ×3); floor 1. =roll = number(1, 330 − remort/3 + ch->assassinate/3)= for level > 300, =270 − remort/3 + assassinate/2= for > 200, else =220 − remort/3 + assassinate/2=. A flavour line rates the attempt ("You made an awesome attempt at a difficult murder"); PLR_DEBUG shows need/roll. Mobs use need 30 vs d101. =roll <= need= or ch is CLASS_IMMORTAL → Bingo: =damage(ch, victim, GET_MAX_HIT(victim)+1000, SKILL_ASSASSINATE)= and =apply_assa_bonus_stack(ch)= (stacking ASSASSINATE_BONUS_STACK buffs: APPLY_ASSASSINATE, all attributes, hitroll). Bingo failed: =AddHated(2*HOUR)=, temporary =hit_bonus += base+2=, =apply_shadowform_bonus=, =hit(ch, victim, SKILL_ASSASSINATE)= (a backstab-strength hit; a miss inside =hit()= adds an apex-based cooldown), hit_bonus restored, =apply_assa_bonus_stack=, 1–2 combo points. Stage 1 failed: =AddHated(HOUR)=, cooldown =MIN(900, apex*20) − IMPROVE − ADVANCED/2= s if positive, =apply_shadowform_bonus=, =damage(0, SKILL_BACKSTAB)=, =FailSkill(SKILL_BACKSTAB)=, 30% chance of the bonus stack + combo. Wait 2 s if the ADVANCED bonus > 10, else 3 s. =unsneak(ch)= runs last, so sneak/hide/vanish count toward =need=.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->equipment[WIELD]->value[3]=, =victim->fighting=, =ch->fighting=, immunities, class, skills SKILL_BACKSTAB/ASSASSINATE..15, affects and pulse affects on both, room light, =ch->hit_bonus=, =ch->resistances.force=, =ch->assassinate=, =ch->remort_count=, mob rank, =victim->hates=.
- Writes :: =ch->hit_bonus= (temporarily), mob =SKILL_BACKSTAB= via =set_skill=, =victim->hates=, victim hp (possibly instant death), fighting pointers/=attackers=, =ch->affected_pulse= (ASSASSINATE_BONUS_STACK, shadowform), =ch->affected_pulse_cooldown=, =victim->affected_pulse= (combo points), skill via =FailSkill=, =ch->wait= (2–3 s), sneak/prowl/vanish/shimmer removed.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =make_criminal= → =vlog(LOG_CHEAT, ... 'backstab')= when applicable.

** Helpers in offensive.c
- =find_group_member_fighting= (18) :: returns the leader if the leader is fighting in ch's room, else the last follower of the leader that is fighting in ch's room; used by =do_assist=.
- =CORRUPT= (36) :: the global "may ch attack v" gate: allows self/immortals/mob-vs-mob/arena; blocks attacking another player's pet, a charmie attacking what its master isn't fighting, attacks by someone more than =MIN_CHOSEN_LEVEL_DIFF= (500) levels above an idle victim, and WORLD_CLASSIC clan PK rules (=CLAN_PKCLAN=, =CLAN_PKOTHERS=). Returns 1 = allowed. Called by every attack here and again inside =damage=/=start_fighting=.
- =find_kill_index= / =num_kills= / =add_kill= (105/115/124) :: per-player kill tally ring (=ch->kills[MAX_KILL_KEEP=125]=), most-recent first; used by the death code elsewhere, not by these commands.
- =do_batter= (660) :: shield-bash skill (SKILL_BATTER) with the =do_= signature but no =cmd_info[]= entry; invoked only through the skill table (h/spell_func.h, fight.c:3205). Knocks the victim to SITTING for 2 s on success, attacker sits 4 s on failure.
- =phys_bash= (731) :: the whole bash mechanic (checks, dex-modified roll, sit/self-damage on failure, victim knockdown and waits on success); returns 1 on a successful knockdown. Also callable from mob AI.
- =phys_pummel= (849) :: pummel mechanic: like =phys_bash= plus a damage formula, crit chance and a "smashes but fails to knock down" branch.
- =sunder_armor= (1483) :: applies the SUNDER_ARMOR all-stopping debuff (−1..−3, clamped to −15), combo points and a zero-damage =damage= call; the =modifier= parameter is unused.
- =strike_bonus_effect= (1590) :: adds/joins STRIKE_AFTER_EFFECT on the victim: =APPLY_RESIST_FORCE −bonus×number(1,10)= for =bonus= ticks, clamped via =affect_join_with_min_max=.
- =ambush_bonus_effect= (1607) :: same shape as =strike_bonus_effect= but AMBUSH_AFTER_EFFECT on =APPLY_MELEE_HASTE=.
- =bweapons[]= (3350) :: function table {geyser, fire, gas, frost, acid, lightning} indexed by breath-affect spell number minus =FIRST_BREATH_WEAPON= (177) in =do_breath=.
- =apply_shadowform_bonus= (3510) :: 3% base chance (improved by shadowform remort bonuses) to grant a 3-pulse SKILL_SHADOWFORM buff (=APPLY_RESIST_ALL +30..40+remort/2=) after a backstab/assassinate; no-op if already shadowformed.
- =apply_assa_bonus_stack= (3543) :: stacking ASSASSINATE_BONUS_STACK pulse buffs on ch (APPLY_ASSASSINATE, APPLY_ALL_ATTRIBUTES +1..2, APPLY_HITROLL), duration =MAX(25, 1.5×remort)=, =reset_timer_on_hit=, stack cap =MAX(5, remort/3)=.
- =apply_backstab_bonus_stack= (3567) :: stacking BACKSTAB_BONUS_STACK pulse buffs on ch (APPLY_MELEE_HASTE, APPLY_ALL_STOPPING +1..2, APPLY_BACKSTAB), same duration/stack rules.
- External helpers relied on throughout: =check_peaceful= (fight.c:182; PEACEFUL room, PK-world 2 and levels > I8 exempt), =unsneak= (utils.c:802; strips SKILL_SNEAK, SKILL_PROWL, SKILL_VANISH, SPELL_SHIMMER), =find_criminal=/=make_criminal= (crime.c; LOG_CHEAT + police summons for unconsented PC-on-PC attacks), =hit= (fight.c:2895; thac0 round that =AddHated=s and =start_fighting=s both sides), =damage=/=damage_no_resist_check= (fight.c:1935/1465; both =CORRUPT=, =unsneak= both parties and =start_fighting= when in the same room), =start_fighting=/=stop_fighting= (fight.c:240/252; the latter also detaches everyone fighting the target and stands them up), =FailSkill= (skills1.c:1912; may reset an over-guild-max skill to 1), =add_combo_point=/=get_combo_points=/=consume_combo_points= (handler.c), =add_stun=/=add_confuse=/=add_silence=/=add_spell_or_ability_cooldown=/=check_spell_skill_cooldown= (handler.c), =pulse_affect_to_char= (handler.c:2528; also puts =af.cooldown= on the caster), =add_hit_shield_to_char= (handler.c:2072), =AddHated=/=AddFeared= (opinion.c), =SetHunting= (utils.c:307; NPC-only), =stealth_penalty= (obj1.c:1349; armour-weight penalty), =Move= (move.c:578), =breath_weapon= (spec_mob.c:1579), =do_resetcds= (wizard.c:643).

* other.c — quit, save, toggles, reports, misc (29 commands)

** massdie (=do_massdie=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:18
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I9 (2009)
:END:
*** Syntax
: massdie
*** Parameters
- none :: the handler takes only =ch=; any arguments are ignored.
*** What it does
Immortal prank. Walks =character_list= and, to every player other than the invoker, sends a canned "Kugamocharo ... acid ... you die! You lose a level." message followed by a fake copy of the Perilous Realms account menu and a "Choice> " prompt. Nothing actually happens to anyone: no death, no level loss, no state change. NPCs are skipped.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =character_list=, =IS_NPC(c)=
- Writes :: none
*** Persistence
- Read-only; no state change.

** modes (=do_modes=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:46
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:FLAGS: CMDF_NOORDER
:END:
*** Syntax
: modes
: modes anon[ymous]
: modes soun[ds]
*** Parameters
- =arg1= :: first word via =one_argument=; matched with =is_abbrev= against "anon" and "soun". Anything else is silently ignored. A second word is parsed into =arg2= but never used.
*** What it does
With no argument prints a boxed status panel of the player's toggles: Priority, Compact, Auction, NoTell, Brief, Gossip, Deaf, Aggressive (=ch->aggressive != -1=), Sports (=!PLR_NOSPORTS=), Show Exits (=!PLR_NOEXITS=), wimpy level (or OFF when =WIMP_OFF=), terminal type (=termlist[ch->desc->term].name=), Anonymous, clan name (=get_clan(ch)->name=), consent target (=ch->consent= or "No One"), page height/width and Hear Sounds. "modes anon" toggles =PLR_ANON= (hides class/level/race from others); "modes sounds" toggles =PLR_HEAR_SND= (MSP sound triggers, marked obsolete in const.h). Note the no-arg panel dereferences =ch->desc= unguarded, so a linkdead player (or a mob ordered around, hence CMDF_NOORDER) would crash here.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->act= bits, =ch->aggressive=, =ch->whimpy_level=, =ch->desc->term=, =ch->consent=, =ch->page_size=, =ch->page_width=, clan table via =get_clan=
- Writes :: =SET_BIT/CLEAR_BIT(ch->act, PLR_ANON)=, =SET_BIT/CLEAR_BIT(ch->act, PLR_HEAR_SND)=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save (=ch->act= is written by =save_char=).

** guard (=do_guard=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:115
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: guard
*** Parameters
- none :: argument ignored.
*** What it does
NPC-only toggle (players get "Sorry. you can't just put your brain on autopilot!"). Flips =ACT_GUARDIAN= on the mob's =act= bits: set means "MOB will guard master" (used by the charm/pet AI elsewhere), with room echoes "$n alertly watches you." / "$n relaxes.". Normally reached via =order <pet> guard=.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =IS_NPC(ch)=, =ch->act=
- Writes :: =SET_BIT/CLEAR_BIT(ch->act, ACT_GUARDIAN)=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only (mob flag, lost when the mob is extracted).

** sacrifice (=do_sacrifice=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:134
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: sacrifice <object>
*** Parameters
- =object= :: rest of line via =only_argument=; must be a visible item in inventory (=get_obj_in_list_vis(ch, arg, ch->carrying)=) and must have a prototype (=real_objp(obj->vnum)=), otherwise "worthless to your deity".
*** What it does
Requires the player to stand at the altar matching their chosen alignment (=IS_ALTAR=: =GOOD_ALTAR= / =EVIL_ALTAR= / =NEUTRAL_ALTAR= room flag by =base_alignment=). Special case, the PC-remort token (vnum 29106): the player must wear nothing and not be immortal; the token is destroyed, and if =ch->level >= 500= the player is teleported (roll 1-3 -> room 3000 "Countess' room", roll 4-6 -> 64491 "Alcanzar's room"; the roll is also echoed raw to the player), otherwise =die(ch)= is called. Any other item: if =get_rarity(obj) < RARITY_UNCOMMON= the gods are angered and the player takes =number(1,5*level)= divine-hand damage from themselves and keeps the item. Otherwise the item is consumed; rarity >= =RARITY_RARE= additionally grants a 24-tick =SPELL_BLESS= (+10 hitroll, -40 save vs spell) if not already blessed. An alignment delta is computed from cost, rarity and the item's applies and pushed toward the player's true alignment (clamped at +-1000, or toward 0 for true-neutrals); "you still need to do more" is shown if actual alignment still doesn't match the chosen camp.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->carrying=, =ch->equipment[]=, =ch->level=, =ch->base_alignment= (via =IS_TRUE_*=), =world= room flags, =obj->vnum=, =obj->cost=, =obj->apply[]=, =obj_apply_stats[]=, =obj->rarity=
- Writes :: =ch->alignment=; =ch->in_room= (remort teleport via =char_from_room/char_to_room=); =obj_from_char= + =extract_obj= on the sacrificed item; =affect_to_char= (bless); =ch->hit= via =damage()=; possibly death via =die()=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save. (=die()= -> =dieWithXPPenalty= may itself save/relocate the corpse; =get_rarity= may upgrade =obj->rarity= to the prototype's value as a side effect.)
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_DEBUG, "%s sacrificed %s for a change in alignment of %f")=

** donate (=do_donate=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:320
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:FLAGS: CMDF_NOARENA
:END:
*** Syntax
: donate <object>
*** Parameters
- =object= :: rest of line via =only_argument=; visible item in inventory.
*** What it does
Moves an inventory item to the donation room (vnum 99), but only from world zone 0 (=GET_WORLDZONE(ch)==0=); zones 1 and 4 get "unavailable in the Nexus", others "not available in this world". Refuses if room 99 doesn't exist (logs urgent) or if the player is in =LOAD_ROOM_BANISHED= (6999). Mortals cannot donate items flagged CURSED, NODROP, TEST or INDESTRUCT, vnum 25, or of type container, spell pouch, note, drink container, key, food, pen or trap; immortals get an informational "Item is CURSED" and proceed anyway. The item is announced in both rooms (everyone in the donation room sees "<name> donates $p.") and given a decay timer of =DROP_TICKS*4= (120 ticks) via =timer2=.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->worldzone=, =ch->in_room=, =ch->carrying=, =tmp_object->extra_flags=, item type, =get_rp(99)->people=
- Writes :: =obj_from_char= / =obj_to_room(obj, 99)=, =tmp_object->timer2 = DROP_TICKS*4=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only. The item leaves the player's inventory, so it disappears from the player file at the next save; room 99 contents are not saved unless the room is AUTOSAVE-flagged elsewhere.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_URGENT, "Donation Room doesn't exist.")= when room 99 is missing.

** junk (=do_junk=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:422
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: junk <object>
*** Parameters
- =object= :: rest of line via =only_argument=; visible item in inventory. =cmd= is unused (barbarian quaff/recite call it internally with -1).
*** What it does
Destroys one inventory item via =phys_junk=. Mortals cannot junk =ITEM_CURSED= items or anything with =real_cost= (bought for real money); immortals can. The item is extracted with a room echo. If the invoker is an auctioneer mob (=is_auctioneer=), =verify_auc_list= is run afterwards to drop the sale entry, which writes the auction file.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->carrying=, =obj->extra_flags=, =obj->real_cost=, =GET_LEVEL(ch)=
- Writes :: =obj_from_char= + =extract_obj(obj,1)=; auctioneer's =user_data= sale list
*** Persistence
- No direct save for players; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save. When =ch= is an auctioneer, =verify_auc_list= -> =save_auc_data(ch)= -> =<name>-<room>.auction=.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_DEBUG, "Someone's junking the auction queue. Naughty.")= (auctioneer path only).

** qui (=do_qui=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:441
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: qui
: qui |
*** Parameters
- =arg= :: raw; only the first character is inspected.
*** What it does
Guard entry so that abbreviating "quit" does nothing: prints "You have to write quit - no less, to quit!". Hidden trap: if the argument's first character is a literal =|= the caller's level is silently set to 1 (=ch->level = 1=). Presumably an anti-script/anti-cheat booby trap; it applies to any level including immortals.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =*arg=
- Writes :: =ch->level = 1= (only on =qui |=)
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** hi (=do_hi=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:448
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: hi
: hi |
*** Parameters
- =arg= :: raw; only the first character is inspected.
*** What it does
Guard entry so that "hi" is not treated as "hit": prints "You have to type hit - no less, to hit someone!". Same hidden trap as =qui=: a leading =|= sets =ch->level = 1=.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =*arg=
- Writes :: =ch->level = 1= (only on =hi |=)
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** title (=do_title=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:456
:MIN_POS: SLE (sleeping)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: title
: title <new title text>
*** Parameters
- =text= :: rest of line, leading whitespace skipped; must be shorter than 256 chars (else "Too long."). Colour codes are allowed; =#{off}= is appended automatically.
*** What it does
Sets or shows the player's title. Refused with "Stop being stupid or go away." if the player has =PLR_STUPID= or the account has =A_STUPID=. Silently ignored for NPCs and for characters with no descriptor. With text: frees the old =ch->title= and stores =strdup(text + "#{off}")=. Without text: shows the current title or "<none>".
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->act=, =ch->desc->acct->flags=, =ch->title=
- Writes :: =ch->title= (FREE + strdup)
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save (=save_char= field 7 writes the title).

** camp (=do_camp=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:499
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:FLAGS: CMDF_NOORDER
:END:
*** Syntax
: camp
*** Parameters
- none :: argument ignored.
*** What it does
"Rent out" without leaving equipment behind. PCs only. Refused while =ch->rent_disable= is in the future (set after attacking another PC: "You can't camp so soon after trying to hurt another pc!") or while fighting. If position is below stunned the player is killed instead (=die(ch)=). A linkdead player (no =ch->desc=) is silently removed with =remove_junk= + =exit_game(ch,0)= (saved to =NOWHERE=, i.e. next login at bindpoint/3000). Otherwise the room must be =RENTABLE= unless the player is an immortal (>= I1); then no-rent junk is disintegrated (=remove_junk=), "has left the game" is echoed, and =exit_game(ch,1)= saves the character with the current room as the load room and drops the connection back to the account menu.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->rent_disable=, =ch->fighting=, =GET_POS(ch)=, =ch->desc=, =world[ch->in_room].room_flags & RENTABLE=, =GET_LEVEL(ch)=
- Writes :: via =remove_junk=: extracts no-rent items from equipment, inventory, warehouse and account warehouse; via =exit_game=: =ch->time.logout=, character extracted and freed, =desc->character = 0=, descriptor moved to account menu (or =CON_WIZLOCK= if =lockout_till=).
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: =exit_game= -> =save_char(ch, ch->in_room)= (or =NOWHERE= if linkdead) -> =stash/<c>/<name>=; the room saved becomes =was_in_room= (field 49) used to place the player on next login.

** quit (=do_quit=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:538
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:FLAGS: CMDF_NOORDER
:END:
*** Syntax
: quit
: quit yes
: quit +<N>          (hidden; three hard-coded names only)
*** Parameters
- =yes= :: required (any prefix of "yes") for mortals above level 10 quitting in a non-RENTABLE room.
- =+N= :: if the argument is =+<positive int>= and the character is on account "Countess" or is named "huttan" or "acaldwel", the value is written to =((s32*)ch)[1]= and the command returns without quitting.
*** What it does
Leaves the game. PCs only. Refused while =rent_disable= is active, while fighting, or while charmed; a player below stunned is killed (=die=). Linkdead players are removed silently with =exit_game(ch,0)=. In a non-RENTABLE room: level <= 10 players are moved to =LOAD_ROOM_MORTAL= (3000, the bounce room) with a warning and must type quit again; mortals above 10 must confirm with "quit yes" and, on confirming, have all equipment and inventory dumped on the floor (=all_to_room=, items get =DROP_TICKS= decay unless the room is AUTOSAVE) and are saved with load room =NOWHERE=. In a RENTABLE room, or for immortals anywhere, =remove_junk= then =exit_game(ch,1)= saves with the current room as the load room. The hidden =+N= branch writes into the second 32-bit word of =char_data=, which on this 64-bit build is the padding after =in_room= (no effect); on a 32-bit build it would overwrite the =name= pointer. There is also a stray =printf("ROOM %d")= to stdout in the level<=10 branch.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->account_name=, =ch->name=, =ch->rent_disable=, =ch->fighting=, =GET_POS=, =AFF_CHARM=, =ch->desc=, room flags =RENTABLE=/=AUTOSAVE=, =GET_LEVEL=
- Writes :: =ch->in_room= (bounce to 3000); =all_to_room=: =unequip_char= + =obj_to_room= for all gear, =o->timer2 = DROP_TICKS=; =remove_junk= extractions; =exit_game= side effects (see camp); =((s32*)ch)[1]= on the hidden branch
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: =exit_game= -> =save_char(ch, ch->in_room)= when rented (RENTABLE room or immortal), else =save_char(ch, NOWHERE)= -> =stash/<c>/<name>=. The bounce-to-3000 branch does not save.

** save (=do_save=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:615
:MIN_POS: SLE (sleeping)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: save
*** Parameters
- none :: argument ignored. =cmd= matters: when called internally with =cmd == -1= (autosave from =comm.c=, =limits.c=, =spec_rooms.c=, =wizard.c=) the "Saving <name>." line is suppressed and "Autosaved." is shown only to players with =PLR_DEBUG=.
*** What it does
Writes the player file immediately. NPCs are ignored. Note it always passes =LOAD_ROOM_MORTAL= (3000) as the load room, so a player whose last save was an explicit/auto =save= (rather than camp/quit) will re-enter at room 3000 after a crash, not at their current room or bindpoint. Priority 25 in =cmd_info= makes it out-rank most other queued commands.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->act= (PLR_DEBUG), whole character for serialisation
- Writes :: none in memory (file only)
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: =save_char(ch, LOAD_ROOM_MORTAL)= -> =stash/<c>/<name>= (field 49 = 3000).
- Log lines: =save_char= logs =LOG_URGENT= if the name is blank or the file can't be opened.

** buy, sell, value, list, browse, rent, offer, deposit, withdraw, awithdraw, adeposit, balance, pull, highfive, gain, instazone, choose, bet, batter, oldload, post, remort (=do_not_here=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:629
:MIN_POS: varies (STA: buy sell rent offer pull highfive; FIG: batter; RES: value list browse deposit withdraw awithdraw adeposit balance gain choose; STA also remort; DEA: instazone bet oldload post)
:MIN_LEVEL: varies (0 for most; 1 for rent offer highfive choose bet batter; I10 (2010) for instazone and oldload)
:FLAGS: CMDF_NOORDER on =post= only
:END:
*** Syntax
: <any of the above> [args]
*** Parameters
- =args= :: ignored. =cmd= carries a =CMD_*= number (CMD_BUY, CMD_SELL, CMD_VALUE, CMD_LIST, CMD_BROWSE_SHOP, CMD_RENT, CMD_OFFER, CMD_DEPOSIT, CMD_WITHDRAW, CMD_AWITHDRAW, CMD_ADEPOSIT, CMD_BALANCE, CMD_GAIN, CMD_POST, CMD_REMORT) so that special-procedure mobs/rooms (shopkeepers, bankers, receptionists, guildmasters, boards, avatars) can intercept the command before dispatch reaches this fallback.
*** What it does
Fallback for commands that only make sense in the presence of a special mob or room. If no spec-proc in the room consumed the command it prints "Sorry, but you cannot do that here!". The real behaviour of each alias lives in the spec-procs (shop.c, bank, reception.c, guild.c, board.c, etc.), not here.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: none
- Writes :: none
*** Persistence
- Read-only; no state change.

** sneak (=do_sneak=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:636
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: sneak
*** Parameters
- none :: argument ignored.
*** What it does
Toggle. If already =AFF_SNEAK= the affect is removed ("no longer sneaking"). Otherwise requires =get_skill(ch, SKILL_SNEAK) > 0=. Rolls =number(1,101)= against skill + remort bonus (2 x =REMORT_IMPROVE_SNEAK= stat bonus, capped so the total does not exceed 100) + =dex_app_skill[dex].sneak= + =stealth_penalty(ch)= (negative, from metal armour weight, floor -95). The player always sees "Ok, you'll try to move silently for a while."; success is silent unless =PLR_DEBUG=. On success an affect of type =SKILL_SNEAK=, duration = level, bitvector =AFF_SNEAK= is applied.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->affected_by=, skill table, =ch->remortInfo[]=, =GET_DEX(ch)=, =ch->equipment[]= (penalty)
- Writes :: =affect_from_char(ch, SKILL_SNEAK)= or =affect_to_char= with =AFF_SNEAK=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save (affects are saved).
