:END:
*** Syntax
: hide
*** Parameters
- none :: argument ignored.
*** What it does
Refused while fighting. Requires =get_skill(ch, SKILL_HIDE) > 0=. Same roll as sneak using =SKILL_HIDE=, =REMORT_IMPROVE_HIDE= and =dex_app_skill[dex].hide= plus =stealth_penalty=. On success sets the =AFF_HIDE= bit directly on =ch->affected_by= (no timed affect; it is cleared by movement/combat code elsewhere). There is no un-hide branch here.

** steal (=do_steal=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:708
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:FLAGS: CMDF_NOARENA
:END:
*** Syntax
: steal <object> <victim>
: steal coins|gold <victim>
*** Parameters
- =object= :: first word (=one_argument=); "coins" or "gold" steals money, otherwise matched against the victim's visible inventory, then (by =isname=) against worn equipment.
- =victim= :: rest of line (=only_argument=); visible character in the room, not self. =cmd= = CMD_STEAL, unused.
*** What it does
Builders (I2..I4) are refused and logged. Refused in PEACEFUL rooms (=check_peaceful=) and below level 6 against players. =CORRUPT(ch,victim)= must allow it (pet/master rules); =find_criminal= / =make_criminal= may flag the thief as a criminal (police mobs, =rent_disable=). Base chance: =number(1,101) - dex_app_skill[dex].p_pocket + victim level=; automatic success if the victim is below sleeping; automatic failure if the victim is above =MAX_MORT= (immortal). Equipment can only be taken from a victim at or below stunned (it is unequipped straight into the thief's inventory, no roll). Inventory items add weight/100 + volume/100 to the roll, compared against =get_skill(SKILL_STEAL) + stealth_penalty=; success moves the item if =CAN_CARRY_OBJ=. Coins: on success takes =number(1,10)%= of the victim's gold, capped at 1782. On failure against an awake victim the victim is told; an awake NPC victim then either shouts "<name> is a bloody thief." (=ACT_NICE_THIEF=) or attacks (=hit=).
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->level=, victim position/level/=desc=, =victim->carrying=, =victim->equipment[]=, =dex_app_skill=, skill table, =consent=
- Writes :: =obj_from_char/obj_to_char= or =unequip_char=; =ch->gold += n=, =victim->gold -= n=; combat start via =hit()=; criminal flags / =rent_disable= via =make_criminal=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_CHEAT, "STEAL: %s tried to steal from %s")= (builders); =vlog(LOG_THIEF, "STEAL: %s from %s[ FAIL]")= for PC-vs-PC without consent; =make_criminal= logs =LOG_CHEAT=.

** group (=do_group=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:1033
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: group
: group <name>
: group <self>
*** Parameters
- =name= :: rest of line via =only_argument=; a visible character in the same room. Naming yourself while grouped disbands the whole group.
*** What it does
No argument: if =AFF_GROUP= is set, lists the leader (=ch->master= or self) and every grouped follower with shield/hp/mana/power/move (colour-coded by percentage), level, class abbreviation and room name. With a name: only a leader (no =ch->master=) may enrol; the target must already be following the leader (or be the leader). If the target already has =AFF_GROUP= they are kicked out; kicking yourself clears =AFF_GROUP= on every follower ("has disbanded the group"). Immortals I1..I10 cannot be grouped by, or group with, anyone below I10 (logged as cheat). Level spread rule for PC-PC groups when the leader is below I10: with =low= the lower level, allowed spread is =MAX_LEVEL_DIFF(50) + low= if =low < 50=, else =low * MAX_LEVEL_SPREAD(2)=; the higher level must not exceed it. On success both get =AFF_GROUP=.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->master=, =ch->followers=, =affected_by=, hp/mana/power/move/shield, levels, =CLASS()=, room names
- Writes :: =set_bit/clear_bit(x->affected_by, AFF_GROUP)= on leader, target, and (on disband) all followers
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only (group membership is not part of the player file in any lasting sense; followers are reset at logout).
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_CHEAT, "%s tried to form a group with %s.")= on the immortal checks.

** makeleader (=do_make_leader=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:1169
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:FLAGS: CMDF_NOARENA
:END:
*** Syntax
: makeleader <name>
*** Parameters
- =name= :: rest of line via =only_argument=; looked up with =get_char_vis_world= (anywhere in the world, despite the "No one here" message), must be a PC follower of the invoker with =AFF_GROUP= set and =master == ch=.
*** What it does
Hands leadership of the current group to one of its PC members. The invoker must be the leader. If both are PCs and the new leader is not =CLASS_IMMORTAL=, the transfer is refused when =count_pc_followers(ch) >= max_followers(new_leader)= (3..8 by charisma). Then the new leader is detached from the invoker, every non-mob follower of the invoker is re-parented under the new leader (=raw_remove_follower= + =raw_add_follower=, =AFF_GROUP= re-set), mobs (pets) are left following the old leader, and finally the old leader follows the new one. Note =count_pc_followers= tests =IS_NPC(ch)= instead of =IS_NPC(k->follower)=, so it counts all followers including pets.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->master=, =ch->followers=, =new_leader->master=, =affected_by=, =GET_CHA(new_leader)=
- Writes :: follower lists (=follow_type= nodes freed/created), =x->master= pointers, =set_bit(AFF_GROUP)= on the invoker, new leader and moved followers
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only.

** quaff (=do_quaff=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:1237
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: quaff <potion>
*** Parameters
- =potion= :: rest of line via =only_argument=; searched in inventory first, then the held item (=equipment[HOLD]=) by =isname=. Must be =ITEM_POTION=. =cmd= = CMD_QUAFF, unused.
*** What it does
Drinks a potion, casting =value[1..3]= as spells at level =value[0]= on the drinker (=SPELL_TYPE_POTION=). Mortals need =magic_ok(room)= (no NO_MAGIC room/sector flag). Barbarians without =REMORT_BARB_NO_JUNK= smash the potion instead (=do_junk=) when =number(1,101) > max(50, (INT+WIS)*1.7)=, unless it has =real_cost=. While fighting there is a fumble check: a held potion shatters if =number(1,20) > base dexterity=, an inventory one if => base dex - 4=; a shattered potion is extracted with no effect. Otherwise the potion is consumed and a 2-second =WAIT_STATE= applied.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->carrying=, =ch->equipment[HOLD]=, =temp->type_flag=, =temp->value[]=, =temp->real_cost=, =ch->fighting=, =ch->base_stats.dexterity=, INT/WIS, remort info, room flags, =spell_info[]=
- Writes :: whatever the spells do to =ch=; =unequip_char= + =extract_obj(temp,1)=; =ch->wait += 2*PULSE_PER_SEC=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** recite (=do_recite=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:1336
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: recite <scroll> [target]
*** Parameters
- =scroll= :: first word; inventory first, then held item. Must be =ITEM_SCROLL=.
- =target= :: optional rest of line; =generic_find= over inventory, room objects, equipment and characters in room; defaults to self. Whole argument must be under 100 chars (else logged as a crash attempt and ignored). =cmd= = CMD_RECITE, unused.
*** What it does
Reads a scroll, casting =value[1..3]= at level =value[0]= on the target (=SPELL_TYPE_SCROLL=); violent spells are skipped in PEACEFUL rooms (=check_peaceful=). Refused while berzerking and, for mortals, in no-magic rooms. Barbarians without =REMORT_BARB_NO_JUNK= destroy the scroll (=do_junk=) if =level < 400/INT= or on a =number(1,100) < 21-INT= roll. Non-caster classes (not MAGE, ILLUSIONIST, CLERIC, WIZARD) must pass =get_skill(SKILL_READ_MAGIC)= vs =number(1,101)=; on failure the scroll is unreadable unless it is word of recall (or identify, for a barbarian: the condition is =v1 != RECALL && (v1 != IDENTIFY && IS_BARB)=, so non-barbarian non-casters can always read identify too). A player under =SPELL_FIRESHIELD= has a 1-in-5 chance of incinerating the scroll instead of casting. The scroll is always consumed at the end (unequipped first if held).
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->carrying=, =equipment[HOLD]=, =scroll->value[]=, class, skills, affects, room flags, =spell_info[].targets=
- Writes :: spell effects on =victim=/=obj=; =unequip_char= + =extract_obj(scroll,1)=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_CHEAT, "CRASH ATTEMPT: %s with buffer overflow in recite!")= for over-long input.

** use (=do_use=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: other.c:1833
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: use <worn item> [target]
: use <item set name> [target]
: use <held staff>
: use <held wand> <target>
*** Parameters
- =item= :: first word; matched against visible worn equipment (=get_object_in_equip_vis=), then against equipped item-set names (=get_item_set_by_name_in_equip_vis=); immortals may also name an inventory item. Empty argument -> "What do you want to use ?".
- =target= :: rest of line; for item/set abilities a visible character (=get_char_vis=, default self); for wands a =generic_find= over room/inventory/equipment. =cmd= = CMD_USE, unused.
*** What it does
Three layers. (1) Item-set abilities: if the name matches an equipped set, =ItemSetPulseAffect= applies the set's pulse buffs (for every bonus tier the player has enough pieces for) and =ItemSetSpell= casts its =APPLY_ITEM_SPELL= entries via =phys_cast=; each use records a cooldown entry (=item_set_ability_to_char=, expiry =now + item_set->use_cooldown=) and mortals are told the remaining wait if still cooling down. (2) Worn item abilities: =ItemPulseAffect= applies the object's =pulse_apply[]= buffs and =ItemSpell= casts its =APPLY_ITEM_SPELL= applies; the object's =last_use_time= is set to =now + use_cooldown= (default =ITEM_USE_FREQUENCY=, 300 s). If the player wears two of the same item and the first is cooling down, the second copy is tried. A "holds up $p ... begins to glow" echo is shown for pure buff uses. (3) Classic staff/wand: if nothing above matched and the named item is held, a staff (=ITEM_STAFF=) is tapped and casts =value[3]= at level =value[0]= on the room (=SPELL_TYPE_STAFF=) consuming one charge (=value[2]=); a wand (=ITEM_WAND=) needs a target and casts on it (=SPELL_TYPE_WAND=), refusing violent spells in PEACEFUL rooms; both give a 2-second wait, "seems powerless" at zero charges. Mortals need =magic_ok(room)= for everything. Immortals bypass all cooldowns.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->equipment[]=, =ch->carrying= (immortals), =obj->pulse_apply[]=, =obj->pulse_duration=, =obj->apply[]=, =obj->last_use_time=, =obj->use_cooldown=, item-set tables (=get_item_set=, =get_set_bonus_count=), =ch->item_set_ability= list, =spell_info[]=, room flags
- Writes :: =pulse_affect_to_char= entries on the target; =ch->item_set_ability= (new nodes via =item_set_ability_to_char=); =obj->last_use_time=; staff/wand =value[2]--=; spell effects via =phys_cast= / spell pointers; =ch->wait=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save (=save_char= writes pulse affects and =item_set_ability= cooldowns; =last_use_time= lives on the object and is saved with the player's equipment).

** deafen (=do_plr_noshout=)

:END:
*** Syntax

*** What it does

- Writes :: =SET_BIT/CLEAR_BIT(ch->act, PLR_DEAF)=; on the trap: =ch->level = 1=, =ch->exp = 0=

** Helpers in other.c
- =phys_junk= (397) :: destroys one carried object with cursed/real-money checks; auctioneer callers get =verify_auc_list= (writes the auction file). Used by junk, quaff, recite.
- =all_to_room= (482) :: strips all equipment and inventory onto the floor of =ch->in_room=, giving inventory a =DROP_TICKS= decay unless the room is AUTOSAVE. Used by quit in non-rentable rooms.
- =ItemSetPulseAffect= (1446) :: for each item-set bonus tier the wearer qualifies for, applies the tier's =pulse_apply[]= buffs to the target and records a cooldown; returns 0 none, 1 used, 2 on cooldown.
- =ItemPulseAffect= (1585) :: applies a single object's =pulse_apply[]= buffs to =ch= and sets =obj->last_use_time=; returns 0/1/2 as above.
- =ItemSpell= (1647) :: casts every =APPLY_ITEM_SPELL= apply on an object via =phys_cast=, sets the object's cooldown; returns cast count or 254 when cooling down.
- =ItemSetSpell= (1703) :: same for an equipped item set's tier bonuses; records per-ability cooldowns on =ch->item_set_ability=.
- External helpers relied on: =exit_game= (handler.c:4594) saves via =save_char(ch, rented ? in_room : NOWHERE)=, extracts and frees the character and returns the descriptor to the account menu; =remove_junk= (reception.c:360) extracts no-rent items (negative =cost_per_day=, TRASH, CONSTRUCTION, ARENA_ONLY, TEST, =is_no_rent=) from equipment, inventory and both warehouses; =save_char= (player.save.c:198) writes =stash/<c>/<name>=, field 49 = load room; =GuildMaster= (guild.c:27) practice listing; =get_rarity= (obj2.c:1410); =stealth_penalty= (obj1.c:1349); =check_peaceful= (fight.c:182); =magic_ok= (magic.c:4285); =CORRUPT= (offensive.c:36); =find_criminal= / =make_criminal= (crime.c:11/47); =raw_remove_follower= / =raw_add_follower= / =count_pc_followers= (spell_parser.c:511/582/473); =max_followers= (move.c:1942); =item_set_ability_to_char= / =get_item_set_ability_cooldown= (handler.c:2097/2122).

* skills1.c — non-combat skills

** disarm (=do_disarm=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: skills1.c:16
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: disarm [victim]
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; a visible char in the room. If omitted or not found, defaults to =ch->fighting=. A mob that is fighting always targets =ch->fighting=.
*** What it does
Refused in PEACEFUL rooms (=check_peaceful=, unless level > I8), against yourself, or when =ch->attackers > 3=. Runs the PvP legality check =CORRUPT= and, if a crime results, =make_criminal=. Rolls =number(1,101)=, minus 10x the attacker's dex reaction, plus 10x the victim's, minus 50 if the attacker is not wielding anything, plus 5 per victim level above 18 when the victim is over level 20; success when the roll is at or below =get_skill(ch,SKILL_DISARM)=. On failure the attacker is knocked to =POSITION_SITTING=, =FailSkill= may award a skill point, and a 3-second =WAIT_STATE= is applied. On success the victim's wielded weapon is unequipped and placed in the *victim's own inventory* (the drop-to-room code is commented out); wait is 1 second. Either way an awake, idle NPC victim starts fighting the attacker.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->fighting=, =ch->attackers=, =ch->equipment[WIELD]=, =victim->equipment[WIELD]=, =dex_app[]=, =GET_LEVEL(victim)=, skill table
- Writes :: =ch->position= (fail), =victim->equipment[WIELD]= / =victim->carrying= (success), =ch->wait=, victim's =fighting= via =start_fighting=, skill value via =FailSkill=/=set_skill=, criminal state via =make_criminal=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =make_criminal= → =vlog(LOG_CHEAT, "Char X attacks Y with 'disarm'")= when a crime is recorded.

** bearform (=do_bearform=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: skills1.c:112
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: bearform
*** Parameters
- none (argument ignored)
*** What it does
Druid-only (or any immortal) shapeshift. Requires =SKILL_BEARFORM= >= 1, not fighting, not already =AFF_BEARFORM=. Mana cost is =MAX(50, mana/8)= (0 for immortals), divided by the =REMORT_IMPROVE_BEARFORM= multiplier. Roll =number(1,101)= minus half the remort stat bonus must be <= skill, else "nature won't obey" and half the mana cost is lost. On success it strips any other form affect (=AFFECT_CATFORM/BOOMFORM/TREEFORM/NORMFORM=), all druid pulse stacks (cat haste/pene, bear hp/stop, killing spree, flurry, roar) and prowl, then applies five =AFFECT_BEARFORM= affects with duration 10: =APPLY_HIT= (+hp, min 50), =APPLY_ALL_STOPPING= (+, min 1), =APPLY_MELEE_HASTE= (negative, i.e. slower, min 5), =APPLY_LIFE_STEAL= (+, min 2), =APPLY_DAMROLL= (+, min 2). Modifiers scale with level, remort count and STR/CON/DEX/INT; the remort bonus multiplies them. Deducts mana and gives a 2-second wait (not for immortals).
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: class, =GET_MANA=, remort bonuses, stats, =ch->remort_count=, =ch->affected=, pulse affects
- Writes :: =ch->mana=, =ch->affected= (adds =AFFECT_BEARFORM=, removes other forms and pulse stacks), =ch->affected_by= (=AFF_BEARFORM=), =ch->wait=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save (affects are saved with the player).

** catform (=do_catform=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: skills1.c:230
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: catform
*** Parameters
- none
*** What it does
Same gating and mana/roll scheme as =bearform= using =SKILL_CATFORM= / =REMORT_IMPROVE_CATFORM=. Strips other forms and druid pulse stacks (but not prowl). Applies five =AFFECT_CATFORM= affects, duration 10: =APPLY_HIT= (*negative*: -15% max hp adjusted, min 50), =APPLY_ALL_STOPPING= (negative, forced to 0 for level <= 100), =APPLY_MELEE_HASTE= (+, min 10), =APPLY_MELEE_PENETRATION= (+, min 5), =APPLY_HITNDAM= (+, min 3). Cat form is the prerequisite for =prowl=, =pounce=, =rake= and =flurry=. A player whose account name is "dingo" gets a different room message. 2-second wait for mortals.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: as bearform, plus =ch->account_name=, =GET_HITROLL=
- Writes :: =ch->mana=, =ch->affected= / =ch->affected_by= (=AFF_CATFORM=), =ch->wait=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** boomform (=do_boomform=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: skills1.c:352
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: boomform
*** Parameters
- none
*** What it does
Owlbeast form. Same gating/mana/roll scheme (=SKILL_BOOMFORM=, =REMORT_IMPROVE_BOOMFORM=). Strips other forms, druid pulse stacks and prowl, then applies a single =AFFECT_BOOMFORM= affect (=APPLY_NONE=, duration 10) whose only effect is the =AFF_BOOMFORM= bit; no stat modifiers are set here (the bit is consumed elsewhere, e.g. spell code). 2-second wait for mortals.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: class, mana, skill, remort bonuses
- Writes :: =ch->mana=, =ch->affected= / =ch->affected_by= (=AFF_BOOMFORM=), =ch->wait=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

:END:
*** Syntax
: treeform
*** Parameters

Walking-tree form. Identical structure to =boomform= with =SKILL_TREEFORM= / =REMORT_IMPROVE_TREEFORM= and =AFFECT_TREEFORM= (=APPLY_NONE=, duration 10, sets =AFF_TREEFORM=). Strips other forms, pulse stacks and prowl. 2-second wait for mortals.

** normform (=do_normform=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: skills1.c:507
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: normform
*** Parameters
- none
*** What it does
Return to the character's racial form (message uses =race_list[ch->race]=). Requires druid/immortal, =SKILL_NORMFORM= >= 1, not fighting, and currently being in bear, cat, boom or tree form. Mana cost =MAX(50, mana/8)= (0 for immortals, and 0 if the bear, cat and tree remort bonuses are all >= 5). The skill roll is skipped entirely when the bear and cat remort bonuses are both >= 5; otherwise failure costs half the mana. On success it removes all four form affects and the druid pulse stacks and prowl, then applies =AFFECT_NORMFORM= (=APPLY_NONE=, duration 10, sets =AFF_NORMFORM=). 2-second wait for mortals.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->race=, class, mana, skill, remort bonuses, form affects
- Writes :: =ch->mana=, =ch->affected= / =ch->affected_by= (=AFF_NORMFORM=), =ch->wait=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** flurry (=do_flurry_attacks=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: skills1.c:733
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: flurry
*** Parameters
- none
*** What it does
Cat-form self-buff usable by mobs, druids or immortals with =SKILL_FLURRY= >= 1; players must be in =AFF_CATFORM=. Refused while the skill is on cooldown (=check_spell_skill_cooldown=). Computes haste/penetration/damroll increases (=dice(5, 5+...)=, floors 5/5/2) and two *debuffs*: =APPLY_ALL_STOPPING= (-10 scaled, -2 at level <= 100) and =APPLY_RESIST_ALL= (-50 scaled, -5 at level <= 100), never above 0. First skill roll failure ("paws get tangled") sets a 10-35s cooldown and a 4s wait. Otherwise a =SKILL_FLURRY= pulse affect is created (duration =20 + remort/2 + bonus=, cooldown =300 - remort/15=) and the two debuffs are applied immediately; a *second* skill roll then decides whether the haste, penetration and damroll buffs are added (failure leaves the debuffs in place with a 4s wait). Success calls =unsneak= and waits 3s.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: class, =AFF_CATFORM=, skill, remort bonuses, stats, level, cooldown list
- Writes :: =ch->affected_pulse= (SKILL_FLURRY buffs/debuffs), =ch->affected_pulse_cooldown= (on failure), sneak/prowl/vanish/shimmer affects removed by =unsneak=, =ch->wait=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** prowl (=do_prowl=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: skills1.c:843
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: prowl
*** Parameters
- none
*** What it does
Cat-form stealth toggle for druids/immortals with =SKILL_PROWL= >= 1 and =AFF_CATFORM=. If already prowling, removes the =SKILL_PROWL= pulse affect and returns. Otherwise checks cooldown, applies a 2-3.5s wait, and rolls =number(1,101)= against =skill + remortBonus + dex_app_skill[DEX].sneak + stealth_penalty(ch)= (the remort bonus is trimmed so the total does not exceed 100). Failure silently sets a 10-35s cooldown. Success adds a =SKILL_PROWL= pulse affect (no modifiers) lasting =number(25, MAX(45, remort*3)) + bonus/3=; its cooldown is =60 - remort/15=, or 0 if a luck roll based on WIS+INT+LCK+remort passes. While prowling, =DisplayMove= hides the cat's arrivals/departures from mortals and =pounce= gets a crit/damage bonus. =PLR_DEBUG= players get a success message.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: class, skill, =dex_app_skill[]=, worn metal weight (=stealth_penalty=), remort bonuses, cooldowns
- Writes :: =ch->affected_pulse= (add/remove SKILL_PROWL), =ch->affected_pulse_cooldown=, =ch->wait=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** pounce (=do_pounce=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: skills1.c:923
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: pounce <victim>
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; required, visible char in room (no default to =ch->fighting= for players; fighting mobs override to =ch->fighting=).
*** What it does
Cat-form opener for mobs, druids or immortals with =SKILL_POUNCE= >= 1 (players need =AFF_CATFORM=). Blocked in PEACEFUL rooms below I9, on yourself, when the victim has >= 3 attackers (below I5), or when already fighting (below I5). Immortals >= I10 pouncing a *player* kill them outright with =die()= (refused with a note to the victim if the imm is < I11 and the target is > I10). Runs =CORRUPT= and =make_criminal=. The "roll" starts at 0 and is only adjusted by the dex-reaction difference, so against comparable dex the skill check practically always passes; failure also occurs if the resisted damage is 0. Damage = =dice(((DEX*(remort+avg_dam))+level)/10, 3+remort/30)=, run through =CheckResistanceAttackType=, x8 for mobs above level 250, boosted while prowling (x1.11 and +10 crit chance, or remort-scaled), min 75, doubled on a crit roll, remort-multiplied, /1.5 vs sanctuary. The victim also gets a =POUNCE_INITIATE= pulse debuff (-melee haste) if not already carrying one. Failure: =unsneak=, =AddHated=, a zero-damage =damage()= to start combat, 1-2s wait. Success against a victim below fighting position (asleep/sitting/resting): damage x(1.07+remort/600) +-5%, cat haste/pene stacks, killing spree on crit; against an awake victim: +-5% damage, stacks, spree on crit; 3-4.5s wait. Victims at or below STUNNED take nothing.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: class, =AFF_CATFORM=, skill, =dex_app[]=, =ch->damnodice/damsizedice=, =GET_DAMROLL=, =str_app[16]=, =ch->hit_bonus=, remort bonuses, victim affects/attackers/position
- Writes :: victim hp via =damage_no_resist_check= / =damage=, victim pulse affects (=POUNCE_INITIATE=), victim hate list (=AddHated= sets =ACT_HATEFUL=), =ch->affected_pulse= (cat stacks, killing spree), sneak removed, fighting state, =ch->wait=, criminal state
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save (death handling in =die=/=damage= may save separately).
- Log lines: =make_criminal= → =LOG_CHEAT= on crime.

** bearslam (=do_bearslam=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: skills1.c:1107
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: bearslam <victim>
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; required, visible in room (fighting mobs use =ch->fighting=).
*** What it does
Bear-form opener (mob/druid/immortal, =SKILL_BEARSLAM= >= 1, players need =AFF_BEARFORM=). Blocked in PEACEFUL rooms, on yourself, victim with >= 3 attackers (below I5), or while fighting (below I5). =CORRUPT= / =make_criminal=. Roll =number(1,101)= +- dex reactions vs =skill + remortBonus/2= (capped at 100). Damage = =dice(((((CON+STR/2)*(remort+avg_dam))+level)/19)/2.7, 2+remort/30)=, min 50, resisted, x8 for mobs > 250, remort-multiplied, /2 vs sanctuary. Failure: attacker falls to =POSITION_SITTING=, takes 10 self-damage, zero-damage hit starts combat, =AddHated=, 3-5s wait. Success against a victim below fighting position also fails the same way ("sitting opponent"). Success against an awake victim: bear hp/stopping stacks, victim gets a 4-6s wait and is knocked to =POSITION_SITTING=, damage +-5% applied, attacker waits 3-4.5s.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: class, skill, stats, remort, victim position/attackers/affects
- Writes :: =ch->position=, =ch->hit= (self damage), =victim->position=, =victim->wait=, victim hp, =ch->affected_pulse= (bear stacks), hate list, fighting state, =ch->wait=, criminal state
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =make_criminal= → =LOG_CHEAT= on crime.

** roar (=do_roar=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: skills1.c:1224
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: roar
*** Parameters
- none
*** What it does
Bear-form shout (mob/druid/immortal, =SKILL_ROAR= >= 1, players need =AFF_BEARFORM=), refused on cooldown. Calls =unsneak= before rolling. Skill roll failure ("turns into a meoowwww") sets a 10-35s cooldown and 4s wait. Success creates a =SKILL_ROAR= pulse affect (duration =10 + remort/2 + bonus=, cooldown =300 - remort/15=) with =APPLY_HIT= (+hp, min 100; current hp is raised by the same amount and =update_pos= run), =APPLY_ALL_STOPPING= (min 3) and =APPLY_RESIST_ALL= (min 10). Then every other character in the room that passes =CORRUPT= (no =make_criminal= call), is visible, is not already debuffed, and fails a 10-point resistance check receives a =DRUID_BEAR_ROAR_DEBUFF= pulse affect lasting =30 + remort + bonus= with negative =APPLY_MELEE_HASTE=, =APPLY_MELEE_PENETRATION= and =APPLY_DAMROLL=. 3s wait.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: class, skill, stats, =GET_MAX_HIT=, room =people= list, victim affects
- Writes :: =ch->hit=, =ch->position= (via =update_pos=), =ch->affected_pulse= (SKILL_ROAR), other chars' =affected_pulse= (roar debuff), cooldowns, sneak removed, =ch->wait=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** maul (=do_maul=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: skills1.c:1340
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: maul [victim]
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; defaults to =ch->fighting= if not given/found (fighting mobs always use =ch->fighting=).
*** What it does
Bear-form attack (mob/druid/immortal, =SKILL_MAUL= >= 1, players need =AFF_BEARFORM=). Blocked in PEACEFUL rooms, while under =BERZERK=, on yourself, when =ch->attackers > 10= or =victim->attackers >= 12=. Calls =unsneak=, =CORRUPT=, =make_criminal=. Roll = =((10 - body_AC/10) << 1) + number(1,101)= vs =skill + remortBonus/2=. Base damage =dice(((((CON+STR/2)*(remort+avg_dam))+level)/17)/2.5, 2+remort/30)=, resisted, x8 for mobs > 250. Failure: zero-damage hit, =AddHated=, 4-7s wait. Success: damage is re-rolled as =dice(MAX(2+remort/15, level/125+remort/30), dmg)= (the base becomes the die size), remort-multiplied, x1.05+ if the victim is below fighting position, /2 vs sanctuary; crit (=roll <= critChance=) doubles it and grants bear hp/stopping stacks, a non-crit grants stacks only on roll > 50. 4-6s wait.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: class, skill, =GET_BODY_AC(victim)=, stats, =ch->dam_bonus=, remort, victim affects/position
- Writes :: victim hp, hate list, =ch->affected_pulse= (bear stacks), sneak removed, fighting state, =ch->wait=, criminal state
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =make_criminal= → =LOG_CHEAT= on crime.

** rake (=do_rake=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: skills1.c:1466
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: rake [victim]
*** Parameters
- =victim= :: =only_argument=; defaults to =ch->fighting=.
*** What it does
Cat-form counterpart of =maul= (=SKILL_RAKE=, =AFF_CATFORM=). Same blocks (peaceful, berzerk, self, attacker counts), =unsneak=, =CORRUPT=/=make_criminal=, and the same AC-adjusted roll. Base damage =dice(((((DEX+STR/2)*(remort+avg_dam))+level)/15)/2, 3+remort/30)=, resisted, x8 for mobs > 250; unlike maul it is *not* re-diced on success. Remort multiplier, x1.05+ vs a victim below fighting position, /1.5 vs sanctuary. Crit doubles damage and grants cat haste/pene stacks plus a =apply_cat_killing_spree= chance; non-crit grants stacks on roll > 50. Failure: zero-damage hit, =AddHated=, 2-5s wait. 3-4s wait on success.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: class, skill, =GET_BODY_AC(victim)=, stats, =ch->hit_bonus=, remort, victim affects
- Writes :: victim hp, hate list, =ch->affected_pulse= (cat stacks, killing spree), sneak removed, fighting state, =ch->wait=, criminal state
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =make_criminal= → =LOG_CHEAT= on crime.

** track (=do_track=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: skills1.c:1596
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: track <name>
*** Parameters
- =name= :: =only_argument=; required. Player name (hashed with =track_hash_num=, negative key) or a mob name matched with =isname= against the namelist of each mob vnum recorded in the room's tracks.
*** What it does
Reads the room's =track[]= array (laid down by =RawMove=). Fails if the room is dark (=IS_LIGHT=), has no track array, no entry matches the name, or the newest matching entry is 48 or more mud-hours old (=SECS_PER_MUD_HOUR= = 60s). Sets =ch->tracking= to the quarry's track vnum, applies a 2s wait, then =track()= rolls =number(1,100)= against =get_skill(ch,SKILL_HUNT) - 2*age= (mobs skip the roll); on success =PLR_HUNTING= is set and =show_track= reports the direction of the freshest track. Note the skill consulted is =SKILL_HUNT=, not a dedicated track skill. While hunting, movement code calls =show_track= each step until the quarry is found or the trail is lost (=clear_tracking=).
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =rp->track[]= / =rp->max_tracks=, =real_mobp(vnum)->mob->namelist= (dereferenced without a NULL check), skill, room light
- Writes :: =ch->tracking=, =SET_BIT(ch->act, PLR_HUNTING)=, =ch->wait=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** doorbash (=do_doorbash=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: skills1.c:1744
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: doorbash <door-keyword> [direction]
: doorbash <direction>
*** Parameters
- =type= / =direction= :: =argument_interpreter=; resolved by =find_door= (keyword plus optional direction; a bare direction works because =find_exit_name= also accepts direction names when the exit has no keyword mismatch).
*** What it does
Fighter door-breaking skill. Not while fighting; needs >= 10 move points. The exit must lead somewhere, must be closed, and going UP into a =SECT_AIR= room requires =AFF_FLYING=. Announces the charge and =consume_moves(ch,10)=. If the door is locked and =EX_PICKPROOF= or =EX_CRACKABLE=, or the character has no =SKILL_DOORBASH=, or =number(1,100)= exceeds the skill, =slam_into_wall= runs: 2-20 hp lost (floored at 0) and position set to STUNNED. On success =EX_LOCKED= is cleared and =raw_open_door= opens both sides (revealing secret doors), the basher loses 1-5 hp, and a dex check (=number(1,23) > DEX=) either carries them through into the next room (=char_from_room/char_to_room=, look, =DisplayMove=, 2s wait, 10 more moves) or leaves them in place (1s wait, 5 more moves).
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->fighting=, =GET_MOVE=, room exits, =SECTOR_TYPE(d->to_room)=, skill, DEX
- Writes :: =ch->move= (10, then 5 or 10), =ch->hit=, =ch->position= (STUNNED on failure), =d->exit_info= and reverse exit (=EX_LOCKED= cleared, =EX_CLOSED= cleared), =ch->in_room= (on stumble-through), =ch->wait=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; door state is in-memory world state (reset by zone resets); character changes persist on the next routine player save.

** retreat (=do_retreat=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: skills1.c:1843
:MIN_POS: FIG (fighting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0
:END:
*** Syntax
: retreat <direction|exit-name>
*** Parameters
- =direction= :: =one_argument=; required; matched with =is_abbrev= against =fname(exit_name(d))= for each exit in the room (direction words or keyword exits).
*** What it does
Controlled flee. Must be fighting, know =SKILL_RETREAT= (> 0), and have at most 5 attackers. The chosen exit is validated with =ValidMove= (which may auto-open/unlock a door). Roll =number(1,101) > skill= fails with a 2s wait and =FailSkill=. On success =stop_fighting=, then =RawMove= moves the character (with normal move cost, tracks, traps, death/fall rooms) but *without* =DisplayMove= departure/arrival messages, followers, or autoclose processing.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->fighting=, =ch->attackers=, skill, room exits
- Writes :: =ch->fighting= cleared, =ch->in_room=, =ch->move=, =ch->wait=, room tracks, skill via =FailSkill=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** fish (=do_fish=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: skills1.c:1936
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0
:END:
*** Syntax
: fish
*** Parameters
- none
*** What it does
Requires =SKILL_FISH= > 0 and >= 15 move points (the leading =AFF_FISH= "stop fishing" branch can never trigger; nothing sets =AFF_FISH=). The room must contain an object named "stream" (fresh water), "wharf" or "sea beach" (=generic_find= FIND_OBJ_ROOM), and the character must have a "fishing rod" equipped (FIND_OBJ_EQUIP). Costs 10 move points (=ch->move -= 10= directly). Roll =number(1,100) > skill + DEX= means no bite. Otherwise a second =number(1,100)=: < 10 loads rubbish (vnum =number(1,10)+1=, i.e. 2-11; a 5 becomes vnum 5105); > 90 loads a fish by water type (stream 10008-10011, wharf 10012-10017, beach 10017-10020) and raises the skill by 1 if below 100; 10-90 is "just a nibble". The catch is loaded to the room (=get_obj=, =setup_obj_ev=, =obj_to_room=) with =timer2 = DROP_TICKS= (30) and 1-200 extra intrinsic weight.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: skill, =GET_MOVE=, =GET_DEX=, room contents, equipment
- Writes :: =ch->move=, skill via =set_skill(ch,SKILL_FISH,+1)=, new =obj_data= in room (=timer2=, =intrinsic_weight=)
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_DEBUG, "Fishing Routine tried to load a non-existing item.")= if the vnum has no prototype.

** dice (=do_dice=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: skills1.c:2071
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0
:END:
*** Syntax
: dice
*** Parameters
- none
*** What it does
Social-style roll of two six-sided dice. Removes =AFF_SNEAK= (=affect_from_char(ch, SKILL_SNEAK)=) with a "no longer sneaking" message, then shows the two results to the whole room via =send_to_room=. No game effect beyond that.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->affected_by=
- Writes :: sneak affect removed
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** Helpers in skills1.c
- =apply_catform_haste_stack= (598) :: adds/refreshes the stacking =DRUID_CAT_STACK_HASTE= pulse buff (+melee haste, stacks up to =MAX(10, remort/1.5)=).
- =apply_catform_pene_stack= (624) :: same for =DRUID_CAT_STACK_PENE= (+melee penetration).
- =apply_bearform_hp_stack= (648) :: stacking =DRUID_BEAR_STACK_HP= (+hit points, stacks up to =MAX(4, remort/5)=).
- =apply_bearform_stopping_stack= (672) :: stacking =DRUID_BEAR_STACK_STOP= (+all stopping).
- =apply_cat_killing_spree= (688) :: chance roll on stats/remort; on success applies =DRUID_CAT_KILLING_SPREE= (haste, hitndam, penetration, life steal, each =dice(5,5)= scaled by =REMORT_IMPROVE_KILLING_SPREE=).
- =track= (1658) :: finds the newest track for =ch->tracking= in the current room, rolls =SKILL_HUNT - 2*age= for players, returns the exit to follow.
- =clear_tracking= (1676) :: zeroes =ch->tracking= and clears =PLR_HUNTING= / =ACT_HUNTING=.
- =show_track= (1686) :: reports quarry found, trail lost (and clears), or the direction of the trail.
- =found_tracked= (1706) :: returns the char in the room whose =track_vnum= matches =ch->tracking=.
- =slam_into_wall= (1716) :: doorbash failure: 2-20 hp damage, position STUNNED, room message.
- =NewFailSkill= (1888) :: unused variant of FailSkill with a 25% chance to lose 1-5 points above guild max, else a chance to gain.
- =FailSkill= (1912) :: on skill failure, if the skill is at/above =max_at_guild= and below =max_learn=, a roll above the current value grants +1 (=set_skill=).

* move.c — movement

** north, east, south, west, up, down, ne, se, sw, nw (=do_move=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: move.c:735
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: north | east | south | west | up | down | ne | se | sw | nw
*** Parameters
- none :: the direction comes from the =cmd= number; =arg= is only consulted when =cmd-1 > LAST_DIR= (never true for the registered entries).
- =cmd= mapping :: =cmd_info= =num= values 1..10 → =cmd-1= → direction index: 1 north (=NORTH= 0), 2 east (1), 3 south (2), 4 west (3), 5 up (4), 6 down (5), 7 ne (=NORTHEAST= 6), 8 se (7), 9 sw (8), 10 nw (9). =LAST_DIR= is 9. Short forms such as =n= / =e= work through the interpreter's abbreviation matching, not separate table entries. No other file calls =do_move=.
*** What it does
Refuses while falling (=ch->fallen_rooms=), or while fighting with more than one attacker ("no place to flee"; if =attackers > 1= but not fighting, the count is reset to 0 with a =LOG_DEBUG= line), or under =SPELL_CLINGING_VINES=. Looks up the exit with =find_exit_num(rp, cmd-1)= and calls =Move(ch, d, 0)=. After a successful move there is an "INTREPID" auto-charge loop, but its condition requires both =IS_AGGR(ch)= (which implies =IS_NPC=) and =!IS_NPC(ch)=, so it can never fire.

Movement rules, as implemented by =Move= → =RawMove= → =ValidMove=:
- Blockers (=Move=): =SPELL_CLINGING_VINES=, pulse =SPELL_PARALYSIS=, =SPELL_CONFUSION=, =SPELL_STONE_ENCASE=. Charmed characters whose master is in the room refuse to leave (=RawMove=).
- Exits and doors (=ValidMove=): no exit → "Alas, you cannot go that way". A closed =EX_ISDOOR= exit is auto-opened; if =EX_LOCKED=, players without =CONFIG_MANUAL_UNLOCK= auto-unlock with a carried/equipped key via =phys_unlock_door= (not for =EX_CRACKABLE= doors, which demand a 4-digit pin), otherwise the move fails. A closed non-door exit with no keyword or =EX_SECRET= is reported as no exit; otherwise "seems to be closed".
- Special procedures: =special(ch, d->num+1, "")= runs room/object/mob SPEC_MOVE procs (north is 1) and can veto the move.
- Room restrictions (below I9): =max_people= capacity (counting the whole follower tree via =count_following=); =NO_GOTO=; =LORD_ONLY=; =ANTI_GOOD/EVIL/NEUTRAL= by true alignment for players (bypassed by =REMORT_ZEN_CONJUNCTION=) or plain alignment for mobs; =PEACEFUL= and =ANTI_*= rooms are also barred to criminals (=ch->rent_disable= in the future) → "A strange force bars your movement".
- Sectors: =sector[to_room->sector_type]->mc[]= holds up to three move-cost rows: [0] boat (requires an =ITEM_BOAT= in inventory, =has_boat=), [1] flying (=AFF_FLYING=), [2] walking. The first applicable row is used; if none applies the messages are "You would need a boat" / "You would need to Fly" / "ask an immortal". Walking into a =SEC_WATER_BREATH= sector needs =AFF_WATERBREATH= (below I9). Sector table is booted from =lib/sector.out= (=SECTOR_FILE=).
- Move points: non-immortals pay =m->move_cost= via =consume_moves= (can go as low as -25% of max). Being at or below the cost does *not* block movement; it adds about one extra second of delay ("You move slowly due to exhaustion"), as does any player follower who is exhausted, and =SKILL_CRIPPLING_STRIKE= adds 1-3s. Base delay for players is =(m->delay+1)*WAIT_SEC/4= (or /5 when flying); it is written straight to =ch->wait= (and to player followers' =wait=) after the move.
- Effects of arriving: fighting stops; leaving an =ARENA= room destroys arena-only gear (=remove_arena_only=); =EX_MESSAGE= exits print the room's =_ex<dir>= extra description; move traps in the old room fire (=CheckForMoveTrap=); =do_look=; tracks are laid in the old room with probability =m->track_prob= (3 slots by default, oldest overwritten); =DEATH= rooms kill non-immortals (=death_trap=); =FALL= rooms start the falling event chain (=fall_down=/=event_fall=, weight-based damage on landing); sector damage =m->damage - 10 + avgAC/10= (bracken/thicket).
- Followers: each follower in the old room with =FIGHT_POS >= STANDING= follows ("You follow $N"); mobs do not follow from a =NO_MOB= room into an =ARENA= or out of an =ARENA=; followers with their own followers recurse. A follower who fails to move aborts the remaining followers.
- Autoclose: after the leader moves, an =EX_AUTOCLOSE= door closes (and =EX_AUTOLOCK= locks, regenerating the code on =EX_CRACKABLE= doors) on both sides.
- Departure/arrival messages (=DisplayMove=) are suppressed for sneaking characters (=AFF_SNEAK=, or prowl in cat form, or vanish) except to immortals, and for immortals >= I10 to lower-level viewers.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->fallen_rooms=, =ch->attackers=, =ch->fighting=, room exits, =sector[]=, room flags, =to_here->max_people=, =ch->followers=, =ch->rent_disable=, affects, =ch->config_bits=, keys carried
- Writes :: =ch->in_room= (=char_from_room=/=char_to_room=), =ch->move=, =ch->wait= and followers' =wait=, =ch->fighting= (=stop_fighting=), =ch->attackers= (reset), =ch->position= (=stop_running=), =d->exit_info= / reverse exit (auto-open, autoclose, autolock), =d->mm_crack_code= / =mm_crack_attempts=, key =value[0]= or key extracted, room =track[]=, =ch->hit= (sector damage, falls, death traps), =ch->fallen_rooms=, arena-only objects extracted
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save. Door/track state is in-memory world state.
- Log lines: =LOG_DEBUG= "fixed X -- Game thought X was being attacked"; =LOG_DEBUG= corrupted room list; =LOG_URGENT= unsupported sector type / cannot make tracks; =LOG_MISC= exit flagged message/autoclose without data; =LOG_FALL= fall entry and damage; =LOG_DEATH= death-trap entry; =LOG_CHEAT= via =make_criminal= inside traps/damage paths.

** open (=do_open=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: move.c:915
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: open <container>
: open <door-keyword> [direction]
: open <direction>
*** Parameters
- =type= / =dir= :: =argument_interpreter=. Objects are tried first with =generic_find(arg, FIND_OBJ_INV|FIND_OBJ_ROOM)= on the full argument; otherwise =find_door(ch,type,dir)=.
*** What it does
Objects: must be =ITEM_CONTAINER= or =ITEM_SPELL_POUCH=, currently =CONT_CLOSED=, =CONT_CLOSEABLE= and not =CONT_LOCKED= (locked crackable containers say to crack the code); then =CONT_CLOSED= is cleared. Doors: the exit must have =EX_ISDOOR= (immortals are told when the flag is missing) and be closed. A locked door is left alone if =CONFIG_MANUAL_UNLOCK= is set; otherwise the game tries =phys_unlock_door= with a key from inventory/containers/equipment (=has_readied(ch, d->key)=) and opens on success. =raw_open_door= clears =EX_CLOSED= on this exit and on the reverse exit in the target room, with "reveals a hidden passage" for =EX_SECRET= doors.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: inventory/room objects, =obj->value[1]=, room exits, =d->exit_info=, =d->key=, =ch->config_bits=
- Writes :: =obj->value[1]= (=CONT_CLOSED= cleared) or =d->exit_info= and reverse =b->exit_info= (=EX_CLOSED=, possibly =EX_LOCKED= cleared); key wear (=value[0]--= or key extracted) when auto-unlocking
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory world/object state (container flags persist with the object if it is later saved in a player file or room save).

** close (=do_close=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: move.c:1011
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: close <container>
: close <door-keyword> [direction]
*** Parameters
- =type= / =dir= :: as =open=.
*** What it does
Containers/spell pouches must be open and =CONT_CLOSEABLE=; sets =CONT_CLOSED=. Doors must have =EX_ISDOOR= and be open; =raw_close_door= sets =EX_CLOSED=, and if =EX_AUTOLOCK= also =EX_LOCKED= ("You hear a *click*"). The reverse exit in the target room is closed too (and autolocked if it carries =EX_AUTOLOCK=), with a message to that room.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: objects, exits
- Writes :: =obj->value[1]= (=CONT_CLOSED=) or =d->exit_info= / =b->exit_info= (=EX_CLOSED=, =EX_LOCKED=)
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory world/object state.

** lock (=do_lock=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: move.c:1146
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: lock <container>
: lock <door-keyword> [direction]
*** Parameters
- =type= / =dir= :: as =open=.
*** What it does
Containers: must be closed, have a key vnum (=value[2] >= 0=) and not be locked. =CONT_CRACKABLE= containers lock without a key and get a fresh 4-digit mastermind code (=create_mm_code=, digits 1-6) with attempts reset; others require =has_readied(ch, value[2])= and then set =CONT_LOCKED= (no key wear for containers). Doors (=raw_lock_door=): must be a door, closed, not already locked. =EX_CRACKABLE= doors lock keylessly with a new code and lock the reverse exit. Otherwise =d->key >= 0= and the key must be carried/equipped; =EX_LOCKED= is set on both sides and the key wears: if =key->value[0] > 1= it is decremented ("developed a crack"), if it is exactly 1 the key crumbles and is extracted.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: objects, exits, key inventory
- Writes :: =obj->value[1]= (=CONT_LOCKED=), =obj->mm_crack_code/attempts=, =d->exit_info= / =b->exit_info= (=EX_LOCKED=), =d->mm_crack_code/attempts=, key =value[0]= or key extracted
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory world/object state.

** unlock (=do_unlock=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: move.c:1255
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: unlock <container>
: unlock <door-keyword> [direction]
*** Parameters
- =type= / =dir= :: as =open=.
*** What it does
Containers: need a keyhole (=value[2] >= 0=), the matching key, and =CONT_LOCKED=; clears the lock and wears the key (decrement or crumble). Doors: =phys_unlock_door(ch, has_readied(ch,d->key), d)= — the key check comes *first* ("You don't have the proper key" even for non-doors), then door/closed/keyhole/locked checks; clears =EX_LOCKED= on both sides, announces "*click*", and wears the key. There is no =EX_CRACKABLE= check here, so a crackable door that also has a key vnum can be unlocked with the key.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: objects, exits, key inventory
- Writes :: =obj->value[1]= or =d->exit_info= / =b->exit_info= (=CONT_LOCKED= / =EX_LOCKED= cleared), key =value[0]= or key extracted
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory world/object state.

** pick (=do_pick=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: move.c:1307
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: pick <container>
: pick <door-keyword> [direction]
*** Parameters
- =type= / =dir= :: as =open=.
*** What it does
The skill roll happens up front: =number(1,101) > get_skill(ch,SKILL_PICK_LOCK)= marks failure (so a character with 0 skill always fails, but still gets the target checks below). Containers must be closed, locked, not =CONT_CRACKABLE= ("pin and needle" message), have a keyhole, and not be =CONT_PICKPROOF=. Doors must be =EX_ISDOOR=, closed, have =d->key >= 0=, be locked, not =EX_CRACKABLE=, not =EX_PICKPROOF=. On failure =FailSkill= runs and a 5-second wait applies. On success the lock flag is cleared (both sides for doors) with "The lock quickly yields to your skills".
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: skill, objects, exits
- Writes :: =obj->value[1]= (=CONT_LOCKED=) or =d->exit_info= / =b->exit_info= (=EX_LOCKED=), =ch->wait=, skill via =FailSkill=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory world/object state; skill gain persists on the next routine player save.

** crack (=do_crack=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: move.c:1388
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: crack <container> <code|reset>
: crack <door-keyword> <code|reset> [direction]
*** Parameters
- =object_arg= / =code_guess= :: =argument_interpreter= (first two words) used for the object lookup.
- =type= / =code_guess= / =dir= :: =three_argument_interpreter= over the same input; =type= names the door, =dir= an optional direction.
- =code= :: 4 digits, each 1-6, parsed with =atoi=; the literal word =reset= regenerates the code.
*** What it does
Mastermind-style lock puzzle for =CONT_CRACKABLE= containers and =EX_CRACKABLE= doors; the target must be closed and locked. =reset= draws a new code and zeroes the attempt counter ("12 tries left"). A non-numeric code is refused. =validate_mm_code= returns 1 for a correct code (lock flag cleared, both sides for doors), 254 for a malformed code (> 6666, fewer than 4 digits, or a digit outside 1-6; 2-second wait for mortals), or 0 for a wrong guess: it reports strong clicks (right digit, right place) and weak clicks (right digit, wrong place), increments =mm_crack_attempts=, regenerates the code after the 12th attempt, and the command applies a 5-second wait to mortals. A code of 0 on the target is lazily initialised on first use.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: objects, exits, =mm_crack_code=, =mm_crack_attempts=
- Writes :: =obj->value[1]= / =d->exit_info= + reverse (lock cleared), =obj|d->mm_crack_code=, =obj|d->mm_crack_attempts=, =ch->wait=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory world/object state.

** enter (=do_enter=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: move.c:1503
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: enter [exit-name]
*** Parameters
- =exit-name= :: =one_argument=; matched with =find_exit_name= (keyword exits first, then direction names, then any exit keyword). =cmd= is =CMD_ENTER= (65), which makes =special()= treat it as a SPEC_MOVE command.
*** What it does
With an argument, finds the named exit, runs =ValidMove= (door auto-open rules) and then =Move= (full movement rules, followers, autoclose). With no argument: if the current room is =INDOORS= you are told so; otherwise the first non-closed exit whose destination is =INDOORS= is taken via =Move=; if none, "You can't seem to find anything to enter."
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: room exits, destination =room_flags=
- Writes :: everything =Move= writes (position, moves, wait, tracks, door state, followers)
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** go (=do_go=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: move.c:1558
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: go <exit> [<exit> ...]
: go [count]<exit> [[count]<exit> ...]     (e.g. "go 3north east 2door")
*** Parameters
- =exit= :: each whitespace token via =one_argument=; leading digits are a repeat count (a count <= 0 stops parsing; the digits are stripped before lookup). Exit names go through =find_exit_name=. =cmd= is =CMD_GO= (67), a SPEC_MOVE special type.
*** What it does
Calls =stop_running= and resets =ch->remaining_moves=. The first step is executed immediately with =do_raw_go= (=find_exit_name= → =ValidMove= → =Move=; a missing exit prints "You see no exit named" and aborts the whole command). Remaining steps are appended as a space-separated string to =ch->remaining_moves=. If more than one step was requested the character enters =POSITION_RUNNING=, =ch->next_move= points at the queue, and an =EVT_RUN= event is scheduled with delay =ch->wait - con_app[CON].speed_walk_bonus= (0 for NPCs or negative). =event_run= performs one queued step per firing (minimum 35 pulses between steps, +15 if =IS_AFFECTED(ch, SPELL_FLY)=), first letting mobs in the room react (=act_state=), and stops if the character is no longer RUNNING (any command with minimum position >= FIGHTING stops a run) or is an NPC; the last step drops back to STANDING so the final look is not brief. Exit names over 30 characters are logged as a crash attempt.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: room exits, =con_app[]=, =ch->wait=
- Writes :: =ch->position= (RUNNING/STANDING), =ch->remaining_moves= (string block), =ch->next_move=, event queue (=EVT_RUN=), plus everything =Move= writes
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_URGENT, "%s just tried to crash the mud using go bug.")= for exit names longer than 30 chars.

** leave (=do_leave=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: move.c:1668
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: leave
*** Parameters
- none
*** What it does
Inverse of bare =enter=: requires the current room to be =INDOORS=, then takes the first non-closed exit whose destination room is *not* =INDOORS= via =Move=. Otherwise "I see no obvious exits to the outside."
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: room flags, exits
- Writes :: everything =Move= writes
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** pray (=do_pray=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: move.c:1690
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: pray
*** Parameters
- none; =cmd= is =CMD_PRAY= (46), passed to =special()= so room/object procs (altars) can intercept it.
*** What it does
Refused while fighting. From STANDING, SITTING, RESTING or RUNNING the character kneels and =ch->position= becomes =POSITION_PRAYING= (position 6, between RESTING and SITTING); already praying gives a notice; SLEEPING must wake first; any other position (unreachable given MIN_POS) also becomes PRAYING with a "plummets from the air" message. Commands with a higher minimum position tell a praying character to "Stop praying first".
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->fighting=, =GET_POS(ch)=
- Writes :: =ch->position=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** stand (=do_stand=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: move.c:1738
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: stand
*** Parameters
- none
*** What it does
Switches on =GET_POS(ch)= (not =FIGHT_POS=, so a fighting character whose stored position is STANDING is told "You are already standing"). SITTING, PRAYING and RESTING become =POSITION_STANDING=; SLEEPING must wake first. The default branch (in practice =POSITION_RUNNING=) prints "You stop floating around, and put your feet on the ground" but does not change =ch->position=, so a running character stays RUNNING.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =GET_POS(ch)=
- Writes :: =ch->position=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** sit (=do_sit=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: move.c:1772
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: sit
*** Parameters
- none
*** What it does
Switches on =FIGHT_POS(ch)=. RUNNING, STANDING, PRAYING and RESTING become =POSITION_SITTING=; already sitting, sleeping ("wake up first") and fighting ("are you MAD?") are refused; any other position also becomes SITTING.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =FIGHT_POS(ch)=
- Writes :: =ch->position=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** rest (=do_rest=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: move.c:1814
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: rest
*** Parameters
- none
*** What it does
Refused while fighting. RUNNING, STANDING, SITTING and PRAYING become =POSITION_RESTING=; already resting and sleeping are refused. The default branch sets =POSITION_SITTING= (not RESTING) after a "stop floating around" message; it is effectively unreachable because lower positions are filtered by MIN_POS.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->fighting=, =GET_POS(ch)=
- Writes :: =ch->position=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** sleep (=do_sleep=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: move.c:1857
:MIN_POS: SLE (sleeping)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: sleep
*** Parameters
- none
*** What it does
Refused while fighting. RUNNING, STANDING, SITTING, PRAYING and RESTING become =POSITION_SLEEPING=; already asleep gives a notice; any other reachable position also becomes SLEEPING.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->fighting=, =GET_POS(ch)=
- Writes :: =ch->position=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** wake (=do_wake=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: move.c:1887
:MIN_POS: SLE (sleeping)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:END:
*** Syntax
: wake
: wake <person>
*** Parameters
- =person= :: =one_argument=; optional; a visible character in the room.
*** What it does
With a target: you cannot wake others while asleep yourself; waking yourself by name is redirected to plain =wake=; a sleeping target under the =AFF_SLEEP= spell cannot be woken, otherwise the target's position becomes =POSITION_SITTING= with messages both ways; awake targets are reported as such. Without a target: if you are under =AFF_SLEEP= and not fighting you cannot wake; if already awake you are told so; otherwise you sit up (=POSITION_SITTING=). If you were asleep but still have =ch->fighting= set, combat is re-established: =ch->fighting= is cleared, the opponent's =attackers= is decremented, and =set_fighting= is called again (which also re-arms the strike event).
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =GET_POS=, =AFF_SLEEP= on self/target, =ch->fighting=
- Writes :: =ch->position= or =tmp_char->position=, =ch->fighting=, opponent =attackers=, new =EVT_STRIKE= event via =set_fighting=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.

** follow (=do_follow=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: move.c:1948
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:FLAGS: CMDF_NOARENA
:END:
*** Syntax
: follow <leader>
: follow self
*** Parameters
- =leader= :: =only_argument=; required; a visible character in the room. Naming yourself means "stop following".
*** What it does
Blocked in ARENA rooms both by =CMDF_NOARENA= (interpreter, immortals exempt) and an in-function =ROOM_FLAG(ARENA)= check (immortals exempt). Charmed characters with a master refuse to follow anyone else. Following yourself with a master calls =stop_follower= (removes you from the master's list, clears =AFF_CHARM=/=AFF_GROUP=) and then sets =AFF_GROUP=; with no master you are "already following yourself". =circle_follow= forbids loops. Mortals below I1 may not follow NPCs. Otherwise any current master is dropped, =AFF_GROUP= is cleared and =add_follower= links you into the leader's =followers= list (players are inserted before NPC followers) with the usual messages (suppressed for =PLR_STEALTH=). Followers move with the leader in =Move= and count toward room capacity. =max_followers= exists in this file but is not consulted here.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: room flags, =ch->master=, =ch->affected_by= (=AFF_CHARM=), =leader->master= chain, levels
- Writes :: =ch->master=, =leader->followers= list (=follow_type= allocated), =ch->affected_by= (=AFF_GROUP= set or cleared, =AFF_CHARM= cleared on stop)
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only (follow links are not saved at all).

** Helpers in move.c
- =average_ac= (15) :: mean of the five body-part AC values; used to soften sector damage.
- =NotLegalMove= (21) :: prints "Alas, you cannot go that way...".
- =ValidMove= (27) :: exit/door gate: auto-opens closed doors, auto-unlocks with a key unless =CONFIG_MANUAL_UNLOCK= or =EX_CRACKABLE=, hides secret closed exits.
- =newest_track= / =oldest_track= / =which_track= (77/90/103) :: index helpers over a room's =track[]= array.
- =track_hash_num= (116) :: negative hash of a name; player track ids.
- =track_vnum= (125) :: mob vnum, or name hash for players.
- =get_mob_alive_by_vnum= / =vnum_to_char= (132/145) :: look up a character by mob vnum or by negative track id.
- =has_boat= (155) :: first =ITEM_BOAT= in inventory.
- =RawMove= (164) :: the real movement engine: restrictions, sector move type, cost, delay, room transfer, exit message, traps, look, tracks, death/fall rooms, sector damage.
- =OpenExit= (435) :: exit is passable and destination not NO_GOTO (for I10+) / LORD_ONLY (below I9); used by fall rooms.
- =event_fall= / =fall_down= (466/516) :: falling event chain through =FALL= rooms; weight-based damage on landing, =LOG_FALL=.
- =death_trap= (543) :: kills a mortal in a =DEATH= room (=die= if carrying object 25 or =PLR_QUESTING=, else 100x max hp damage), =LOG_DEATH=.
- =Move= (578) :: spell blockers, =RawMove=, =DisplayMove=, follower propagation, autoclose/autolock handling.
- =DisplayMove= (798) :: leave/arrive messages, honouring sneak/prowl/vanish and imm invisibility.
- =find_door= (846) :: resolve a door keyword plus optional direction into a =room_exit=.
- =is_secret= (875) :: =EX_SECRET= test.
- =raw_open_door= / =raw_close_door= (880/979) :: flip =EX_CLOSED= on both sides with messages; close applies =EX_AUTOLOCK=.
- =has_readied_in_list= / =has_readied= (1056/1070) :: find an object by vnum in inventory (recursing into containers) or equipment.
- =raw_lock_door= (1085) :: lock a door with key (with key wear) or as a crackable lock (new code).
- =phys_unlock_door= (1196) :: unlock a door with a key object, both sides, key wear.
- =do_raw_go= (1538) :: one =go= step: exit by name → =ValidMove= → =Move=.
- =event_run= (1606) :: EVT_RUN handler performing queued =go= steps.
- =stop_running= (1656) :: clears the run queue and drops RUNNING to STANDING.
- =max_followers= (1942) :: CHA-based follower cap (3..8); defined but unused by =do_follow=.

* builder.c — room building, area save/load, goto/at, purge
** offset (=do_offset=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: builder.c:261
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I2 (2002)
:END:
*** Syntax
: offset <number>
*** Parameters
- =number= :: =atoi(arg)= of the whole argument; no validation (0 clears the offset).
*** What it does
Sets the builder's personal vnum offset. Every room number a builder types into
=goto=, =at=, =mat=, =form=, =rstat=, =redit exit/teleport=, =rdig= etc. goes through
=OFFSET(ch,val)= (h/utils.h:569): values 1..999 have =ch->offset= added, values
>= 1000 or <= 0 are used as-is. So a builder with offset 105000 can type
=goto 12= to reach room 105012.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =arg=
- Writes :: =ch->offset=
*** Persistence
- No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save (=ch->offset= is field 81 in player.save.c).

** foreach (=do_foreach=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: builder.c:267
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I10 (2010)
:END:
*** Syntax
: foreach <command template>
*** Parameters
- =command template= :: rest of line, used as a =snprintf= format string; up to four =%s= are replaced by the target player's name.
*** What it does
Iterates every player in =character_list= other than the invoker (NPCs skipped),
formats the template with that player's name and runs the result as a command
*by the invoker* via =command_interpreter= / =command_process=. E.g.
=foreach tell %s hello= sends a tell to everyone online. The template is passed
straight to =snprintf= as a format string, so stray =%= specifiers other than
=%s= are undefined behaviour.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =character_list=, =c->name=
- Writes :: none directly; whatever the generated commands do.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; effects depend on the generated command.

** rload (=do_rload=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: builder.c:371
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I2 (2002)
:END:
*** Syntax
: rload
: rload <filename>          (I9+ only)
*** Parameters
- =filename= :: optional, rest of line; only honoured if =GET_LEVEL(ch) >= I9=, otherwise ignored and the builder's own name is used.
*** What it does
Opens the binary area file =lib/Area/<ch->name>= (or =lib/Area/<filename>= for
I9+), checks the leading magic id 255 (only warns if wrong), then calls
=read_one_room= (roomdb.c:85) repeatedly. Each room read is allocated in
=room_db= via =allocate_room= (overwriting an existing room with the same vnum)
and gets =rp->zone = find_zone(vnum)=. Reports "N rooms loaded (lo-hi)". Note
=count= starts at 1, so the count reported is one more than the rooms actually
read. There is no build-range check: any room in the file is loaded.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: file =Area/<name>=, =ch->name=, =GET_LEVEL(ch)=
- Writes :: =room_db= (rooms created/overwritten by =read_one_room=)
*** Persistence
- Reads a file only; world in memory changes. No direct save.

** rsave (=do_rsave=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: builder.c:409
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I2 (2002)
:END:
*** Syntax
: rsave
: rsave <filename> <lo_vnum> <hi_vnum>      (I9+ only)
*** Parameters
- (none) :: saves =ch->build_lo= .. =ch->build_hi= to =Area/<ch->name>=. Fails with "You don't have a range yet." if =build_hi= is 0.
- =filename lo hi= :: I9+ only; file is =Area/<filename>=, range is =atoi= of the two numbers, =hi >= lo= required. Note the numbers are NOT offset-adjusted.
*** What it does
Binary-dumps every existing room in the range with =bin_write_room=
(builder.c:284), one record per room, id 255 leading each. If the filename has
no '.', the previous file is first renamed to =<file>.bak= via =system("mv ...")=
(if it has a '.', it calls =unlink(buf)= on an uninitialised buffer, which is a
bug but harmless in practice). Appends a line to =BUILD.LOG= with timestamp,
builder, room count, byte size and filename. Also used as the builder's normal
"save my work" command; the world proper is not touched.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->build_lo/build_hi=, =room_db= via =get_rp=, all room fields (=bin_write_room=)
- Writes :: none in memory
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: =bin_write_room= -> =lib/Area/<ch->name>= (or =lib/Area/<filename>=); old file renamed to =.bak=; appends to =lib/BUILD.LOG=.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_MISC,"%s saved %d rooms (%d bytes)")= and the old-file size; =LOG_URGENT= if the file cannot be opened.

** tsave (=do_save_range=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: builder.c:500
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I9 (2009)
:END:
*** Syntax
: tsave <filename> <lo_vnum> <hi_vnum>
*** Parameters
- =filename= :: first whitespace-delimited word (no path sanitising).
- =lo hi= :: =atoi= of the next two args; =hi >= lo= required; warns (but proceeds) if more than 1000 rooms.
*** What it does
Text-format world export: calls =save_range(filename,lo,hi)= (builder.c:481),
which opens =WORLD/<filename>= and writes each existing room in the range with
=write_room= (builder.c:130) in the =tran= source syntax (=name {..} desc {..}
sector {..} exits { to {..} ..} ..=). Room numbers are written relative to =lo=
(or as =area:offset= for rooms outside the range when =area.list= has been
loaded by =boot_areas=). This is the human-readable counterpart of =rsave=,
intended for feeding back into the =tran= world compiler.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =room_db= via =get_rp=, =area_list=, =sector[]=, =dirs[]=, =room_bits=, =exit_bits=
- Writes :: statics =current_low/current_high= (used by =room_number=)
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: =save_range= -> =lib/WORLD/<filename>= (text, tran format).
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_DEBUG,"Saving %s (%d-%d)")=; =LOG_URGENT= on open failure.

** at (=do_at=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: builder.c:548
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I2 (2002)
:END:
*** Syntax
: at <room#> <command>
: at <char name> <command>
: at <object name> <command>
*** Parameters
- =location= :: first word (=half_chop=). Digits -> =OFFSET(ch,atoi)= must be an existing room; else a visible char (=get_char_vis=), else a visible object in the world with =in_room != NOWHERE=.
- =command= :: rest of line, executed via =command_interpreter=/=command_process=.
*** What it does
Temporarily moves the immortal to the target room, runs the command there, then
moves them back if they are still in that room. Refuses rooms flagged =NO_GOTO=
below I10 and =LORD_ONLY= below I9. Immortals below I3 are restricted to their
own build range (=CHK_PLAYER_ZONE=). NPCs may not use it. Also used internally
by =do_mat= (builder.c:1079), the =look= code (look.c:838) and relocation
spells (magic_relo.c) with =cmd=0=.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =room_db=, =ROOM_FLAG=, =ch->build_lo/hi=
- Writes :: =ch->in_room= (via =char_from_room=/=char_to_room=, twice); =stop_fighting(ch)= clears combat both ways.
*** Persistence
- No direct save; depends on the embedded command.

** goto (=do_goto=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: builder.c:617
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I1 (2001)
:END:
*** Syntax
: goto <room#>
: goto <char name | object name>
: goto linkdead
*** Parameters
- =room#= :: digits with no '.', passed through =OFFSET(ch,..)=.
- =name= :: visible char anywhere (=get_char_vis_world=) or visible object with a room.
- =linkdead= :: literal; closes the immortal's own socket (=close_socket(ch->desc)=), simulating link death.
*** What it does
Teleports the immortal. If the numbered room does not exist and the caller is
I2+ with a non-negative vnum inside their build range, the room is *created* on
the spot ("You form order out of chaos."): =allocate_room=, zeroed, named after
its number, description "Empty". Refuses =NO_GOTO= rooms below I10, =LORD_ONLY=
below I9, and =PRIVATE= rooms with 2+ occupants below I6 unless =PLR_TESTER=.
Picks random departure/arrival messages from two 18-entry tables; with
=PLR_STEALTH= set they are shown only to players of equal or higher level.
Ends with =do_look=. Called by =do_rdig= (redit.c:716) to step into the new room.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =room_db=, room flags, =ch->act= (=PLR_STEALTH=, =PLR_TESTER=), =ch->build_lo/hi=
- Writes :: =ch->in_room=; may create a =room_data= (=rp->number/zone/name/description=)
*** Persistence
- No direct save; a room created here lives only in memory until =rsave=.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_URGENT,"Massive error in do_goto...")= if the chosen room vanished.

** purge (=do_purge=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: builder.c:777
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I6 (2006)
:END:
*** Syntax
: purge
: purge <mob | player | object name>
: purge allmob                     (I10+)
: purge allobj                     (I10+)
: purge room <start> [<end>]       (I10+, or internal cmd=-1)
*** Parameters
- (none) :: cleans the current room (requires I5+ even though the command is I6).
- =name= :: =only_argument=; char in room first (=get_char_room_vis=), then object in room.
- =allmob= / =allobj= :: literal, I10+.
- =room start [end]= :: raw vnums (not offset-adjusted); 0 rejected.
*** What it does
No argument: extracts every NPC (=strip_char(v,1)= then =extract_char=) and every
object in the room with a "scrubbing bubbles" act. Named target: NPCs are
stripped and extracted; purging a *player* needs I9 (I10 if they have a live
connection): the victim is stripped, =exit_game(vict,0)= and their socket closed.
=allmob= extracts every mob in the game, =allobj= every object in =object_list=.
=purge room a b= runs =purge_one_room= (builder.c:238) over the vnum range,
which dumps occupants and contents into room 0 (the Void), calls
=completely_cleanout_room= and removes the room from =room_db= (room 0 itself is
never purged). wizard.c:3357 calls it with =cmd=1= as part of a player reset.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =character_list=, =object_list=, =room_db=
- Writes :: removes chars/objects from the world; =room_db= entries deleted for =purge room=
*** Persistence
- No direct save. Purged players have already been saved by =exit_game=. Purged rooms are gone from memory only; a later =rsave= will omit them.

** zone (=do_zone=) -- NOT REGISTERED
:PROPERTIES:

:MIN_LEVEL: n/a
:END:
*** Syntax
: zone <zone#> <reset_time>       (dead)
*** Parameters

Entire body is inside =#if 0=; the function is a no-op. The cmd_info entry
(h/inter.h:200, level I10) is commented out. The old code set
=zone_table[which_zone].lifespan= which no longer exists (zones are now the
=Zone= linked list in zone.c).
*** Data touched

- Writes :: none
*** Persistence
