* alchemy.c — potion brewing

** potion_add (=do_potion_add=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: alchemy.c:42
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:FLAGS: CMDF_NOORDER
:END:
*** Syntax
: potion_add <component> <bottle>
*** Parameters
- =arg1= :: component in inventory (=generic_find FIND_OBJ_INV=); vnums 29001..29014 map to ingredient codes 1..14; vnums 29016..29019 return silently; anything else "not a potion component".
- =arg2= :: bottle in inventory; must be vnum 29000 (=POTION_BOTTLE=).
*** What it does
The bottle's =value[1]= counts ingredients (max 4, refused when already 4) and =value[2]= encodes the recipe as base-16 digits: the code is multiplied by =turn*16= for the 2nd..4th ingredient (so the encoding is =a + b*16 + c*32 + d*48=, not the =16^n= the comment describes; =identify_recipie= uses =16^n=, so only 1- and 2-ingredient recipes decode as intended). The component is removed with =obj_from_char= but never extracted, so it leaks.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->carrying=, =component->vnum=, =bottle->vnum=, =bottle->value[1]=.
- Writes :: =bottle->value[1]++=, =bottle->value[2] += code=, component detached from inventory.
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: none. No direct save; in-memory only, bottle persisted by the next routine player save.

** potion_make (=do_potion_make=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: alchemy.c:163
:MIN_POS: STA (standing)
:MIN_LEVEL: 0 (mortal)
:FLAGS: CMDF_NOORDER
:END:
*** Syntax
: potion_make <bottle>
*** Parameters
- =arg1= :: bottle in inventory (=argument_interpreter=; second word ignored); vnum 29000 with =value[2] != 0=.
*** What it does
=skill_level= is the highest of =SKILL_ALCHEMY_1..4= the player knows; =potion_level = min(4, value[1])=. Success rate starts at 100 and is multiplied by =get_skill(ALCHEMY_n)/100= for each tier up to =potion_level= that the player knows (tier 4 only when =skill_level == 4=); tiers the player lacks impose no penalty. A =number(1,100)= above the rate fails: the bottle is detached (=obj_from_char=, not extracted) and the brewer takes =number(1, 50*level^2)= self-damage typed =SPELL_FIRE_BREATH= (4), =SPELL_POISON= (3), =TRAP_DAM_FIRE= (2) or =TRAP_DAM_ACID= (1). On success the bottle is likewise detached and =get_obj(identify_recipie(value[2]),0)= creates the potion (30-entry recipe table; unknown combinations yield vnum 3974, a poison potion), =setup_obj_ev=, =obj_to_char=.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->carrying=, =bottle->value[1..2]=, skill entries/levels.
- Writes :: bottle detached from inventory (leaked), new potion in =ch->carrying=, =damage()= to self on failure.
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: none. No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_DEBUG,"rate: %d level %d potion %d")= on every attempt.

** Helpers in alchemy.c
- =identify_recipie= (259) :: decode =value[2]= against the recipe table (=list[i][0..3]= ingredient codes as =16^n= digits → potion vnum).

* spell_parser.c — casting

** cast (=do_cast=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: spell_parser.c:797
:MIN_POS: RES (resting)
:MIN_LEVEL: 1
:END:
*** Syntax
: cast '<spell name>' [<target>]
*** Parameters
- =spell name= :: text between single quotes, lowercased; matched with =is_abbrev= against =CLASS(ch).spells= (the caster's class spell list) in list order, first hit wins.
- =target= :: everything after the closing quote (leading spaces skipped); resolved later in =event_spell= by =argument_interpreter= (first word).
- =cmd= :: =CMD_CAST= (58). Branches on =cmd == 129= ("Sing what, praytell?") are dead: no =cmd_info= entry passes 129 and =CMD_SING= is 68.
*** What it does
Pre-checks: =IsHumanoid(ch)=; Druids in =AFF_BEARFORM=/=AFF_CATFORM= cannot cast; mortals need =magic_ok(ch->in_room)=; the argument must start with a quote and contain a closing one. An unknown spell answers with random gibberish. Then =phys_cast(ch, spl, 0, argument, NULL, NULL)=: looks up the =spell_entry= in =classes[ch->job].spells=; without =REMORT_REAGENT_MASTERY= checks =has_components= (missing reagents are compensated by mana); refuses unimplemented spells; stops running; position must reach =spell_info[spl].minimum_position= (spells above fighting cannot be cast while fighting); =sp->min_level= must be met; regrowth/lifebloom require tree/normal form; silence and confusion pulse affects block; =check_spell_skill_cooldown=. Mana: =sp->mana * (5 - min(4, level - min_level))= capped at 500 (negative =mana= means a fixed cost), low spells (=min_level <= 10=) use =max(mana, 100/(2+level-min_level))=, ×(1+difficulty) without components, divided by =REMORT_MANA_EFF=/=REMORT_POWER_EFF= and tree-form bonuses. Mortals below =I1= (non-mobs): =SOURCE_MANA= spells require the caster's alignment to match =GET_TRUE_ALIGN= (unless =REMORT_ZEN_CONJUNCTION=) and =GET_MANA >= mana_needed=; =SOURCE_POWER= spells require =GET_POWER=. Verbal spells (=SPLF_VERBAL=) fail underwater. Casting delay = =WAIT_QTR_SEC + PULSE_VIOLENCE*(beats-1)= minus remort haste (0 for =CLASS_IMMORTAL=). Skill roll: =number(1,101)= must not exceed =get_spell(ch,spl)= (+=power_penalty= for power spells, +10 for =REMORT_REDUCE_WARPS=/=REMORT_FOCUS_CASTING=, capped 99); failure costs half the mana/power and may raise the spell by 1 (=set_spell=) when between =max_at_guild= and =max_learn=. Success: =WAIT_STATE(delay + 2 s)= and =add_event(delay, EVT_SPELL, …, event_spell)=; verbal spells print "muttering", =say_spell= (garbled for other classes), =unsneak=, and aggressive mobs attack casters below =I7= without =PLR_NOHASSLE=; immortals run =event_spell= immediately. =event_spell=: fizzles if the caster changed rooms; =TAR_VIOLENT=/=TAR_FIGHT_VICT= spells are blocked by =check_peaceful=; the target word is resolved by =TAR_CHAR_ROOM= → =get_char_room_vis=, =TAR_CHAR_WORLD= → =get_char_vis=, =TAR_FIGHT_VICT= defaults to =ch->fighting=, =TAR_SELF_ONLY= defaults to self / refuses others, =TAR_SELF_NONO= refuses self, =TAR_OBJ_INV=/=TAR_OBJ_ROOM=/=TAR_OBJ_EQUIP=/=TAR_OBJ_WORLD= object searches, =TAR_IGNORE= always ok; specific "not found" messages otherwise. Violent spells on a victim run the criminal/consent logic (=find_criminal=, =make_criminal=, =HAS_CONSENT=/=CORRUPT=); =SPLF_NOQUEST= and =SPLF_NOARENA= gates; then the mana is recomputed and deducted (=use_components= consumes reagents) and =spell_info[spl].spell_pointer(level, ch, args, SPELL_TYPE_SPELL, victim, obj, level - min_level + 1)= is invoked.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =ch->job=, =classes[].spells=, =spell_info[]=, =GET_POS(ch)=, =ch->fighting=, =GET_MANA=/=GET_POWER=, alignment, remort effects, =ch->spell_casting_haste=, =ch->remort_count=, spell skill values, =SECTOR_TYPE(ch->in_room)=, room people.
- Writes :: =ch->mana= / =ch->power=, =WAIT_STATE=, event queue, spell skill (=set_spell=) on lucky failure, =unsneak= affects, combat started by aggressive mobs, reagent =value[0]=/extraction, whatever the spell function does.
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: none. No direct save; in-memory only, persisted by the next routine player save.
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_URGENT)= when an item spell has no class entry (item path only).

** Helpers in spell_parser.c
- =skip_spaces= (780) :: advance past spaces.
- =say_spell= (619) :: syllable-garbled incantation to non-classmates, plain to classmates/immortals.
- =phys_cast= (888) :: all checks, cost, roll and scheduling; also used for item/scroll casting (=casting_item=) and item sets.
- =event_spell= (1249) :: deferred target resolution, peace/quest/arena gates, mana deduction and spell dispatch.
- =SpellComponent= (19), =is_spell_component= (33), =has_components= (44), =use_components= (58) :: reagent lookup in pouch/held/inventory and consumption.

:PROPERTIES:

*** Syntax

Stores the new string with =strdup= into =ch->prompt= (old one freed). =make_prompt= renders it each turn: =%h=/=%H= hp (with shield in braces), =%z= shield, =%m=/=%M= mana, =%R= rage, =%E= energy, =%c=/=%C= combo points, =%p=/=%P= power, =%v=/=%V= moves, =%o=/=%O= opponent condition/name, =%i= invis/prowl/vanish/sneak/hide letters, =%r= room (immortals), =%I= invis level (immortals), =%l=/=%L= leader hp/status, =%u=/=%U= group status, =%x=/=%X= exp / exp to level (mortals), =%g=/=%G= gold/bank, =%F= immortal flags, =%S= snoop target, =%%= literal, =%*= toggles colouring of the following fields; =\n \r \\ \e= escapes; =<>&= are MXP-escaped when MXP is on.

* Commands whose handler is not named =do_*=

Three =cmd_info[]= entries point straight at library functions rather than a
=do_= wrapper. They are easy to miss when grepping for =do_=.

** delete (=delete_entry=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: db.c:484
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I10 (2010)
:END:
*** Syntax
: delete <player name>
*** Parameters
- =name= :: =one_argument=; the exact character name. Empty → "Who do you want to delete?"
*** What it does
Immortal-only hard delete of a player file. Refused silently for NPCs. Calls
=delete_char(name)=: opens =stash/<c>/<name>= to confirm it exists (and that the
name is at least 2 characters), then runs =/bin/mv <path> PURGED/= via =system()=
and decrements the global =num_players=. Nothing in memory is touched: if the
character is online they keep playing until they quit, at which point
=save_char= recreates the file. Does not remove the name from the account's
character list or from =players.new=.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: player file on disk
- Writes :: =num_players= (global counter)
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: moves =stash/<c>/<name>= into =PURGED/= (shell =mv=, not a rewrite)
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_DELETE, "%s deleted %s")=

** saveworld (=save_world=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: builder.c:530
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I10 (2010)
:END:
*** Syntax
: saveworld
*** Parameters
- none :: arguments ignored
*** What it does
Rewrites the whole text-format world from the in-memory room database. Opens
=WORLD/world= and writes an =#include <area.list>= header, then for each area in
=area_list= calls =save_range(name, offset, next->offset-1)= (the same routine
=tsave= uses) to dump that area's rooms, and appends
=#offset <area>= / =#include <area>= lines to =WORLD/world=. Note the loop
condition =a && a->next= means the *last* area in the list is never saved and
never included; it exists only to bound the previous area's range.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =area_list=, =room_db= (via =save_range=)
- Writes :: none in memory
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: =WORLD/world= (index) plus one file per area via =save_range=
  (see the =tsave= entry under builder.c for the per-area path)
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_URGENT, …)= only if =WORLD/world= cannot be opened

** testsave (=full_save_world=)
:PROPERTIES:
:FILE: world.save.c:102
:MIN_POS: DEA (dead)
:MIN_LEVEL: I10 (2010)
:END:
*** Syntax
: testsave
*** Parameters
- none :: the handler takes no arguments at all (=void full_save_world()=); the interpreter's =ch/arg/cmd= are simply dropped
*** What it does
Forces the binary "WorldSave" snapshot that normally happens incrementally, one
zone per tick. Counts the saveable zones (those with a 2-value =save= range in
their zone file), calls =SaveZone()= that many times so every one is written,
then walks =room_db= with =SaveAutosaveRooms= to write any room flagged
=AUTOSAVE=, and finally rewrites the limited-object count file by iterating
=obj_db= with =SaveLimitCount=. This is the on-disk state that =rload= /
boot-time restore reads back; it is separate from the text world written by
=saveworld=.
*** Data touched
- Reads  :: =zone_list=, =room_db=, =obj_db=
- Writes :: =SaveZone='s static cursor (which zone is "next")
*** Persistence
- Direct saves: =WorldSave/zone.<number>= for every saveable zone (binary,
  id 252 header, then =WriteRoom= per room); autosave rooms via
  =SaveAutosaveRooms=; =WorldSave/Misc/limited.obj= (limited-object counts)
- Log lines: =vlog(LOG_ZONE, "Saving Zone …")= per zone when =DEBUG= is set;
  =LOG_URGENT= if a zone file cannot be opened

* Findings surfaced while documenting

Things noticed in passing that are not documentation of intended behaviour.
All were found by reading the code at =eb40d67=; none has been tested live.
Items marked ✓ were re-checked by a second reader.

** Hidden traps and back doors (highest priority)

These affect ordinary play or bypass the level system.

- ✓ =qui |= and =hi |= (other.c:444, 451) :: any character, immortal included,
  who types the command with an argument beginning =|= has =ch->level= set to 1.
  A typo away from demoting yourself.
- ✓ =deafen |N= (other.c:1978) :: sets =level = 1=, =exp = 0=, and logs
  ="<name> s32"= at =LOG_URGENT=.
- ✓ =quit +N= (other.c:546) :: only for account =Countess= or characters =huttan= /
  =acaldwel=: writes N into the =char_data= struct at word offset 1 via a raw cast.
  On this 64-bit build that is padding after =in_room= (no-op); on 32-bit it would
  have overwritten the =name= pointer.
- ✓ =invisible= (wizard.c:3018) :: the clamp "cannot go invisible above your own
  level" is skipped when the immortal's current hit points are exactly 13.
- =set= (wizard.c) :: granting level I10/I11 is gated on the hard-coded names
  =Countess= / =Alcanzar= / =Nitemare=; anyone else attempting it is wizlocked and
  disconnected.
- =snoop= (wizard.c:1907) :: hard-coded name =Bunta= bypasses the level check.
- =stat= (stat.c:1312) :: hard-coded account =raymond= bypasses the 2001 guard.
- =gain_condition= (limits.c) :: hard-coded skip for account =hutt=, so that
  account never gets hungry or thirsty (affects =eat= / =drink= / =sip= / =taste=).
- =massdie= (other.c) :: I9 prank that sends a fake death and fake account menu to
  every other player. No state change, but worth knowing it exists.

** Permission / rule checks that don't hold

- =claninvite= (clan.c:190) :: officer-rank check prints a refusal but does not
  =return=, so any clan member can invite.
- =clanevict= (clan.c:277) :: equal rank can evict equal rank; target's
  =clan_rank= is never reset; error message names the evictor, not the target.
- =clanjoin= (clan.c:87) :: bad flag prints usage but continues (missing =return=).
- =unlock= (move.c:1196) :: no =EX_CRACKABLE= check, so a crackable door with a
  key vnum ≥ 0 can also be key-unlocked.
- =follow= (move.c:1948) :: =max_followers()= exists but is never consulted; no cap.
- =pounce= (skills1.c:923) :: the skill roll is never randomised (starts at 0,
  only dex-adjusted), so it essentially always succeeds; an I10+ pouncing a
  player is an instant =die()=.
- =roar= (skills1.c:1224) :: debuffs everyone in the room who passes =CORRUPT=
  with no =make_criminal=; group members are not excluded.
- =recite= (other.c) :: precedence in the read-magic exception lets any
  non-barbarian non-caster read identify scrolls.
- =do_who= :: =is_new_player(ch, v->invis_level)= tests the *viewer*, not the
  target, so new players can still see mortal-invisible players.

** Dead code (defined, never reachable)

- ✓ =do_throw= (cmds1.c:1080) :: no =cmd_info[]= entry and no caller, ever
  (checked history). An unfinished copy of =do_hit= (offensive.c): aimed melee hit
  via =ch->aimloc= / =hit()=, minus the legality, crime and shimmer checks. Not an
  item throw and not dice. Safe to delete.
- ✓ =do_appraise= (cmds1.c:1538) :: no =cmd_info[]= entry and no caller.
- ✓ =add_apex_point= (cmds3.c:3876) :: empty stub, no callers.
  =append_apex_point_bonuses= (cmds3.c:3882), =value_stat= (cmds3.c:2046) :: no callers.
- =do_zone= (builder.c:898) :: body is =#if 0= and its =cmd_info= line is commented out.
- =do_ruby_eval= (ruby_cmds.c) :: entry is inside =#ifndef NO_RUBY= and the file is
  not in the Linux makefile; inert on this build.
- =do_polymorph= (wizard.c:798) :: empty, unregistered stub.
- =christmas= and =invasion= (wizard.c) :: registered, but loop bodies do nothing.
- =do_log_locker= (lockers.c:300) :: registered, empty stub. The locker =logStr=
  built elsewhere is never written anywhere.
- =repair= / =clean= (wizard.c) =cmd == -1= self-branches, =do_commune= =cmd == -1=
  broadcast path, =do_cast= =cmd == 129= "sing" branches :: no callers.
- =do_move= post-move "INTREPID" auto-attack (move.c) :: requires =IS_AGGR(ch)= AND
  =!IS_NPC(ch)=, which can never both hold.
- =do_fish= =AFF_FISH= "stop fishing" branch :: nothing ever sets =AFF_FISH=.
- =in_wrong_world= (wizard.c helper) :: every path returns 0 after the "-perci"
  edits; a no-op still called from interpreter.c, spells2.c and =transfer=.
- =NewFailSkill= (skills1.c:1888) :: unused.
- =dumpmode= (wizard.c) :: registered =CMDF_DISABLED=; off until =enable dumpmode=.

** Probable bugs

*** Communication and info (cmds1–3)
- ✓ =quote= (cmds2.c:680, 685) :: "You have no %s." passes the NULL =obj_data*=
  instead of the name string (the same message at 573/583 is correct).
- =tell= :: =is_granted(ch, -cmd)= receives a positive =CMD_TELL= number, so the
  =PLR_NOTELL= escape can never match (interpreter.c:286-298).
- =apexlist= :: Dexterity line multiplies =apex_points.hitpoints= (copy/paste).
- =kills= :: in leaderboard rooms the "Your ranking is:" line goes to a string
  block that is never paged.
- =hitall= :: =skill <= 0= branch prints an error but does not =return=; moves are
  still consumed.
- =berserk= :: on cooldown =berserk_setBerserk= applies nothing, but the command
  still prints success and applies the recovery penalty.
- =shout= :: immortal non-local shouts skip =propagate_message=, so the world-wide
  loop filters on stale =rp->visited= flags.

*** Combat (offensive.c)
- ✓ =berzerk= (offensive.c, =do_berzerk=) :: declared =(char_data *ch, char_data *v)=
  but dispatched as =(ch, argument, num)=, so =v= is really the argument string
  pointer, and it is handed to =act(..., v, TO_VICT)= as a character. Memory
  corruption / crash risk whenever the command is typed with an argument.
- ✓ =breath= (offensive.c:3350) :: =bweapons[]= starts with =cast_geyser= but is
  indexed from =FIRST_BREATH_WEAPON= (177 = =SPELL_FIRE_BREATH=; geyser is 176).
  So fire breath casts geyser, gas casts fire, frost casts gas, and so on: every
  breath weapon is off by one element. The =GET_MANA <= -3*manacost= test also
  runs before =manacost= is assigned.
- =kick= :: a NINJA satisfies both the ninja branch and the =else= of the WARRIOR
  test, so damage is applied twice.
- =rescue= :: after the skill check =tmp_ch= is reassigned to =victim->fighting=,
  and =stop_fighting(victim->fighting)= detaches everyone fighting that mob.
- =assist= :: =WAIT_STATE= is applied to the assisted character, not the assister.
- =flee= :: =AddHated= / =SetHunting= fire only when the opponent is a PC, where
  =SetHunting= is a no-op — condition looks inverted; mobs never hunt fleers.
- =backstab= :: =find_criminal= / =make_criminal= run before the weapon-type and
  "too busy" refusals, so a refused attempt can still flag the attacker.
- =order= :: the "followers" path skips the =CMDF_NOORDER= check the single-target
  path enforces.
- =sunder= :: "You can't sunder the dead!" is missing a =return=.
- =garrote= :: the miss branch calls =damage(..., SKILL_CRIPPLING_STRIKE)=.
- =daze= :: cooldown is =… + DEX/6=, so higher DEX lengthens it (probably meant minus).
- =preparation= :: =remortBonus= overwritten with the vanish bonus (copy/paste);
  resets cooldowns by calling the wizard command =do_resetcds= on itself.
- =mute_strike= :: per-level damage caps are immediately overridden by a flat 750;
  wait is applied to immortals too.
- =crippling_strike= :: ADVANCED branches use the IMPROVE bonus; no wait state if
  the blow kills.
- =shadowform= :: ADVANCED branch *divides* power by the IMPROVE multiplier.
- =whirlwind= :: first swing's resistance adjustment is computed against the loop
  enemy before retargeting.
- =cheapshot= / =crippling_strike= / =strike= / =holy_strike= :: call
  =check_spell_skill_cooldown= but no cooldown is ever added (dead check).
- =assassinate= :: criminal-mode string is "backstab"; no =attackers >= 3= limit.
- =vital_stab= :: victim knock-out wait is commented out; only NPC victims sleep.
- =do_batter= (offensive.c:660) :: has the =do_= signature but no =cmd_info= entry;
  reached only through the skill table (fight.c:3205).

*** Items and world (obj1/obj2/look/lockers/alchemy)
- =get all= (obj1.c:492) :: =found= is never set, so it reports "You see nothing
  here." even after picking things up.
- =put= (obj1.c:898) :: =generic_find= always returns =FIND_OBJ_INV=, so the
  room-container branch is dead.
- =pay= (obj1.c:1032) :: registered at I9 (an immortal gold grant, not a mortal
  command); I5+ payers are not debited.
- =fill= (obj2.c:43) :: missing =return= after the syntax message.
- =remove N= (obj2.c:1327) :: numbers occupied slots only, while =equipment= numbers
  all 22 slots; they disagree whenever a slot is empty.
- =read= (look.c:1038) :: =generic_find= result is discarded; it is just =look at=.
- =get= from locker (lockers.c:71) :: empty-arg message says "Put what in the
  locker?"; =all.= / =N*= prefixes are stripped but only one item is taken.
- =put= in locker (lockers.c:140) :: =str_cmp= on a possibly-NULL =owner=; capacity
  test compares container weight to =container->cost=.
- =potion_add= (alchemy.c:42) :: encodes with =code*turn*16= but
  =identify_recipie= decodes =16^n=, so 3-ingredient recipes never match; removed
  component is never extracted (leak).
- =potion_make= (alchemy.c:163) :: bottle detached but never extracted (leak).

*** Movement and skills (move.c / skills1.c)
- =stand= while RUNNING (move.c:1738) :: prints "stop floating around" but never
  changes position.
- =rest= default branch (move.c:1814) :: sets SITTING instead of RESTING.
- =go= → =event_run= (move.c:1606) :: tests =IS_AFFECTED(ch, SPELL_FLY)= — a spell
  number used as an affect bit; almost certainly meant =AFF_FLYING=.
- =flurry= (skills1.c:733) :: debuffs applied before the second roll, so a failed
  second roll leaves only the debuffs.
- =track= (skills1.c:1596) :: =real_mobp(...)->mob= dereferenced without a NULL check.
- =fish= (skills1.c:1936) :: deducts =ch->move -= 10= directly instead of via
  =consume_moves=.
- =maul= (skills1.c:1340) :: on success re-rolls =dice(N, dmg)= with base damage as
  die size, unlike =rake=; possibly intentional.

*** Immortal and admin (wizard.c / builder.c / redit.c / other.c)
- =norent= remove :: off-by-one in the compaction loop drops the last vnum and
  duplicates the second-to-last.
- =iremort= (do_remort) :: =isdigit= check inverted; =DeleteHatreds= / =DeleteFears=
  and =ch->mana = GET_MAX_MANA(victim)= act on the immortal, not the victim;
  =raw_kill_no_move= leaves a corpse of the victim's gear.
- =restore= :: hunger/thirst decision tests the *caller's* =CONFIG_HUNGER=.
- =resetallplayers= :: =affect_remove= on the invoker instead of =temp_player=; no
  guard against link-loading someone already online (duplicate =char_data=).
- =passwd= :: silently frees the character's =account_name= before saving.
- =clean= :: =FREE(v->kills)= leaves a dangling pointer; equipment affects are not
  re-applied.
- =atell= :: both messages use =vict->name= where the sender's name is intended.
- =rchar= :: prints "Done." twice. =comment= :: clear-level refusal leaks an
  =acct= reference. =lockout= :: missing reason prints usage but proceeds.
- =newbieset= :: declared with two parameters, called with three; a NULL from
  =do_load_object= would be dereferenced.
- =goodies= :: the bag has no prototype vnum, so it will not survive rent/save.
- =echo= :: =%d= given a =char*=. =reboot= :: =%.0f= given a =long=; writes
  =BOOT_CLEAN= but messages say =CLEAN_BOOT=.
- =wizlock= / =nonew= :: whitespace-only input adds an empty string to the list.
- =crash= :: =arg++= skips the first character unconditionally.
- =redit dtime= / =odtime= (redit.c:870) :: assigns =b= but tests =!d= (always NULL),
  so it always says "Create the exit first."
- =redit proto= (redit.c:754) :: NULL check is on =rp= instead of =protoRP=.
- =room_copy= :: overwrites =ex_description= without freeing the old list.
- =rsave= :: filename containing =.= leads to =unlink()= on an uninitialised buffer.
- =foreach= :: user text used directly as a =printf= format string.
- =form= :: range validation nesting means only ">200 rooms below I10" is rejected.
- =rdig= :: a failed later check leaves the newly allocated empty room in =room_db=.
- =saveworld= (builder.c:530) :: loop condition =a && a->next= means the last area in
  =area_list= is never saved or included.
- =sacrifice= (other.c) :: remort-token path echoes the raw die roll to the player.
- =idea= (other.c) :: appends to the =ideas= file without a trailing newline.
- =save= (other.c) :: always records load room 3000, so a crash after =save= puts the
  player at 3000 rather than where they were.
- =makeleader= :: =count_pc_followers= tests =IS_NPC(ch)= not =IS_NPC(k->follower)=,
  so pets count against the follower limit.
- =do_resetplayer= / =do_get_world_stats= / =listspells= / =listskills= :: prototype
  parameter mismatch with the =cmd_info= pointer (harmless in practice).

** Behaviour worth a second look (may be intended)

- =save_char= does not write =ch->away=, =ch->consent=, =ch->aggressive= or
  =ch->aimloc=; those settings are session-only.
- =terminal= :: sets only =desc->term=; not saved from the command (the login menu
  writes =acct->term_type_id=).
- =kill_table= (=kills=) is in-memory only, rebuilt at each login from nanny.c.
- =practice <arg>= always says "no guildmaster here"; learning happens in the
  guildmaster spec-proc before dispatch, so only the no-arg listing works here.
- =do_not_here= backs 22 aliases (=buy=, =sell=, =deposit=, =bet=, …); all real
  behaviour lives in room/mob spec-procs and this is only the fallback message.
- =delete= (db.c:484) :: moves the file to =PURGED/= but leaves the account's
  character list and =players.new= untouched, and an online victim keeps playing
  until their next save recreates the file.
- =zfile= writes nothing; zone definitions are only ever read from =zone.out=.
- Lockers are per room and shared by everyone in the room
  (=LockerSave/locker.<vnum>.room=).
- Clans are read-only from =clans.out=; nothing in the game writes it. Membership
  persists only through =save_char= fields 66 and 120.
